We collect raw questions from students in an AI course, clean and annotate them with structured reference solutions, and propose a task taxonomy tailored to the resulting dataset of 235 samples. We further develop an automated grading pipeline to evaluate a wide range of models, including open-weight and closed-source language, vision-language, and image-generation models. Our analysis on $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ reveals that closed-source frontier models can substantially outperform open-weight models with even $\approx10\%$ gap, even when they attain comparable performance on existing benchmarks. A more fine-grained analysis shows that no single model dominates across all task types, and that some tasks remain challenging for all evaluated models. These results highlight the value of $\texttt{blind-spots-bench}$ as a diagnostic stress test for identifying concrete weaknesses in current modern models.

</details>


### [37] [MobiDiff: Semantic-Aware Multi-Channel Discrete Diffusion for Human Mobility Data Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08357)
*Rongchao Xu,Lin Jiang,Dahai Yu,Ximiao Li,Taichi Liu,Desheng Zhang,Yuan Tian,Guang Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MobiDiff是一个端到端离散扩散框架，通过多通道语义骨架去噪高效生成人类移动数据，在亚特兰大、波士顿、西雅图三个真实数据集上表现出色，推理速度比现有方法快5.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的移动数据生成方法依赖连续或潜在时空轨迹，无法原生建模离散语义事件（如区域、活动、时间、间隔结构），且需要昂贵的插值和粗到细的实现流程。

Method: MobiDiff将签到事件分解为空间、活动、时间三个通道，采用结构化的事件级、组级和通道级掩码策略，在多通道语义骨架上直接去噪，无需插值和潜在轨迹构建。

Result: 在三个大规模真实数据集上，MobiDiff有效保持了轨迹长度和时间间隔分布，在更广泛的移动统计指标上具有竞争力；推理速度平均比GeoGen快5.3倍。

Conclusion: 离散扩散为合成移动数据生成提供了一种可解释且高效的框架。

Abstract: Human mobility data are essential for transportation optimization, urban planning, and resource allocation, yet real-world mobility data are costly to collect and difficult to share due to privacy concerns. Recent diffusion-based methods have shown promise in synthesizing realistic mobility patterns, but they typically rely on continuous or latent spatio-temporal traces, limiting their ability to natively model discrete semantic events with explicit region, activity, time, and interval structures. To address this issue, we introduce MobiDiff, an end-to-end discrete diffusion framework that efficiently generates mobility data by directly denoising multi-channel semantic skeletons, avoiding the costly interpolation, latent trace construction, and coarse-to-fine realization pipelines widely used in existing diffusion-based methods. Specifically, MobiDiff decomposes each human check-in event into spatial, activity, and temporal channels, and employs structured event-, group-, and channel-level masking to jointly capture trajectory-level mobility patterns and within-event dependencies. We evaluate generation fidelity, privacy-preserving, and efficiency on three large-scale real-world datasets from Atlanta, Boston, and Seattle. Results show that MobiDiff effectively preserves trajectory length and temporal interval distributions while remaining competitive across broader mobility statistics; it is also much faster than state-of-the-art methods, e.g., 5.3$\times$ faster than GeoGen on average during inference. These findings suggest that discrete diffusion offers an interpretable and efficient framework for synthetic mobility data generation.

</details>


### [38] [FedOPAL: One-Shot Federated Learning via Analytic Visual Prompt Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08368)
*Lingyu Qiu,Daniela Annunziata,Stefano Izzo,Fabio Giampaolo,Francesco Piccialli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FedOPAL框架通过视觉提示作为特征整流器，结合局部近端约束修正异构数据分布，在保持零服务器训练成本的同时，使解析联邦学习在非独立同分布场景下达到与迭代方法相当的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有解析联邦学习在非独立同分布数据环境下静态特征假设失效，导致特征流形错配，严重损害模型性能；而迭代方法存在高计算成本和超参数敏感性问题。

Method: 提出FedOPAL框架，将视觉提示作为特征整流器，通过局部近端约束主动修正异构数据的特征分布至线性可分离空间，以满足解析联邦学习的理论假设。

Result: FedOPAL在多个基准上显著优于原始解析方法，精度与最先进的迭代方法相当，同时保持零服务器端训练成本。

Conclusion: FedOPAL为边缘大模型的高效协作提供了新的工程范式，在通信高效性的前提下实现了高性能。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of basic models in edge intelligence, communication bandwidth has become a core bottleneck restricting the scalability of federated learning. Although one-shot federated learning alleviates this problem by minimizing communication rounds, existing iterative fine-tuning or knowledge distillation methods still face challenges such as high server-side computational costs and hyperparameter sensitivity. Analytical federated learning achieves efficient gradientfree aggregation using least-squares closed-form solutions, but in environments with non-independent and identically distributed data, its static feature assumptions fail, leading to feature manifold misalignment and severely impairing model performance. To address this contradiction, this paper proposes the FedOPAL framework. This framework adapts the visual prompts as feature rectifiers, actively correcting the feature distribution of heterogeneous data to a linearly separable space by applying local proximal constraints, thereby satisfying the theoretical assumptions of analytical federated learning. Experimental results show that FedOPAL not only significantly outperforms the original analytical methods on several benchmarks, but also achieves accuracy comparable to state-of-the-art iterative methods while maintaining zero server-side training costs, providing a new engineering paradigm for efficient collaboration of large models on the edge.

</details>


### [39] [Towards Mechanistically Understanding Why Memorized Knowledge Fails to Generalize in Large Language Model Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08393)
*Lu Dai,Ziyang Rao,Yili Wang,Hanqing Wang,Hao Liu,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 微调LLM注入新知识时，模型能快速记忆但无法用于推理任务，这种现象被称为"知道-使用差距"。作者通过自修补技术发现，记忆的表征存在于模型内部但未被路由到有效计算层，并设计启发式策略恢复了58-75%的泛化失败性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化并理解微调LLM时出现的"知道-使用差距"现象——即模型能快速记忆新知识却无法在下游推理任务中应用这一矛盾。

Method: 提出自修补(self-patching)干预技术，通过定位激活位置并重新定位表征来改善泛化失败案例，从而揭示知识的内部空间渗透动力学。

Result: 识别出知识电路错配假说：记忆的表征存在于内部但未被路由到计算有效层；设计的启发式策略可恢复58-75%的oracle上限性能。

Conclusion: 微调LLM新知识时，记忆与泛化之间的差距源于内部表征的路由错配，而非知识缺失；通过干预路由机制可大幅缓解该问题，且该发现跨领域具有鲁棒性。

Abstract: Fine-tuning LLMs to inject new knowledge faces a critical challenge: LLMs can quickly memorize new facts, yet fail to use them for downstream reasoning tasks. We formalize this failure as the \textit{\textbf{Knowing--Using Gap}}, characterized by an accuracy gap and a temporal lag between memorization and generalization. To understand this phenomenon, we fine-tune LLMs with unseen knowledge and monitor the spatial permeation dynamics of the knowledge internally using a novel intervention technique called self-patching. Self-patching identifies activation locations where relocating representations substantially improves failed generalization cases. These results are consistent with a knowledge-circuit misalignment hypothesis: memorized representations can exist internally but may not be routed to computation-effective layers. To demonstrate the practicality of this diagnostic finding, we design a simple heuristic strategy which recovers 58--75\% of the oracle headroom in generalization failure. Experiments are done cross-domain for the robustness of this finding.

</details>


### [40] [Game Theory Driven Multi-Agent Framework Mitigates Language Model Hallucination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08403)
*Runzhe Liu,Biquan Bie,Zihao Wang,Yuchao Ma,Yexin Liu,Xinghai Li,Harry Yang,Wenbo Yang,Jinzhe Cao,Shengyang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出G-Frame自适应多智能体框架，通过贝叶斯和团队博弈原理生成结构化推理数据，训练出7B模型OmniChem，在化学领域达到GPT-4o mini水平，幻觉率降低79.46%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轻量级大语言模型在基于规则的科-学领域中严重受限，因为它们倾向于模仿语言模式而非复现公理推理，导致频繁的幻觉现象。

Method: 提出G-Frame自适应多智能体框架，融合贝叶斯和团队博弈原理，构建自动化闭环实现高质量数据合成与模型训练。通过强制领域约束的结构化推理，合成了363,045条思维链和199,589个问答对，训练出7B参数模型OmniChem。

Result: OmniChem在自定义基准和ChemBench上达到GPT-4o mini的性能水平，相比基础架构幻觉率降低79.46%，并在分子设计与合成规划中展现出先进能力。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了利用自适应多智能体克服固有推理缺陷的可扩展范式，为加速科学发现提供了可行路径。

Abstract: The application of lightweight Large Language Models in rule-based scientific domains remains severely limited by their tendency to mimic linguistic patterns rather than reproduce axiomatic reasoning, causing frequent hallucinations. Here, we show that G-Frame, an adaptive multi-agent framework integrating Bayesian and team game principles, establishes an automated closed-loop for high-quality data synthesis and model training. By forcing the internalization of domain constraints through structured reasoning, we synthesized a specialized corpus of 363,045 chains-of-thought and 199,589 question-answer pairs. The resulting 7B model OmniChem achieves performance parity with GPT 4o mini on custom benchmarks and ChemBench while exhibiting a 79.46% reduction in hallucinations relative to its base architecture. We further demonstrate the advanced capabilities of OmniChem in molecular design and synthesis planning. This work establishes a scalable paradigm utilizing adaptive multi-agents to overcome inherent reasoning deficiencies, offering a feasible pathway for accelerating knowledge discovery in specialized scientific fields.

</details>


### [41] [OmniFood-Bench: Evaluating VLMs for Nutrient Reasoning and Personalized Health Advice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08423)
*Qian Jiang,Zhecheng Shi,Jingpu Yang,Zirui Song,Miao Fang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniFood-Bench基准，用于评估大视觉语言模型在食品领域的"系统性信息不对称"挑战。测试发现模型在名称识别上接近人类，但在质量估计和医疗建议方面存在严重缺陷，揭示了"语义-物理鸿沟"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有食品基准仅关注粗粒度分类（如菜品识别），无法评估模型从识别隐藏成分、估计物理质量到合成安全医疗建议的完整推理链。真实世界饮食管理需要应对"视觉外观与内在营养组成之间的系统性信息不对称"。

Method: 基于MM-Food-100K数据集构建OmniFood-Bench，设计三个渐进式能力评估层级：基础感知（成分与烹饪方法）、定量推理（份量大小与营养分析）、安全关键建议（疾病特异性推荐）。评估了包括gpt-5.1、gemini-3-flash、qwen3-vl-8B在内的六种先进VLM。

Result: 模型在菜品命名上接近人类准确率，但在质量估计方面出现灾难性失败，并频繁对高风险糖尿病案例产生良性误导建议。揭示了"语义-物理鸿沟"（Semantic-Physical Gap）现象。

Conclusion: 这项工作为部署于公共卫生领域的自主智能体建立了可信赖性的严格评估标准。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Vision-Language Models (VLMs) into critical infrastructure promises to revolutionize
  personalized healthcare and dietary management. However, in the domain of food systems, autonomous agents face a
  unique and persistent challenge: the "Systemic Information Asymmetry" between visual appearance and intrinsic
  nutritional composition. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on coarse-grained classification tasks, such as food
  category recognition, which fail to evaluate the intricate reasoning chain required for real-world dietary management
  -- specifically, the ability to traverse from identifying hidden ingredients to estimating physical mass, and finally
  synthesizing safety-critical medical advice. In this paper, we introduce OmniFood-Bench, a comprehensive benchmark
  constructed from the MM-Food-100K dataset. Unlike previous works, OmniFood-Bench evaluates VLMs across three
  progressive capabilities: Basic Perception (Ingredients & Cooking Methods), Quantitative Reasoning (Portion Size &
  Nutritional Profiling), and Safety-Critical Advisory (Disease-Specific Recommendations). We evaluate six
  state-of-the-art VLMs, including gpt-5.1, gemini-3-flash, and qwen3-vl-8B. Our extensive experiments reveal a
  startling "Semantic-Physical Gap": while models achieve near-human accuracy in naming dishes, they exhibit
  catastrophic failure in mass estimation and frequently hallucinate benign advice for high-risk diabetic profiles. This
  work establishes a rigorous standard for trustworthiness in autonomous agents deployed for public health. The code
  and datasets are available in: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/OmniFood-Bench-7D0B

</details>

Abstract: I-JEPA and V-JEPA learn by matching latent predictions to target encoder outputs rather than regenerating the original input, and this has worked well for images and video. We explore whether the same objective works for compact network fingerprints. We built JA4-JEPA, a Transformer-based model trained on JA4, JA4H, JA4S, and JA4X subfields drawn from JA4DB and CIC-IDS- 2017. The training data combines roughly 397K samples from both sources, though no single sample contains all four view families. We evaluated the learned representations with a frozen kNN probe on protocol-family classification across TLS, DNS, and SSH. On 39,416 heldout samples the model achieved a cosine similarity of 0.9899 and a kNN accuracy of 0.9220. These results indicate that JEPA-style predictive learning can produce useful embeddings from JA4-derived fingerprints, even with incomplete view overlap across sources.

### [43] [Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) for Adaptive Semantic Modeling in Biomedical Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08490)
*Bharathwaj Vijayakumar,Sahana K. Varadaraju*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DATGR框架，通过动态重布线共现边来建模生物医学概念演化，在BIOMRC数据集上AUROC提升约0.066，AUPRC保持可比，实现了轻量、可解释的语义漂移适应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生物医学语言随新发现快速演化，静态嵌入和共现图无法捕捉这种语义漂移，导致检索和知识发现任务性能下降。

Method: 提出漂移感知时间图重布线框架，通过逻辑斯谛更新规则对边权重进行轻量级、反馈驱动的动态更新，无需为每个时间片重新训练嵌入。

Result: 在Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus上，AUROC相较静态基线提升约0.066（0.699 vs. 0.633），AUPRC保持可比（0.738 vs. 0.744）。

Conclusion: 边级适应能有效捕捉演化文本中的时间语义变化，同时保持计算效率和可解释性。

Abstract: Biomedical language evolves rapidly as new discoveries emerge, causing traditional text models to lose semantic fidelity over time. Static embeddings and co-occurrence graphs cannot capture such evolution, leading to performance degradation in retrieval and knowledge discovery tasks. This paper introduces a Drift-Aware Temporal Graph Rewiring (DATGR) framework that models concept evolution by dynamically updating co-occurrence edges based on estimated semantic drift. Instead of retraining embeddings for each time slice, DATGR performs lightweight, feedback-driven rewiring using a logistic update rule applied to edge weights. Evaluated on the Biomedical Multi-Relation Corpus (BIOMRC), the method achieved a mean Area Under the Receiver Operating Characteristic (AUROC) improvement of approximately 0.066 absolute difference (0.699 vs. 0.633) over a static baseline. Area Under the Precision-Recall Curve (AUPRC) remained comparable (0.738 vs. 0.744), showing that drift-aware adaptation enhances link-prediction recall without a loss in precision. These results demonstrate that edge-level adaptation effectively captures temporal semantic change in evolving biomedical text while remaining computationally efficient and interpretable.

</details>


### [44] [AI-guided stimuli discovery and generation to optimize facial emotion perception studies in autism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08533)
*Kushin Mukherjee,Na Yeon Kim,Maren Wehrheim,Ralph Adolphs,Kohitij Kar*

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 训练群体特异性人工神经网络模型预测图像层面的情绪判断；用模型选择能最大化群体分离的新面孔；在独立队列中验证模型选择图像与随机匹配图像的效果；使用生成对抗网络将诊断性图像转换为趋于群体一致性；在表型匹配验证中测试合成图像的效果。

Result: 模型选择的图像比随机匹配图像产生了更大的行为差异；合成的图像相比原始图像减少了行为分离。

Abstract: Understanding perceptual differences between autistic and neurotypical adults requires behavioral assays that are sensitive, reliable, and mechanistically informative. Facial emotion perception is a useful test case because group differences have been reported, but findings vary across studies. Here we show that this variability may reflect image-level sparsity: autistic-neurotypical differences in emotion judgments were concentrated in a small subset of diagnostic facial expressions rather than spread uniformly across stimuli. We trained population-specific artificial neural network models to predict image-level judgments for autistic and neurotypical participants, then used these models to select novel faces predicted to maximize group separation. In an independent cohort, model-selected images produced larger behavioral differences than matched random images. We then used the same models with a generative adversarial network to transform diagnostic images toward greater predicted group agreement. In phenotype-matched validation, synthesized images reduced behavioral separation relative to their matched originals. These results establish a model-guided framework for discovering and transforming stimuli that reveal population-specific perceptual differences. More broadly, they show how behavioral phenotyping can move beyond averaging across fixed stimulus sets toward optimized assays that identify the conditions under which neurodivergent perception diverges or converges.

</details>

*Hongye Yang,Shien Liu,Zhihao Xie*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CommuniWave机器学习模型，通过整合BCN、YLX和BEM三个模块，从街景视频中检测和量化城市社区中的非正式行为程度（DIB），生成波动图表以支持城市管理者进行精细化决策，从而提升社区韧性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前社区规划常采用自上而下的方式，缺乏量化居民非正式行为的有效指标，导致规划与实际冲突频繁。城市需要提升社区功能属性以增强应对复杂性和不确定性的韧性。

Result: 模型能够从街景视频中生成DIB波动图表，实现动态监测，支持精细化决策。

Conclusion: CommuniWave可有效量化和监测社区非正式行为，为城市管理者提供数据支持，有助于提升社区整体韧性。

Abstract: For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.

</details>


### [46] [SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08573)
*Adis Alihodzic,Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF）方法在多模态情感识别中的应用，重点分析了不同SHAP归约策略（均值-绝对值、中位数-绝对值、总和-绝对值）对专家权重的影响，发现总和-绝对值策略能有效保留高维跨模态专家的贡献，使XGAF性能接近甚至略优于早期融合，同时保持模块化架构的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 早期融合精度高但模型整体化，晚期融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互信息。现有方法难以在保持模块化的同时有效捕捉跨模态交互，且缺乏对SHAP归约方式如何影响不同维度专家权重的深入理解。

Method: 提出XAI引导的自适应融合（XGAF），采用基于TreeSHAP属性幅值的树状混合模型，将单模态专家与跨模态专家进行样本级加权融合。重点研究了均值-绝对值、中位数-绝对值、总和-绝对值三种SHAP归约策略对专家权重的影响，并在MELD和CMU-MOSEI数据集上使用三种人脸序列聚合器（含Transformer变体）进行评估。

Result: 在MELD 7类情感识别任务中，总和-绝对值XGAF（Transformer变体）达到0.5983 WF，接近早期融合的0.6018，显著优于概率平均晚期融合的0.4598；McNemar检验显示与早期融合无显著差异（p=1.000），与晚期融合有显著差异（p<0.0001）。在CMU-MOSEI 3类情感识别中，总和-绝对值XGAF达到0.6519 WF，略超早期融合（0.6485）和晚期融合（0.5696）。消融实验表明主要增益来自跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂的样本级路由机制。诊断分析显示均值-绝对值和单位数-绝对值权重近乎均匀分布，而总和-绝对值权重集中于三模态专家。

Conclusion: 本文的主要贡献在于对SHAP归约策略、专家维度与跨模态专家设计如何影响模块化多模态融合进行了透明化的实证分析，证明了总和-绝对值归约策略能够有效平衡高维跨模态专家的贡献，使XGAF在保持模块化架构的同时达到与早期融合相当甚至略优的性能。

Abstract: Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.

</details>


### [47] [Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08602)
*Peng Cui,Jitao Wang,Siyan Xue,Yao Huang,Haoming Xia,Dong Li,Dengxiang Liu,Weilin Wang,Liping Liu,Leida Zhang,Yunfu Cui,Tao Peng,Daolin Ji,Haitao Zhao,Wei Zhang,Xiaojuan Wang,Weijie Ma,Zongren Ding,Jinlong Li,Yuan Ding,Jiajing Zhao,Zhiyu Chen,Chengkun Yang,Ziyue Huang,Jiaqi Liu,Fusheng Liu,Yang Zhou,Xiaojuan Wang,Zhongquan Sun,Shiyun Bao,Xiaojun Wang,Ming Yang,Guangxin Li,Bin Shu,Yong Liao,Hongxuan Li,Yao Tang,Shizhong Yang,Yongyi Zeng,Yufeng Yuan,Yinpeng Dong,Jihui Hao,Jun Zhu,Jiahong Dong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: HCC-STAR是一个基于大语言模型的临床决策支持系统，通过读取电子病历叙述，实现肝癌的风险分层、治疗推荐和生存预测，在多中心评估中优于临床指南和GPT-5等模型，并能在辅助场景下提升医生的决策准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有肝癌指南和分期系统（如BCLC）虽然提供了粗略的分类，但无法捕捉同一分期内部的异质性，也未能充分利用电子病历中丰富的临床上下文信息，导致个体化诊疗决策存在不足。

Method: 基于约30,000例SEER数据，通过临床验证的提示增强工作流将其扩展为电子病历风格的叙述训练数据；构建了知识对齐的推理框架，并使用步骤可验证的复合奖励进行优化，超越了简单的文本记忆式学习。

Result: 在来自中国12家医院、6,668名患者的多中心队列中，HCC-STAR在治疗推荐和风险分层方面优于临床指南以及GPT-5、Gemini-2.5 Pro等模型。依从HCC-STAR推荐的中位总生存期为51个月，而BCLC和CNLC分别为29和32个月。盲法评估中，肝胆专科医生认为其推理和循证依据值得信赖；模型在治疗准确性上超过住院和主治医师，作为辅助工具时帮助医生更快地做出更准确的决策。

Conclusion: HCC-STAR是一种可靠且可验证的决策支持系统，能够有效支持肝癌的风险分层和精准治疗。

Abstract: Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.

</details>


### [48] [Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08716)
*Yifan Wu,Lizhu Zhang,Yuhang Zhou,Mingyi Wang,Bo Peng,Serena Li,Xiangjun Fan,Zhuokai Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“行为状态衰减”问题——长程任务中决策相关信息随轨迹增长而被埋没，并设计了一个独立运行的记忆代理模块，通过结构化的记忆库和选择性干预注入来辅助未经修改的动作代理。实验表明该方法在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上分别提升+8.3和+6.8个百分点，且选择性干预优于被动暴露、始终注入等基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在长程任务中，决策相关信息分散在不断扩展的轨迹中，随着轨迹增长，任务需求、环境事实、先前尝试、诊断结果和未完成子目标等信息会被掩盖在上下文窗口中或超出窗口限制，导致无法在需要时影响决策。作者将这种失败模式定义为“行为状态衰减”，并针对性地提出主动记忆干预机制。

Method: 设计一个独立的记忆代理模块，与未经修改的动作代理并行运行。该模块从最近的轨迹中更新结构化的记忆库，并决定是否注入基于记忆的提醒或保持沉默。该模块可与前沿动作代理及现有代理框架即插即用。此外，在SETA数据集上使用SFT和GRPO训练Qwen3.5-27B，作为开放权重记忆策略的初步探索。

Result: 在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上，该方法对较弱和较强的动作代理均提升了pass@1指标，分别提升+8.3个百分点和+6.8个百分点。消融实验表明，选择性干预优于被动库暴露、始终注入、仅顾问指导和通用检索等基线方案。

Conclusion: 主动记忆干预是解决长程任务中行为状态衰减的有效方法，选择性干预策略显著优于其他被动或始终注入的记忆机制。训练的记忆策略在验证奖励上有所提升，并展示出向Terminal-Bench的部分迁移能力，为开放权重记忆策略的研究迈出了早期一步。

Abstract: In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.

</details>


### [49] [Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08740)
*Emanuele Quinto,Carlo Andrea Rozzi,Francesco Zanitti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受Lisp启发、语言无关的概念模型，将工作流定义、实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象，核心语义区分在于确定性计算（derive）与LLM中介推理（infer），从而让工作流本身成为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM应用虽已使用显式工作流处理工具调用、检索、分支、检查点等人机协作问题，但缺乏一个统一的概念模型来将工作流本身视为持久的、可检查的知识对象，而非仅仅是执行痕迹。

Method: 采用Lisp启发的概念建模方法，引入符号形式、对象标识和 live-image 思维作为解释性透镜，提出 derive（确定性计算）与 infer（LLM中介推理）的语义区分，并将工作流构件表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。

Result: 形成了关于语义持久性的初步概念性说明：工作流不仅能产生知识和留下痕迹，其本身也可被表示为可检查、可恢复、可审查的知识对象。

Conclusion: 该概念模型为LLM工作流的持久化语义提供了理论基础，但形式化的迁移语义仍是未来工作。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy.
  The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.

</details>


### [50] [AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08745)
*Siddharth Damodharan,Radhika Gupta,Ali Alshami,Ryan Rabinowitz,Jugal Kalita*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AUTOPILOT-VQA，一个面向行车记录仪视频的以事故为中心的视觉问答基准，用于评估自动驾驶系统在安全关键场景中的推理能力，涵盖天气、交通环境、道路布局、事故可避免性等多类别安全相关任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语言模型在自动驾驶场景理解、决策等任务上取得了进展，但缺乏对模型在安全关键事故中推理能力的系统性评估，因此需要构建一个以事故为中心的标准化基准来弥补这一空白。

Method: 构建了基于真实驾驶事故及近事故场景的行车记录仪视频数据集，设计结构化提问框架，围绕天气光照、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标志标线、涉及实体、事故是否发生、撞击位置、可避免性推理等安全相关维度进行问答任务设计。

Result: AUTOPILOT-VQA作为AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026竞赛的一部分发布，提供了一个超越物体识别、聚焦于时间维度和安全感知推理的标准化评估基准，能够评估不同模型在多样化场景下的可靠性。

Conclusion: 该基准有助于推动自动驾驶领域开发更可解释、更鲁棒且更注重安全的视觉语言系统，为实际应用中的安全评估提供标准化工具。

Abstract: Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.

</details>


### [51] [Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08748)
*Kristina Schaaff,Quintus Stierstorfer,Valerie Heckel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 基于77,543名远程教育学生的客观日志数据，对AI学习助手Syntea在高等教育中的使用模式进行了大规模描述性分析，发现其已融入学习常规，但使用情况因人口统计和结构性背景而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育聊天机器人研究多依赖小样本和自我报告调查数据，缺乏基于实际使用行为的大规模证据。

Method: 基于77,543名远程学习者的客观日志数据，分析Syntea的使用模式，考察性别、年龄组、学习集群、学位和学习模式等维度。

Result: Syntea已嵌入许多学习者的学习常规中，但使用情况在不同人口统计和结构性背景之间存在差异。

Conclusion: 研究为AI学习支持的进一步发展提供了实证基础，并贡献了高等教育中教育聊天机器人使用的大规模分析。

Abstract: In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.

</details>


### [52] [Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08758)
*Yifan Zhou,Qihao Yang,Yan Li,Donggang Li,Xiru Hu,Hokin Deng,Ziyang Gong,Xuanyi Zhou,Huacan Wang,Xiangchao Yan,Wanghan Xu,Wenlong Zhang,Shaofeng Zhang,Yue Zhou,Yifan Yang,Zhihang Zhong,Xue Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出IdeaGene-Bench基准，通过模拟科学思想的遗传进化机制（继承、突变、重组等），评估AI系统在科学谱系推理和基于谱系的创意生成能力。实验表明最先进的LLM系统仅达27.3%准确率，存在组合瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准未能评估AI系统是否能够遵循科学思想的继承结构（即从已有工作中继承机制、修复已知局限、重组先前成果），而科学思想的演化类似于生物基因组。

Method: 构建IdeaGene框架，将论文/提案表示为最小化、带类型、有证据支持的Idea Genome对象集合，通过GenomeDiff记录六种进化动力学（继承、突变、丢失、外部导入、新插入）下的对齐关系。包含1,961条谱系轨迹、1,085个Idea Genome对象、920条GenomeDiff记录，覆盖10个科学领域。设计IG-Exam（42种任务类型，1,029实例）和IG-Arena（基于谱系条件的群体进化评分PES）两套评估体系。

Result: 对14个基于LLM的科学家的实验显示，最强系统在谱系推理上仅达27.3%精确准确率。结构化谱系上下文会重新排列系统排名，而非均匀地帮助所有参与者。

Conclusion: 当前AI系统在科学谱系推理和基于谱系的创意生成方面存在组合瓶颈，推理能力严重不足，且不同系统对谱系信息的利用效率差异显著。

Abstract: Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.

</details>

### [53] [DreamCharacter-1: From 3D Generative Foundation Models to Product-Ready Character Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07817)
*Weizhe Liu,Yunjie Wu,Xiangqian Shu,Guangwei Wang,Xiangyu Xu,Peng Li,Yujie Li,Hengkai Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: DreamCharacter-1是一个轻量级后适配框架，通过对预训练3D基础模型进行校准，实现高保真、生产就绪的3D角色生成，包含几何后训练、纹理后训练和推理加速三个组件，在视觉质量和结构鲁棒性上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型在生成高保真、生产级3D角色方面存在不足，缺乏针对角色生成任务的精细校准，无法满足实际应用中对几何细节、纹理质量和部署效率的需求。

Method: 构建于3D基础骨干网络之上，提出三个任务导向组件：(1) 几何后训练——通过几何偏好优化增强细粒度表面细节；(2) 纹理后训练——合成高分辨率纹理并优化遮挡区域外观；(3) 推理加速——支持可扩展部署。

Result: 大量定量和定性实验表明，DreamCharacter-1能够生成视觉上引人注目且结构鲁棒的3D角色资产，在各项指标上持续超越当前最先进的角色生成方法。

Conclusion: DreamCharacter-1作为一种轻量级后适配框架，有效解决了3D基础模型在角色生成任务上的校准问题，为生产级3D角色生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: We present DreamCharacter-1, a lightweight post-adaptation framework that calibrates pretrained 3D foundation models toward high-fidelity, production-ready 3D character generation. Building upon a 3D foundation backbone, our pipeline incorporates three task-oriented components: (1) geometry post-training, which enhances fine-grained surface details through geometric preference optimization; (2) texture post-training, which synthesizes high-resolution textures and refines the appearance of occluded regions; and (3) inference acceleration, which enables scalable deployment. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that DreamCharacter-1 produces visually compelling and structurally robust 3D character assets, consistently surpassing state-of-the-art character generation methods.

</details>


### [54] [GIRAF: Towards Generalizable Human Interactions with Articulated Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07880)
*Xiaohan Zhang,Sebastian Starke,Alexander Winkler,Federica Bogo,Samir Aroudj,Yuting Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种文本条件扩散模型，通过物体中心表示、混合域训练策略和基于接触的数据增强，实现了全身动态与铰接物体的逼真交互，在未见物体配置上超越了现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法局限于静态物体或仅手部操控，无法生成协调的全身运动来接近、操控和移动铰接物体，关键困难在于联合推理运动、精细接触和物体关节，且缺乏大规模配对数据。

Method: 提出三种核心技术：1) 物体中心表示，统一手-物体接触与物体表面；2) 混合域训练策略，平衡运动与交互；3) 基于接触的数据增强，扩大训练多样性。整体采用文本条件扩散模型。

Result: 实验表明该方法在未见物体配置上具有强泛化能力，超越了当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 通过联合建模运动、接触和物体关节点，结合混合域训练和接触增强，有效解决了全身动态与铰接物体交互的泛化难题。

Abstract: Synthesizing realistic full-body human interactions with articulated objects is a fundamental challenge for embodied AI and graphics, with applications in robotics training and virtual agents. Existing models remain limited: some focus on simple activities with static objects, while others restrict attention to hand-only manipulation. This leaves open the problem of generating coordinated full-body motion that approaches, manipulates, and moves articulated objects in a realistic and generalizable way. The key difficulty lies in reasoning jointly about locomotion, fine-grained contact, and object articulation. Models must capture subtle hand-object correspondences that transfer across object geometries, while also producing seamless transitions from navigation to manipulation. At the same time, the scarcity of large-scale paired motion-scene data makes it difficult to generalize across diverse object positions and shapes. We introduce a text-conditioned diffusion model that addresses these challenges through three core ideas: an object-centric representation that unifies hand-object contact with object surfaces, a mixed-domain training strategy that balances locomotion and interaction, and a contact-based augmentation scheme that expands training diversity. Through experiments, our method demonstrated strong generalization to unseen object configurations, surpassing current state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [55] [3D Reconstruction of deciduous Trees using low-cost UAV- and Crane-based Photogrammetry for Monitoring Shoot Elongation across entire Canopies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07905)
*Stephan Nebiker,Micha Tschanz,Nando Amport,Frederik Baumgarten*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文探索利用低成本无人机摄影测量和多相机CraneCam系统，在真实条件下实现对原生落叶乔木整个树冠的3D重建，以测量和监测枝梢伸长（初级生长）。结果表明，使用重量不足250g的消费级无人机可实现5-6 mm的3D点精度和92%-98%的重建完整性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 树木初级生长（枝梢伸长）对气候变化（升温、干旱、晚霜等）的响应机制尚不明确，主要原因在于缺乏合适的监测技术。现有树木径向生长测量方法成熟，但初级生长监测长期被忽视。

Method: 采用低成本无人机摄影测量和多相机CraneCam系统，在两个研究区域进行整个生长季的数据采集。通过摄影测量3D点云的精度、分辨率和完整性分析，并使用3D打印的地面真值枝条评估细部结构重建能力。

Result: 使用重量小于250g的消费级无人机可获得5-6 mm的3D点精度，重建完整性在92%-98%之间（取决于无人机类型）。论文提出了用于评估细部结构重建能力的3D打印地面真值枝条。

Conclusion: 低成本的无人机摄影测量方法能够有效重建树木3D结构并监测枝梢伸长，为研究气候变化对树木初级生长的影响提供了可行的技术手段。未来需进一步探索基于摄影测量点云的完整树木骨架化方法。

Abstract: Tree growth determines how much CO2 is sequestered from the atmosphere and temporarily stored in woody biomass. At the same time tree growth is affected by increasing temperatures, more frequent drought periods, late frosts and other extreme events associated with climate change. While continuous measurements of radial (secondary) tree growth using dendrometers are well established, monitoring of shoot elongation (primary growth) has largely been neglected because suitable measurement techniques are lacking. As a result, the effects of climate change on primary tree growth remain insufficiently understood. This work aims at reconstructing native deciduous trees in 3D as a basis for measuring and monitoring shoot elongation over entire tree canopies. Here we explored the use of low-cost UAV photogrammetry and of a multi-camera CraneCam system under real-world conditions. Data were collected in two study areas over an entire growing season. We present sensor evaluations, photogrammetric data acquisition and processing strategies. A special focus is placed on the analysis of the resulting photogrammetric 3D point clouds in terms of accuracy, resolution and completeness. Results demonstrate 3D point accuracies of 5-6 mm for entire trees using consumer-grade UAVs weighing less than 250 g and a 3D reconstruction completeness between 92% and 98% depending on the UAV type. The paper introduces a novel 3Dprinted ground-truth branch to evaluate the capability to reconstructing fine-detail structures such as thin tree shoots. Finally, we discuss operational challenges and initial experiments towards a skeletonization of entire trees based on photogrammetric point clouds.

</details>


### [56] [Adversarial Decoys: Misdirecting Attention-Based Defenses in ViT](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07922)
*Giulia Marchiori Pietrosanti,Giulio Rossolini,Giorgio Buttazzo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为"对抗性诱饵"(adversarial decoys)的攻击方法，通过独立优化的图像块来转移注意力，从而绕过基于注意力分数的测试时防御，揭示了将注意力大小作为对抗相关性指标的根本局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时防御方法通过抑制注意力分数异常高的图像token来抵御局部对抗攻击（如对抗补丁），但其有效性依赖于注意力与对抗效果之间的强耦合关系。作者旨在揭示这种防御策略的根本缺陷。

Method: 提出对抗性诱饵方法，将误导分类和逃避防御两个目标解耦：原始对抗区域负责诱导错误预测，独立的诱饵图案负责操纵防御所用的注意力排名。通过层级的损失函数提高目标token的注意力分数，使其超过其他非目标token。诱饵独立于底层攻击优化，因此可适配任何现有对抗补丁攻击。

Result: 在ImageNet上针对多种ViT架构和攻击方法的实验表明，对抗性诱饵能够将高注意力分数从真正的对抗区域转移开，同时保持大部分攻击有效性。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了使用注意力大小作为对抗相关性指标存在根本性局限，当前的注意力防御机制可以被轻易绕过。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) remain vulnerable to localized adversarial attacks, e.g., adversarial patches, while recent test-time defenses mitigate them by suppressing image tokens with abnormally high attention scores. These defenses exploit a strong coupling between attention and adversarial effectiveness: adversarial tokens often need to attract substantial attention to influence the prediction. We introduce adversarial decoys, independently optimized image patches that redirect the attention, and therefore related defenses, toward selected target tokens. Rather than jointly optimizing misclassifications and defense evasion, our approach decouples the two objectives: the original adversarial region induces the incorrect prediction, while a separate decoy manipulates the attention ranking used by the defense. A layer-wise objective increases target-token attention and promotes these tokens above competing non-target ones. Since the decoy is optimized independently of the underlying attack, the method is attack-agnostic and can be easily integrated with any existing adversarial patch attack. Experiments on ImageNet across multiple ViT architectures and attacks show that decoys can redirect high attention scores away from the true adversarial region while preserving much of the attack effectiveness. These results reveal a fundamental limitation of using attention magnitude as an indicator of adversarial relevance.

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Thermal Imaging: Inferring Thermophysical Properties from Time-Resolved Thermal Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07962)
*Chenghao Xu,Malcolm Mielle,Olga Fink*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ThermoField框架，通过可微分热传导模拟统一热场景重建与热物理参数估计，能够联合重建几何、估计空间变化热扩散率，并预测未见环境条件下的热演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有热场景重建方法能恢复3D环境中的温度场但无法识别控制热演化的热物理属性，而逆方法虽能提供物理可解释的参数估计但依赖于简化几何和受控实验条件，缺乏统一框架。

Method: 将空间变化的热物理量表示为神经场，利用场景几何、热传导物理和时序热观测作为约束，通过可微分热传导求解器联合优化，实现端到端的物理可解释参数推断。

Result: 成功实现了几何重建、空间变化热扩散率估计以及在未见环境条件下的热演化预测，验证了联合重建与参数估计的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架在热场景重建与逆热传导分析之间建立了桥梁，为从热观测中进行几何重建、热物理属性估计和预测性热仿真提供了统一方法。

Abstract: Inferring latent physical properties from sensory observations is a fundamental challenge in machine perception. Among available sensing modalities, thermal imaging is particularly promising because temperature evolution is directly governed by heat-transfer physics and therefore encodes information about underlying thermophysical properties of a scene. Recovering spatially resolved thermophysical properties from thermal observations could transform applications ranging from digital twins and infrastructure monitoring to robotics and scientific imaging. However, existing thermal scene reconstruction methods can recover temperature fields in complex 3D environments without identifying the thermophyiscal properties that govern thermal evolution, whereas inverse methods provide physically interpretable parameter estimation but typically rely on simplified geometries and controlled experimental conditions.
  Here we introduce ThermoField, a framework that unifies thermal scene reconstruction and thermophysical parameter estimation through differentiable heat-transfer simulation. The proposed framework represents these quantities as spatially varying neural fields and constrains them through scene geometry, governing heat-transfer physics, and temporal thermal observations. We demonstrate that ThermoField jointly reconstructs geometry, estimates spatially varying thermal diffusivity, and predicts thermal evolution under previously unseen environmental conditions. By integrating neural scene representations with differentiable heat-transfer solver, the framework enables physically interpretable parameter inference in complex 3D scenes. Our results establish a bridge between thermal scene reconstruction and inverse heat-transfer analysis, providing a unified approach for geometry reconstruction, thermophysical property estimation, and predictive thermal simulation from thermal observations.

</details>


### [58] [LOGOS: Language-guided Oriented Object Detection in Aerial Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08004)
*Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LOGOS是一种基于Transformer的文本提示引导定向目标检测方法，通过在遥感图像中引入提示调制内容查询，动态调整模型关注点，在DOTA数据集上优于现有方法，尤其适用于密集和旋转目标场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 遥感图像中的定向目标检测面临目标方向多变、密度不均、背景复杂等挑战，传统方法存在角度不连续性、固定查询尺寸、对稀疏或杂乱场景处理效率低等问题。

Method: 提出LOGOS框架，基于Transformer架构，利用文本提示引导定向目标检测。核心是提示调制内容查询（prompt-modulated content queries），根据提供的文本动态调整模型关注焦点。

Result: 在DOTA数据集上的大量实验表明，LOGOS在密集排列和旋转目标场景中优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: LOGOS为遥感应用中的定向目标检测提供了更好的鲁棒性和可扩展性，是提升该领域性能的重要进展。

Abstract: Object detection in geospatial scenes, such as satellite and aerial imagery, poses significant challenges due to the varying orientations and densities of objects, as well as the complex backgrounds inherent to remote sensing imagery. Traditional methods for oriented object detection have struggled to address issues such as angular discontinuity, fixed query sizes, and inefficiencies in handling sparse or cluttered scenes. In this paper, we propose LOGOS, a novel transformer-based approach that leverages textual prompts to guide the detection of oriented objects in aerial scenes. In particular, our proposed approach incorporates prompt-modulated content queries to dynamically adjust the model's focus based on the provided text, thereby improving object detection accuracy in complex environments. Empirically, extensive experiments on the DOTA dataset demonstrate that LOGOS outperforms existing state-of-the-art methods, particularly in densely packed and rotated object scenarios. Our approach offers a significant step forward in improving the robustness and scalability of oriented object detection in remote sensing applications.

</details>


### [59] [FedTR: Federated Learning Framework with Transfer Learning for Industrial Visual Inspection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08014)
*Vikash Sathiamoorthy,Shuo Huai,Hao Kong,Di Liu,Wendy Yong Yi Loy,Christian Makaya,Daren Ho,Ravi Subramaniam,Qian Lin,Weichen Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FedTR，一种结合迁移学习的联邦学习框架，用于工业视觉检测中的标签缺陷识别。该方法先在公开数据集上预训练模型，再在分布式私有数据上进行联邦微调，在喷墨打印标签数据集上实现了同质数据95.5%和异质数据94.2%的词级准确率，性能与集中式训练相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习在工业视觉检测中存在数据可用性有限和检测任务复杂性的挑战，导致模型性能受限。

Method: 提出FedTR框架，先利用公开数据集进行迁移学习的预训练，再在分布式私有数据上进行联邦学习微调，实现端到端文本识别。

Result: 在私有喷墨打印标签数据集上，FedTR在同质和异质数据上分别达到95.5%和94.2%的词级准确率，性能与集中式训练相当。

Conclusion: FedTR有效解决了联邦学习在工业视觉检测中的数据稀缺和任务复杂性问题，是一种可行的标签缺陷识别方案。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) is a collaborative learning scheme to train deep learning models, where collaborating parties can consolidate their models without sharing local data with other parties, hence preserving data privacy. Nevertheless, when implementing FL in Industrial visual inspection (IVI), the constraints posed by limited data availability and the intricate nature of the inspection tasks significantly impact the performance of the resulting model. This paper introduces FedTR, a novel FL framework incorporating transfer learning designed for Autonomous IVI, focusing on the challenging task of identifying label defects through end-to-end text recognition. Transfer learning is a method that leverages the knowledge of a pre-trained model to adapt to a different dataset. FedTR initially trains the model using a publicly available dataset, after which performs the essential federated learning process with model fine-tuning on the distributed and limited private data. Extensive experiment results demonstrate the effectiveness and feasibility of FedTR on private ink cartridge datasets for label defect identification. FedTR achieves an end-to-end text recognition word-level accuracy of 95.5% and 94.2% on homogeneous and heterogeneous data respectively. Additionally, it attains performance levels that are on par with those achieved through centralized training.

</details>


### [60] [LightCrafter: PBR-Conditioned Video Diffusion Refinement for Controllable and Consistent Relighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08016)
*Zixin Guo,Yehonathan Litman,Yifeng He,John Miller,Chuhan Chen,Deva Ramanan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LightCrafter提出了一种混合管线，将视频重光照重新定义为代理视频的视频翻译：通过将输入视频在目标光照下的PBR渲染结果翻译为最终目标，而非直接翻译输入视频。该方法在现有真实世界重光照基准上超越了先前最优方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频重光照方法存在两类问题：(1) 基于逆渲染和正向渲染的方法受限于噪声重建和难以建模全局光照等效果；(2) 基于生成式视频到视频翻译的方法受限于光照控制灵活性和时序稳定性，且依赖输入/重光照训练对的可用性。

Method: LightCrafter采用混合管线：首先将输入视频在目标光照下进行PBR渲染得到代理视频，然后将该代理视频通过扩散模型（基于CogVideoX后训练）翻译为最终重光照结果。该方法将光照目标烘焙到PBR代理中，避免了扩散模型学习光照概念（如环境贴图）的需求。

Result: 在真实世界重光照基准上超越了先前最优方法；PBR渲染本身已优于部分先前工作但在全局光照等效果上存在不足；在合成视频对和真实非配对视频上后训练CogVideoX来捕捉这些效果。

Conclusion: LightCrafter通过混合PBR和生成式方法的优势，实现了更精细的光照控制、长时时序一致性，并在现有基准上达到最佳性能。将开源数据集、基准、指标和代码。

Abstract: Video relighting requires balancing long-form temporal consistency with a physically grounded understanding of light transport, which depends on accurate estimation of intrinsic scene properties such as materials, geometry, and illumination. Existing methods follow two paradigms: (1) reconstruct a video's photometric properties via inverse rendering and relight them to a target illumination via forward rendering, using physically-based rendering (PBR) or a neural renderer; these suffer from noisy reconstructions and struggle with hard-to-model effects such as global illumination. (2) Frame the task as generative video-to-video translation conditioned on relighting targets (a target environment map or text); this limits relighting control and temporal stability, since diffusion models struggle to translate long-form videos, and is constrained by the availability of input/relit training pairs. We propose LightCrafter, a hybrid pipeline that reformulates video relighting as video translation of a proxy video: rather than translating the input video directly to the target, we translate a PBR rendering of the input under the target illumination to the final target. This bakes illumination targets into the PBR proxy, removing the need to teach the diffusion model illumination concepts like environment maps, and enables more intricate lighting control while naturally providing long-form temporal consistency. We show PBR renders alone already outperform some prior art but struggle with effects like global illumination; to capture these, we leverage photometric priors in video generation models by post-training CogVideoX on synthetic video pairs and real-world unpaired videos. We outperform prior state-of-the-art on existing real-world relighting benchmarks and contribute a synthetic benchmark for further analysis. We will release our dataset, benchmark, metrics, and code.

</details>


### [61] [SAGA: Stable Acceleration Guidance for Autoregressive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08020)
*Thanh-Nhan Vo,Trong-Thuan Nguyen,Trung-Hoang Le,Tam V. Nguyen,Minh-Triet Tran*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGA，一种无需训练的稳定加速引导方法，用于自回归视频生成。通过频谱运动分析发现离散潜变量加速度可作为揭示高频时间扰动信号，并基于Slepian投影的加速度域频谱引导目标与结构化自回归噪声初始化策略，有效减少时序误差累积，提升视频生成的时间质量与视觉保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在因果上下文中重复复用已生成潜变量会放大时序误差，导致闪烁、运动抖动和结构漂移。作者从频谱运动学角度分析该失效模式，发现离散潜变量加速度可作为揭示不稳定高频时间扰动的有效信号。

Method: SAGA包含两个关键技术：(1) 基于有限窗Slepian投影的加速度域频谱引导目标，用于抑制高频时序扰动；(2) 结构化的自回归噪声初始化策略，抑制短程时序相关性同时保留长程运动结构。该方法无需重新训练或修改主干网络，可直接应用于现有分块自回归扩散模型。

Result: 实验表明SAGA在多个自回归扩散模型上持续提升时序质量。在Self-Forcing模型上，时序质量从97.30提升至97.91，图像质量从69.60提升至70.51。频谱分析和人工偏好研究证明SAGA在保持视觉保真度的同时减少了时序不稳定性。

Conclusion: SAGA作为一种无需训练的即插即用方法，通过加速度域频谱引导和结构化噪声初始化有效解决了自回归视频生成中的时序误差累积问题，在保持视觉质量的前提下显著提升了生成视频的时序稳定性。

Abstract: Autoregressive video diffusion enables efficient streaming and long-horizon video generation, but repeatedly reusing generated latents as causal context can amplify temporal errors, resulting in flickering, motion jitter, and structural drift. In this paper, we investigate this failure mode from a spectral kinematic perspective and identify discrete latent acceleration as an effective signal for revealing unstable high-frequency temporal perturbations. To this end, we propose SAGA, a training-free \textbf{\textit{s}}table \textbf{\textit{a}}cceleration \textbf{\textit{g}}uidance approach for \textbf{\textit{a}}utoregressive video generation. SAGA integrates an acceleration domain spectral guidance objective based on finite-window Slepian projections with a structured autoregressive noise initialization strategy that suppresses short-range temporal correlations while preserving long-range motion structure. Without retraining or modifying the backbone, SAGA can be directly applied to existing chunk-wise autoregressive diffusion models, which is the prevalent setting for high-quality generation. Extensive experiments show that SAGA consistently improves temporal quality across multiple autoregressive diffusion models. On Self-Forcing, SAGA improves Temporal Quality from 97.30 to 97.91 and Image Quality from 69.60 to 70.51. Moreover, spectral analysis and human preference studies demonstrate that SAGA reduces temporal instability while maintaining visual fidelity.

</details>


### [62] [APIVOT: Adaptive Planning with Interleaved Vision-Language Thoughts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08024)
*Emily Jin,Joy Hsu,Yiqing Xu,Weiyu Liu,Nick Haber,Jiajun Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: APIVOT是一种基于VLM的自适应规划器，通过交错使用语言和视觉思维进行长时域机器人规划，在语义推理和几何可行性验证方面均优于现有方法，尤其在空间受限场景下表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时域机器人规划需要同时推理语义任务结构和几何可行性，现有方法难以兼顾两者，因此需要一种能自适应结合语言和视觉推理的规划框架。

Method: 提出APIVOT框架，它基于视觉语言模型（VLM），自适应地交错使用语言思维（用于语义推理）和视觉思维（作为想象的未来状态，用于内部验证几何可行性），以实现长时域规划。

Result: 在长时域厨房任务中，APIVOT优于通用VLM和先前规划框架，在空间受限场景下提升最大，并展现出有意义的模态选择行为。

Conclusion: 自适应交错视觉-语言思维能够同时提升规划成功率和推理效率，APIVOT证明了这一策略的有效性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot planning requires jointly reasoning over semantic task structure and geometric feasibility. To successfully execute a task, a robot must decompose goals, select task-relevant objects, and sequence actions, while ensuring that plans satisfy spatial constraints such as limited free space and object collisions. In this work, we propose APIVOT, a VLM-based planner that adaptively interleaves language and visual thoughts for long-horizon planning. APIVOT learns to leverage language for semantic reasoning, while using visual thoughts as imagined future states for internal verification of geometric feasibility. On long-horizon kitchen tasks, APIVOT outperforms general-purpose VLMs and prior planning frameworks, achieving the largest gains in spatially constrained settings. We find that APIVOT learns meaningful modality selection behavior, demonstrating that adaptive interleaving of vision-language thoughts improves both planning success and reasoning efficiency.

</details>


### [63] [Post-Training in End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08072)
*Ruining Yang,Muxing Wang,Yixiao Chen,Tongfei Guo,Yi Xu,Can Cui,Zichong Yang,Yitian Zhang,Ziran Wang,Yun Fu,Lili Su*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了自动驾驶中端到端模型的后训练技术，指出单纯模仿学习不足以应对安全关键和交互密集的驾驶环境，并根据监督形式将现有后训练方法分为四大类，讨论了各自的能力、局限与开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶中的端到端模型（包括视觉-语言-动作模型和轨迹生成规划器）在安全关键和交互密集的环境下，传统的开环模仿学习存在三个主要问题：(1) 微小执行误差随时间累积；(2) 训练数据中缺乏恢复行为；(3) 点状标签无法捕捉长程目标（如安全性和驾驶舒适性）。这些局限性促使研究者转向后训练技术，以进一步优化驾驶策略。

Method: 本文以综述形式，首先界定了自动驾驶后训练的范围，然后根据监督信号的来源，将现有后训练文献组织为四大类：(1) 基于人工反馈的强化学习；(2) 基于任务奖励的优化；(3) 自我对弈与数据增强；(4) 其他监督形式。对每一类方法分析了其能力、局限及开放挑战。

Result: 梳理了自动驾驶后训练技术的完整图谱，明确了四大监督范式各自的特点与适用场景，指出了当前后训练方法在可靠性、效率和泛化性方面仍面临的挑战。

Conclusion: 后训练技术是提升自动驾驶端到端模型安全性和鲁棒性的关键环节，但该领域仍处于早期阶段，亟需更高效、更可靠的训练方法和评估基准。本文旨在为后续研究提供系统化的理论基础和方向指引。

Abstract: End-to-end models that map multimodal inputs directly to future trajectories/maneuvers have emerged as an increasingly prominent research paradigm in autonomous driving. This class of models includes both Vision-Language-Action models and trajectory-generative planners. Unlike classic machine learning applications, autonomous vehicles operate in safety-critical and interaction-intensive environments where traditional open-loop imitation of expert demonstrations is not sufficient to ensure reliability. In particular, small execution errors can accumulate over time, while recovery behaviors are scarce in training data. In addition, long-horizon objectives such as safety and driving comfort are not captured by pointwise labels either. These limitations have motivated a shift toward post-training techniques, which further refine driving policies beyond pure imitation. This survey presents a unified view of post-training for autonomous driving by defining its scope and organizing the existing literature into four major families based on the form of supervision they use. For each family, we discuss its capabilities, limitations, and open challenges. We aim to facilitate a systematic understanding of this emerging area and stimulate future research on reliable and efficient post-training for autonomous driving.

</details>


### [64] [UAV-OVVIS: Unmanned Aerial Vehicles Also Need Open-Vocabulary Video Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08075)
*Mingyu Dou,Shi Qiu,Ming Hu,Yifan Chen,Zhe Sun*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了无人机开放词汇视频实例分割任务(UAV-OVVIS)及无需训练的AeroTrack框架，通过复用视觉基础模型实现开放场景下无人机视频的实例级分割追踪，并构建了包含9类目标、8279条轨迹的AeroVIS基准，实验表明该方法显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无人机视频感知依赖预定义类别的框级定位与轨迹关联，难以同时支持开放场景中的灵活查询和细粒度实例级动态理解。

Method: 提出AeroTrack，一种无需训练的统一框架，核心包括周期性开放词汇检测、短片段掩码传播、跨片段身份统一，复用现有视觉基础模型实现UAV-OVVIS。

Result: 构建AeroVIS基准（9类目标、8279条轨迹），实验表明AeroTrack大幅优于现有通用视频实例分割方法，具备强开放词汇鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Conclusion: AeroTrack与AeroVIS作为UAV-OVVIS的统一框架与基准，可支持未来相关研究。

Abstract: Unmanned Aerial Vehicle (UAV) videos are widely used in traffic monitoring, urban management, and emergency rescue. However, existing UAV video perception mainly relies on box-level localization and trajectory association under predefined categories, making it difficult to simultaneously support flexible queries and fine-grained instance-level dynamic understanding in open scenarios. To this end, we introduce a new task, UAV Open-Vocabulary Video Instance Segmentation (UAV-OVVIS), which discovers targets in UAV videos according to open-vocabulary queries and outputs instance-level segmentation trajectories with globally consistent identities. Considering the scarcity of instance-level annotations in UAV scenarios, we propose AeroTrack, a training-free unified framework. AeroTrack centers on periodic open-vocabulary detection, short-segment mask propagation, and cross-segment identity unification, reusing existing visual foundation models to enable UAV-OVVIS. Based on this framework, we instantiate five AeroTrack variants and construct AeroVIS, an evaluation benchmark for UAV-OVVIS containing 9 UAV object categories and 8,279 trajectories. Experiments show that AeroTrack substantially outperforms existing general video instance segmentation methods in UAV scenarios and demonstrates strong open-vocabulary robustness and generalization. To support future research, we release AeroTrack and AeroVIS as a unified framework and benchmark for UAV-OVVIS.

</details>


### [65] [LDFE: Laplacian Decoupled Feature Enhancement Block for Dual-Stream CNN-based RGB-IR Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08076)
*Wenhao Dong,Xiaoyan Luo,Linlin Yang,Haodong Zhu,Xiaorong Shi,Guodong Guo,Baochang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出拉普拉斯解耦特征增强模块（LDFE），通过全局-局部分解、去噪、融合和重建的序列化设计，结合全局状态空间增强模块（GS2E）和局部卷积相关增强模块（LC2E），实现RGB与红外图像的双流特征融合，在六个数据集上超越SOTA方法2.0%-6.2%mAP。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB与红外图像的互补信息能显著提升极端条件下的目标检测性能，现有方法多基于YOLO构建双流CNN骨干并聚焦特征融合设计，但缺乏同时考虑模态特性与结构特性的融合机制。

Method: 提出LDFE模块，基于拉普拉斯金字塔将特征分解为全局和局部分量，分别通过GS2E（双分支架构，交叉模态注意力抑制主模态噪声，状态空间模型捕获长程依赖，主辅模态交替角色实现双向交互）和LC2E（抑制局部噪声，利用空间和通道维度的三重卷积提取细粒度细节）进行去噪与融合，最后重建特征。

Result: 在M3FD、DroneVehicle、LLVIP、FLIR-Aligned、KAIST和VEDAI数据集上，mAP分别超越SOTA方法6.2%、3.7%、4.7%、2.3%、4.1%和2.0%。

Conclusion: LDFE模块通过全局-局部分解与分治去噪融合策略，有效提升了双流RGB-IR目标检测的性能，验证了同时考虑模态特性和结构特性的融合设计的有效性。

Abstract: The complementary information between RGB and IR images can significantly enhance object detection performance under extreme conditions. Existing methods prefer dual-stream CNN backbones built upon YOLO for feature extraction and focus on the design of feature fusion. In this paper, we introduce the Laplacian Decoupled Feature Enhancement block (LDFE) to fuse features from different stages of the dual-stream CNN backbone. By design, LDFE simultaneously considers the characteristics of modalities and structures for feature fusion by employing global-local decomposition, denoising, fusion, and reconstruction, sequentially. The LDFE first separates features into global and local components based on Laplacian Pyramid, and then performs denoising and fusion based on Global State Space Enhancement module (GS2E) and Local Convolutional Correlation Enhancement module (LC2E) separately. Specifically, the GS2E conducts a two-branch architecture for the main and auxiliary modalities. It dynamically suppresses noise in the main modality through cross-modal attention derived from the auxiliary modality, while employing a State Space Model to capture long-range dependencies within the global feature representations of the main modality. To obtain bidirectional interaction, the two modalities systematically alternate their main/auxiliary roles. Moreover, the LC2E suppresses noise in local features and leverages spatial and channel dimension along with triple convolution to extract fine-grained details for fusion. These innovative designs achieve a significant performance improvement, with mAP surpassing the SOTA methods 6.2%, 3.7%, 4.7%, 2.3%, 4.1% and 2.0% on M3FD, DroneVehicle, LLVIP, FLIR-Aligned, KAIST and VEDAI datasets,respectively.

</details>


### [66] [Mixture of Enhanced-View Experts for Multi-Query Vehicle ReID and A Large-Scale Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08085)
*Aihua Zheng,Jie Zhen,Chenglong Li,Jiaxiang Wang,Jin Tang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为EV-MoE（混合增强视角专家）的多查询车辆重识别方法，通过视角特定特征增强模块（VFEM）和动态多视角融合模块（DMFM）改进特征表示，并引入多视角对齐损失（MAL）进行双向跨视角对比学习与重构约束。此外，收集了大规模数据集LCRI-1K（1,090个身份，107,805张图像，23,637个摄像头）以评估真实环境下的多查询车辆ReID性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理多查询车辆ReID时存在简单的特征融合问题，容易忽略重要的视角信息和跨视角关系，导致特征学习不鲁棒。

Method: 提出EV-MoE方法，包含视角特定特征增强子模块（VFEM）和动态多视角融合子模块（DMFM），并引入多视角对齐损失（MAL）通过双向跨视角对比学习与重构约束对齐特征。

Result: 在收集的大规模LCRI-1K数据集上进行了大量实验，验证了CAFNet在解决多查询车辆ReID问题上的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: EV-MoE方法通过增强每个视角的特征表示并利用MoE高效集成，有效解决了多查询车辆ReID中的特征融合与跨视角关系问题。

Abstract: Multi-query vehicle ReID aims to leverage complementary information from diverse views for robust feature learning. However, current methods suffer from simplistic feature fusion and thus easily ignores some important view information and cross-view relationships. To handle these problems, this work presents a novel approach called Mixture of Enhanced-View Experts (EV-MoE), which enhances the feature representation of each view and efficiently integrate the view-specific enhanced features by MoE, for robust multi-query ReID. In particular, we design a mixture of enhanced-view experts module, which consists of two parts including view-specific feature enhancement sub-Module (VFEM) and dynamic multi-view fusion sub-Module (DMFM). Moreover, we further introduce Multi-view Alignment Loss (MAL), which aligns features through bidirectional crossview contrastive learning and reconstruction constraints, addressing the challenges of consistency between multi-query features and single-image features. In addition, to evaluate multi-query ReID in real-world environments, we collect LCRI-1K, a largescale vehicle ReID dataset with 1,090 identities, 107,805 images, across 23,637 cameras, where each vehicle appears in an average of 67.5 cameras, providing a comprehensive benchmark to test the robustness in complex environments. Extensive experiments demonstrate the robustness of CAFNet in addressing the multiquery vehicle ReID problem. The code is available at https: //github.com/xiaozhen28/CAFNet.

</details>


### [67] [GRE-Diff: Gaussian Room Embeddings for Structured Layout Diffusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08086)
*Jing Wang,Haoran Xiong,Zihao Yan,Minglun Gong,Hui Huang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GRE-Diff是一个基于扩散模型的交互式框架，通过高斯房间嵌入（GRE）和人类介入编辑，实现受约束的公寓楼层平面图自动生成与编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 设计功能性与美学一致的楼层平面图需要探索巨大的房间排列空间，这对人类设计师而言任务繁重且容易失控，亟需自动化工具辅助。

Method: 提出GRE-Diff框架，核心为高斯房间嵌入（GRE）——一种将每个房间建模为空间高斯分布的连续隐式表示，捕捉其位置和范围；结合扩散模型生成、LLM解析指令或GUI交互实现约束指定与实时人机协同编辑。

Result: 在RPLAN数据集上的实验表明，GRE-Diff能够生成高质量、约束感知且可编辑的多边形布局。

Conclusion: 该工作为连接AI驱动的自动化与人类创造力在空间设计中的融合提供了实用的一步。

Abstract: Designing functional and aesthetically coherent floor plans requires exploring a vast space of possible room arrangements, a task that quickly becomes overwhelming for human designers. In this paper, we propose GRE-Diff, a controllable and interactive diffusion-based framework that automates the creation and editing of apartment floor plans under user-specified constraints.
  By combining AI-generated suggestions with real-time, human-in-the-loop editing, the system enables users to specify room types, room counts, boundary shapes, and editing operations through LLM-parsed instructions or GUI-based interaction.
  It then generates a diverse set of plausible and well-structured designs for refinement.
  At the core of our approach is Gaussian Room Embedding (GRE), a continuous latent representation that models each room as a spatial Gaussian distribution capturing its location and extent.
  Extensive experiments on the RPLAN dataset show that GRE-Diff produces high-quality, constraint-aware, and editable polygonal layouts, offering a practical step toward bridging AI-driven automation and human creativity in spatial design.

</details>


### [68] [EVIS: A Physics-Grounded Event Camera Plugin for NVIDIA Isaac Sim](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08098)
*Linli Shi,Ruijun Zhang,Ziyun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EVIS是一个基于物理的事件相机插件，集成于NVIDIA Isaac Sim，可在物理仿真器中生成高帧率、完全标注的事件流，支持运动向量插值实现单GPU实时生成，并兼容预训练事件网络。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机具有微秒级时间分辨率、低延迟和高动态范围等优势，但针对特定机器人和场景的标注事件数据稀缺且采集成本高昂，阻碍了基于事件的感知与控制方法的发展。

Method: 实现了一个保真度高的对数强度对比度事件模型，包含逐像素异步参考更新；支持从普通RGB相机迁移；提供插值选项，仅渲染稀疏关键帧并通过双向运动向量变形合成中间帧，实现单GPU实时生成；可选传感器噪声和运动模糊模块。

Result: 在Isaac Sim/Isaac Lab场景中可直接生成高帧率、完全标注的事件流，兼容预训练事件网络用于下游任务，单GPU即可实现实时生成。

Conclusion: EVIS通过物理仿真环境提供了低成本、高保真的事件相机数据生成方案，加速基于事件感知和控制的机器人研究发展。

Abstract: Event cameras offer microsecond temporal resolution, low latency, and high dynamic range, making them attractive for robotics. However, labeled event-camera data for a specific robot and scene is scarce and expensive to collect, which slows the development of event-based perception and control. We present EVIS: a physics-grounded event camera plugin for NVIDIA Isaac Sim that generates high-rate, fully labeled event streams directly inside a physics simulator. The plugin implements a faithful log-intensity contrast event model with per-pixel asynchronous reference updates; it migrates from a normal RGB camera with few changes and integrates into any Isaac Sim / Isaac Lab scene, inheriting the simulator's physics and frame-perfect ground truth. It is fully configurable, and offers an interpolation option that renders only sparse keyframes and synthesizes the in-between frames through bidirectional motion-vector warping, making real-time generation on a single GPU possible. Optional sensor noise and motion blur further narrow the gap to real cameras. The generated streams are directly usable by pretrained event networks for downstream tasks. Code repository: https://github.com/spikelab-jhu/isaac-sim-event-camera-plugin

</details>


### [69] [VSRo-200: A Romanian Visual Speech Recognition Dataset for Studying Supervision and Multimodal Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08112)
*Iulia-Maria Udrea,Alexandra Diaconu,Bogdan Alexe*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了VSRo-200，首个罗马尼亚语视觉语音识别（唇读）大规模数据集，包含200小时真实播客视频，并系统研究了监督质量、领域泛化和多模态融合在低资源场景下的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉语音识别研究主要集中于英语等资源丰富的语言，而低资源语言（如罗马尼亚语）缺乏大规模数据集，限制了该领域的发展。

Method: 构建200小时罗马尼亚语播客视频数据集，伪标签由微调的罗马尼亚语ASR模型生成，其中100小时子集额外提供人工标注。基于此数据集建立基准，系统比较监督质量（人工 vs. 伪标签）、评估领域漂移鲁棒性，并分析噪声条件下音视频融合的效果。

Result: 人工标注在固定数据规模下性能更优，但伪标签可通过扩展数据规模持续提升性能。多模态融合在噪声条件下显著优于纯音频模型。在VSRo-200上学到的表示可有效迁移至LRRo孤立词识别基准，大幅超越此前最优结果。

Conclusion: VSRo-200为低资源视觉语音识别研究提供了监督质量、领域泛化和多模态融合的全新测试平台。

Abstract: We introduce VSRo-200, the first large-scale dataset for visual speech recognition (lip reading) in Romanian, comprising 200 hours of real-world podcast videos. All samples are annotated with pseudo-labels generated by a fine-tuned Romanian ASR model, while a subset of 100 hours is additionally transcribed by humans, enabling controlled analysis of supervision quality under a unified framework. Building on this dataset, we establish a benchmark for visual speech recognition in low-resource settings. We systematically study the impact of supervision quality, showing that while human annotations provide better performance at fixed data scales, pseudo-labels enable continued improvements through scalability. We further evaluate robustness under domain shift using curated out-of-distribution (OOD) test sets, and analyze audio-visual speech recognition (AVSR) under noisy conditions, where multimodal fusion significantly improves robustness compared to audio-only models. Finally, we demonstrate that representations learned on VSRo-200 transfer effectively to the LRRo benchmark for isolated word recognition, substantially outperforming previously reported results. Overall, VSRo-200 provides a new testbed for studying supervision, domain generalization, and multimodal fusion in low-resource visual speech recognition.

</details>


### [70] [Understanding and Mitigating the Video-Action Generalization Gap via Temporal Ratio](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08127)
*Utkarsh A. Mishra,Yongxin Chen,Danfei Xu,Yang Liu,Xi Chen,Jiayuan Mao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现视频基础模型在微调为动作模型后会丧失组合性先验（视频-动作泛化差距），提出时间比率（TR）作为衡量模型对未来潜在表征依赖程度的注意力指标，并基于此设计了一种推理时自适应引导方法，在LIBERO和真实任务中缩小了组合泛化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式视频基础模型具有强大的组合性先验，但经过机器人动作数据微调后的世界动作模型和视频动作模型往往会丢失这些先验，现有研究对这种现象缺乏系统性理解。

Method: 1) 系统评估视频动作模型的设计空间，发现标准设计选择无法产生涌现解释模式；2) 提出时间比率（TR），一种基于注意力机制衡量动作头依赖未来潜在表征程度的指标；3) 基于TR的两个关键性质（预测组合泛化能力、随任务阶段动态变化），提出推理时自适应引导方法，动态增强组合性视频条件信号。

Result: 在LIBERO基准和真实世界任务上，所提方法有效缓解了分布外与分布内组合泛化差距。

Conclusion: TR能有效预测模型的组合泛化能力，利用其内在的注意力模式进行推理时自适应引导能够弥补视频-动作模型的泛化差距。

Abstract: Generative video foundation models exhibit strong compositional priors, yet world-action models (WAMs) and video-action models (VAMs) often lose these priors after finetuning on robotic action data. We refer to this discrepancy as the video-action generalization gap. In this paper, we systematically investigate this gap by evaluating a comprehensive design space of VAMs, demonstrating that standard design choices yield no emergent explanation pattern. To explain this behavior, we introduce the Temporal Ratio (TR), an attention-based measure of how strongly the action head relies on future latent rollouts relative to the anchored current frame. TR has two key properties: first, a model's structural reliance on future-predictive latents, measured via TR, acts as a predictor of its compositional generalization capacity; second, it natively fluctuates based on task phase, shifting attention to future frames during planning and reverting to the present frame for precise manipulation. Finally, based on these findings, we propose an inference-time adaptive guidance method, which exploits this intrinsic feature attention pattern to dynamically amplify compositional video conditioning signals precisely when the policy relies on future rollouts. Evaluated on the LIBERO benchmark and real-world tasks, our approach mitigates the OOD-ID compositional generalization gap. More details: https://umishra.me/temporal-ratio/

</details>


### [71] [Unified Face Attack Detection via Fine-Grained Semantic Guidance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08156)
*Ning Jiang,Shijie Yu,Dingheng Zeng,Haiyang Yi,Yanhong Liu,Haifeng Shen,Ying Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文在MS-UFAD数据集基础上，为每张攻击图像添加细粒度伪造线索文本描述，并提出DAF-Net网络来利用这些文本信息进行人脸伪造检测，实验证明该方法优于纯视觉方法和粗粒度描述方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据集缺乏详细的伪造线索文本描述，导致大多数方法仅将人脸攻击检测视为视觉识别任务，忽略了文本信息对检测的帮助。

Method: 基于包含800万攻击图像的大规模MS-UFAD数据集，为每张图像添加细粒度的伪造线索文本描述，并提出双对齐伪造网络(DAF-Net)来充分利用这些文本信息。

Result: 该方法提取了更具泛化性和语义意义的伪造表示，性能优于纯视觉方法和基于粗粒度描述的方法。

Conclusion: 引入细粒度文本描述作为辅助信息，可以有效提升人脸伪造检测的性能和泛化能力。

Abstract: The growing applications of facial recognition systems are accompanied by increasingly diverse security threats. Existing datasets lack detailed textual descriptions of forgery cues, leading most prior methods to treat face attack detection primarily as a visual recognition task. In this paper, building upon the large-scale MS-UFAD dataset which contains over 8 million attack images, we enrich each image with a fine-grained textual description of forgery cues. Furthermore, we propose a Dual Alignment Forgery Network(DAF-Net) to better leverage these textual information. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach extracts more generalizable and semantically meaningful forgery representations from attack images, outperforming both vision-only methods and approaches based on coarse-grained descriptions.

</details>


### [72] [ProsMAE: Multi-Source MAE Pretraining for ISUP Grade Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08162)
*Anna Jung,Kyeonghun Kim,Youngung Han,Eunseob Choi,Jiwon Yang,Ken Ying-Kai Liao,Hyuk-Jae Lee,Nam-Joon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProsMAE，一种基于多源掩码自编码器的组织病理学表示学习框架，使用来自PANDA、CAMELYON17和BRACS的多源组织切片数据进行预训练，并将学习到的编码器迁移至前列腺癌ISUP分级任务（ProsCLS），在PANDA数据集上取得了优于vanilla MAE的验证QWK评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全切片图像（WSI）存在千兆像素尺度、染色差异、扫描仪差异、组织伪影及专家标注有限等问题，使得鲁棒模型训练极具挑战，亟需一种能处理多源数据异质性的表示学习方法。

Method: 提出ProsMAE，采用多源掩码自编码器框架，利用来自PANDA（前列腺癌）、CAMELYON17（淋巴结转移）和BRACS（乳腺癌分型）的组织切片图像块进行预训练，使编码器暴露于多样化的组织形态和采集条件；随后将冻结的编码器与线性分类头结合，形成ProsCLS用于ISUP分级分类。

Result: 在PANDA数据集的特定划分上，ProsMAE的冻结线性探针验证二次加权卡帕（QWK）均值高于vanilla MAE基线。

Conclusion: 多源MAE预训练可提升组织病理学表示学习的泛化能力，但需进一步通过重复划分实验来验证其在不同划分组成下的鲁棒性。

Abstract: Whole slide images (WSIs) provide rich diagnostic information for computational pathology, but their gigapixel scale, stain variation, scanner differences, tissue artifacts, and limited expert annotation make robust model training challenging. This paper presents a multi-source Masked Autoencoder (MAE) framework, named ProsMAE, for histopathology representation learning. Tiles from Prostate cANcer graDe Assessment (PANDA), CAncer MEtastases in LYmph nOdes challeNge 2017 (CAMELYON17), and BReAst Carcinoma Subtyping (BRACS) are used for ProsMAE pretraining to expose the encoder to diverse tissue morphology and acquisition conditions. The learned encoder is transferred for International Society of Urological Pathology (ISUP) grade classification through ProsCLS, using a frozen encoder and a linear classification head. ProsMAE achieved a higher mean validation quadratic weighted kappa (QWK) than the vanilla MAE frozen linear-probe baseline under the evaluated disjoint PANDA split. Repeated-split evaluation remains necessary to further establish robustness across split compositions.

</details>


### [73] [Continual Test-Time Adaptation in Computer Vision: Methods, Benchmarks, and Future Directions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08164)
*Sarthak Kumar Maharana,Shambhavi Mishra,Yunbei Zhang,Shuaicheng Niu,Taki Hasan Rafi,Jihun Hamm,Marco Pedersoli,Jose Dolz,Yunhui Guo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文全面综述了持续测试时自适应（CTTA）方法，该技术旨在解决深度神经网络在真实部署中面对持续分布漂移时的适应问题，系统分类了优化策略、参数高效方法和架构方法三大类技术，并提供了基准评测和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在训练与测试数据同分布时表现优异，但真实部署中数据分布持续变化导致性能下降。CTTA旨在不依赖源数据或标签的情况下，让预训练模型在线适应非平稳的目标分布，同时克服灾难性遗忘和伪标签误差累积这两个关键问题。

Method: 本文首先形式化定义CTTA问题，分析多种持续域漂移模式及其评估协议；然后提出层次化分类法，将现有方法分为三类：(1) 基于优化的策略（熵最小化、伪标签、参数恢复）；(2) 参数高效方法（归一化层适应、自适应参数选择）；(3) 基于架构的方法（师生框架、适配器、视觉提示、掩码建模）。最后对各方法进行系统综述和对比基准实验。

Result: 提供了CTTA方法的完整分类体系，包含各类代表性方法的系统综述，以及在标准评估设置下的比较基准和实验结果。

Conclusion: 讨论了当前方法的局限性，指出了未来研究方向，包括基础模型和黑盒系统的自适应，为CTTA领域的研究提供了路线图。相关资源已开源在GitHub仓库。

Abstract: Deep neural nets achieve remarkable performance when training and test data share the same distribution, but this assumption frequently breaks in real-world deployment, where data undergoes continual distributional shifts. Continual Test-Time Adaptation (CTTA) addresses this challenge by adapting pretrained models to non-stationary target distributions on-the-fly, without access to source data or labeled targets, while mitigating two critical failure modes: catastrophic forgetting of source knowledge and error accumulation from noisy pseudo-labels over extended time horizons. In this comprehensive survey, we formally define the CTTA problem, analyze the diverse continual domain shift patterns that characterize different evaluation protocols, and propose a hierarchical taxonomy that categorizes existing methods into three families: optimization-based strategies (entropy minimization, pseudo-labeling, parameter restoration), parameter-efficient methods (normalization layer adaptation, adaptive parameter selection), and architecture-based approaches (teacher-student frameworks, adapters, visual prompting, masked modeling). We systematically review representative methods within each category and present comparative benchmarks and experimental results across standard evaluation settings. Finally, we discuss limitations of current approaches and highlight emerging research directions, including adaptation of foundation models and black-box systems, providing a roadmap for future research in robust continual test-time adaptation. We encourage visiting our repository at [https://github.com/sarthaxxxxx/Awesome-Continual-Test-Time-Adaptation](https://github.com/sarthaxxxxx/Awesome-Continual-Test-Time-Adaptation)

</details>


### [74] [Attention-Based Segmentation of WMHs and Differentiation of Vascular vs. Demyelinating Lesions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08171)
*Aina Tur-Serrano,Gabriel Moyà-Alcover,Francisco J. Perales López*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出结合注意力机制分割（BAM、CBAM、Attention U-Net）与形态特征分类的方法，用于区分血管性和脱髓鞘性白质高信号病变，在五个公开数据集上验证了该方法的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
