However, existing methods either lack comprehensive world cognition or suffer from fragmented world foresight, inherently confining these models to reactive driving. To address this limitation, we propose WCog-VLA, a novel dual-level World-Cognitive VLA framework that successfully bridges semantic world forecasting with generative world evolution to achieve proactive autonomous driving. At the semantic level, WCog-VLA unifies world cognition and reasoning by incorporating 3D spatial perception and injecting agent tokens to capture the world dynamics, while concurrently enabling Game-theoretic Chain-of-Thought (Game-CoT) reasoning. At the generative level, we introduce the Aligned Decoupled Diffusion Transformer (ADDT) as a powerful generative world model that synthesizes physically-plausible joint multi-agent trajectories. Through scene representation alignment, ADDT reduces the number of denoising steps required and thus significantly accelerates inference. To facilitate strategic reasoning, we further construct a large-scale dataset featuring 85k Game-CoT annotations. Extensive experiments on the NAVSIM benchmark demonstrate that WCog-VLA achieves a State-Of-The-Art (SOTA) PDMS score of 92.9.

</details>


### [112] [Swapping Faces, Saving Features: A Dual-Purpose Pipeline for Pedestrian Privacy in ITS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08402)
*Roba H. Farouk,Catherine M. Elias*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个五阶段流水线，通过面部替换（face swapping）技术来保护自动驾驶数据集中行人的隐私，同时保留关键面部属性以维持数据可用性。在Egy-DRiVeS数据集上评估了Roop和Ghost-v2两种模型，结果表明Roop在隐私保护与数据可用性之间取得了更好的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆需要大规模多样化数据集来训练行人意图和轨迹预测模型，但这些数据集中的行人图像存在身份盗窃和跟踪等严重安全风险。现有隐私保护方法虽能保护隐私，但会降低图像可用性，影响模型训练效果。因此需要一种既能保护行人隐私又不损害数据可用性的方法。

Method: 实现了一个五阶段流水线，采用面部替换技术保护行人隐私。具体评估了Roop和Ghost-v2两种面部替换模型在Egy-DRiVeS数据集上的表现，通过身份隐藏和面部属性保留两个维度衡量隐私保护与数据可用性的平衡。

Result: Roop面部替换模型在多个方面优于Ghost-v2，能够更好地在行人隐私保护（身份隐藏）和数据可用性（面部属性保留）之间取得平衡。

Conclusion: 采用Roop面部替换模型的五阶段流水线可有效保护自动驾驶数据集中行人的隐私，同时保留必要面部属性以维持模型训练效果，为智能交通系统中的隐私保护问题提供了一种可行的解决方案。

Abstract: Large-scale and diverse datasets are needed to train AI models to take real-time decisions for autonomous vehicles (AVs), an intelligent transportation system (ITS) application. Pedestrian intention and trajectory prediction are critical models used in AVs, requiring datasets involving diverse pedestrian images. Unrestricted access to these datasets imposes serious security risks, like identity theft and pedestrian tracking. The challenge is to apply privacy preservation procedures while maintaining the image attributes needed to train the models. Existing privacy methods may preserve the pedestrian's privacy, but degrade the image usability, which hinders the models' effectiveness. This work's focus is to implement a five-stage pipeline to protect pedestrians' privacy through face swapping while keeping the essential facial attributes intact. It should be tailored to satisfy the privacy needs of the Egy-DRiVeS dataset. Moreover, Roop and Ghost-v2 face-swapping models are evaluated. Provenly, Roop outperforms Ghost-v2 in various aspects, as will be discussed. Consequently, Roop is the face-swapping model to be used in the pipeline to strike the balance between pedestrian privacy via identity concealment and data usability via facial attribute preservation.

</details>


### [113] [Track2Map: Online Deformable SLAM with Motion-Aware Pose Optimization in Robotic Surgery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08408)
*Tianyi Song,Sierra Bonilla,Xinwei Ju,Evangelos Mazomenos,Danail Stoyanov,Adam Schmidt,Omid Mohareri,Sophia Bano,Francisco Vasconcelos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Track2Map是一种在线3D高斯泼溅SLAM方法，能够从手术视频中联合优化相机轨迹和3D可变形场景表示，无需准确的相机轨迹先验，在StereoMIS数据集上展示了优于现有SLAM和非SLAM方法的重建质量与轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于高斯泼溅的密集可变形3D解剖重建管线多为离线方法，且依赖准确的相机轨迹先验（如机器人运动学），在先验缺失或存在噪声时应用受限。

Method: 提出Track2Map在线管线，联合优化相机轨迹与可变形3D场景表示；引入锚定于轨迹的形变初始化（基于密集2D点跟踪）；利用轨迹统计检测静态相机周期并减少增量建图过程中的漂移，从而解耦相机运动与场景形变。

Result: 在StereoMIS数据集上，Track2Map在重建质量和相机轨迹精度上均优于对比的SLAM方法，并且优于使用相机轨迹先验的非SLAM方法。

Conclusion: Track2Map有效解决了手术视频中缺乏可靠相机先验时的3D重建问题，具备SLAM能力，为RAMIS中的在线密集3D重建提供了新方案。

Abstract: Gaussian splatting is the current state-of-the-art for dense, deformable 3D anatomy reconstruction in robot-assisted minimally invasive surgery (RAMIS); however, most pipelines are offline and depend on accurate camera trajectory priors (often from robotic kinematics), limiting applicability when priors are missing or noisy. To address these limitations, we propose Track2Map, an online 3D Gaussian Splatting pipeline that jointly optimizes camera trajectory and 3D deformable scene representation directly from surgical video. Track2Map is therefore capable of robust 3D reconstructions when camera trajectory priors are either absent or noisy, and due to its online nature it effectively works as a Simultaneous Localisation and Mapping (SLAM) method. To stabilize optimization in the presence of tissue motion and ambiguous visual cues, we introduce a track-anchored deformation initialization using dense 2D point tracks. Track statistics are further utilized to disentangle camera motion from scene deformation by detecting static camera periods and reducing drift during incremental mapping. Experiments on StereoMIS show improved reconstruction quality and camera trajectory against competing SLAM methods, as well as compared to non-SLAM methods that utilize camera trajectory priors. The code is available at https://track2map.github.io/.

</details>


### [114] [Benchmark Evaluation of Feredated Learning on Multi-organ Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08219)
*Junbin Mao,Xu Tian,Jianchun Zhu,Ludi Li,Jin Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了MobenFL基准，整合了20种联邦学习算法和22个医学影像数据集，覆盖12个关键器官，从性能、效率、隐私保护等多维度评估FL在真实临床环境中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦医学影像基准缺乏对最新算法的集成、局限于单一器官或模态、过度关注模型准确率，难以全面评估FL在真实医疗环境中的效果，亟需统一评价标准以推动其临床落地。

Method: 构建MobenFL基准，集成20种前沿FL算法与22个医学影像数据集（覆盖12个人体关键器官），在传统性能评估基础上，系统纳入算法效率和隐私保护能力等关键指标，并针对不同疾病、设备和成像模态的真实临床场景进行专项评估。

Result: MobenFL在覆盖广度（算法数量、器官范围、数据集规模）和评估深度（多维度指标、真实场景专项评估）上均超越了现有基准。

Conclusion: MobenFL为联邦学习在医学领域的临床应用提供了全面深入的评估框架，有望推动医疗AI向可靠临床实践迈进。

Abstract: The privacy requirements of medical data and its substantial variations across organs and modalities hinder the clinical implementation of medical AI. Federated learning (FL) is a feasible approach to overcome these challenges. Due to the continuous emergence of FL algorithms and the highly heterogeneous nature of medical data, objectively evaluating their performance in real-world clinical settings remains difficult. Therefore, a comprehensive federated medical imaging benchmark, serving as a unified evaluation standard, is crucial for advancing the technology toward reliable clinical application. Existing federated medical imaging benchmarks have not yet adequately incorporated state-of-the-art algorithms, are limited to data from single organs or modalities, and overly emphasize model accuracy, making it difficult to comprehensively assess the overall efficacy of FL in real-world medical environments. To address these challenges, we developed the MobenFL benchmark. This benchmark integrates 20 cutting-edge FL algorithms and 22 medical imaging datasets, covering 12 critical organs across the human body, surpassing existing benchmark in breadth. In terms of evaluation dimensions, MobenFL not only assesses performance but also systematically incorporates key metrics such as algorithmic efficiency and privacy protection capabilities. Additionally, it conducts specialized evaluations for complex real-world clinical scenarios involving different diseases, devices, and imaging modalities, thereby providing a comprehensive and in-depth evaluation framework for the clinical application of FL in the medical field.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [115] [Controllability-Aware Adversarial Examples Against LLM-Based Network Traffic Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07739)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架，用于评估LLM作为网络入侵检测分类器的对抗鲁棒性。通过将流特征分为直接可控、间接可控和不可控三类，在物理约束下生成对抗样本并迁移攻击LLM和传统ML模型，发现LLM的迁移脆弱性显著但具有数据集依赖性，梯度/分数攻击比贪心攻击更有效，且约束违规率为0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM被越来越多地用作网络入侵检测分类器，但其在现实攻击者约束下的对抗鲁棒性尚不清楚，现有研究缺乏对网络通信语义可控制性的考虑。

Method: 提出可控性感知的黑盒迁移攻击框架：根据网络通信语义将流特征分为直接可控(DC)、间接可控(IC)和不可控(UC)三类，仅扰动DC特征；使用共享XGBoost替代模型生成有限差分PGD、贪心坐标和NES对抗样本，迁移攻击7个LLM和2个传统ML目标，在5个IDS基准（1999-2022）上评估。

Result: 在27个有效LLM配置和50万+对抗样本上，LLM迁移脆弱性显著但依赖数据集和比较基准。LLM在RT-IoT2022和CIC-IDS-2018上比LightGBM更脆弱，在NSL-KDD和UNSW-NB15上相当，在HIKARI-2021上更鲁棒；与平均ML基线相比，LLM在所有5个数据集上ASR更高。梯度/分数攻击比贪心攻击迁移效果更好，跨替代模型验证结果一致，约束违规率0%。

Conclusion: LLM作为网络入侵检测分类器存在显著的对抗迁移脆弱性，但脆弱程度因数据集而异；所提出的可控性感知框架能有效生成物理可行的对抗样本；梯度/分数攻击是更有效的迁移攻击方式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly explored as network intrusion detection classifiers, but their adversarial robustness under realistic attacker constraints remains unclear. We present a controllability-aware black-box transfer framework for LLM-based network traffic classifiers. The framework partitions flow features into directly controllable (DC), indirectly controllable (IC), and uncontrollable (UC) groups according to network communication semantics, then restricts perturbations to DC features while freezing IC/UC features. Using a shared XGBoost surrogate, we generate finite-difference PGD, greedy coordinate-wise, and NES adversarial examples and transfer them to seven LLM targets and two conventional ML targets across five IDS benchmarks from 1999 to 2022. Across 27 valid LLM configurations and over 500,000 adversarial examples, we find that LLM transfer vulnerability is substantial but dataset- and comparator-dependent. Compared with LightGBM, LLMs are more vulnerable on RT-IoT2022 and CIC-IDS-2018, comparable on NSL-KDD and UNSW-NB15, and less vulnerable on HIKARI-2021; compared with the averaged ML baseline, LLMs show higher ASR on all five datasets. We further observe a consistent cross-architecture transfer hierarchy: gradient- and score-based perturbations transfer more effectively than greedy perturbations across all 27 LLM cells and 9/10 ML cells. Cross-surrogate validation with tree, neural, and linear surrogates yields similar LLM ASR, reducing evidence that the findings are XGBoost-specific. Constraint violation rate is 0\% by construction.

</details>


### [116] [Forensic Schema for Psychological Manipulation in Cyber Fraud: LLM-Driven Victim Reports Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07751)
*Zikai Alex Wen,Corrazon Ogot,Juan Li,Yan Bai*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个包含11种心理操纵指标和加密货币证据字段的网络犯罪取证分类模式，基于10,994份受害者报告通过LLM标注验证，发现每种诈骗类型具有独特的操纵特征，但存在取证细节缺口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络犯罪分类模式仅捕获联系元数据和金融交易，忽略了犯罪者使用的心理操纵技术，需要更全面的取证框架。

Method: 构建四类别35问题的取证模式，新增11项操纵指标和加密货币证据字段；利用LLM对10,994份受害者报告进行标注，并与两名人工标注者进行一致性验证。

Result: LLM-人工一致性κ=0.69，与人工间一致性κ=0.68相当；不同诈骗类型具有统计显著的独特操纵特征（Cramer's V最高达0.790）；但发现取证细节缺口——操纵技术检测可靠，受害者叙述中的可操作细节差异大，区块链标识符几乎缺失。

Conclusion: 最直接的改进方案是AI辅助受害者信息采集，结合模式化的后续追问；分层标注策略可作为LLM在其他取证文本领域提取的可复用模板。

Abstract: Existing cybercrime classification schemas capture contact metadata and financial transactions but omit the psychological manipulation techniques perpetrators employ. We present a forensic schema (four categories, 35 questions) adding 11 manipulation indicators and cryptocurrency evidence fields to established forensic foundations. Applied to 10,994 victim reports via large language model (LLM)-driven annotation and validated against two human annotators (mean LLM-human $κ= 0.69$, matching inter-annotator $κ= 0.68$), the schema revealed a statistically distinct manipulation profile for each major fraud type (Cramer's $V$ up to $0.790$). A rationale-based evidence audit nonetheless exposed a forensic detail gap: detection of manipulation techniques was reliable, but victim narratives varied widely in the actionable detail supporting each Yes answer, and blockchain-specific identifiers were nearly absent. These findings point to AI-assisted victim intake with schema-informed follow-up questions as the most direct way to close the gap. The tiered annotation strategy also provides a reusable template for LLM-based extraction from other forensic text domains.

</details>


### [117] [ScopeJudge: Cost-Aware Pre-Execution Gating for Offensive Security Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07774)
*Shane Caldwell,Max Harley,Ads Dawson,Michael Kouremetis,Vincent Abruzzo,Will Pearce*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ScopeJudge基准，包含4,897个工具调用（7.7%越界违规），由专业渗透测试员标注。研究发现静态安全策略不足以推断用户请求中的范围边界，必须采用基于请求条件的分界监控。通过评估八种法官模型和五种对话策略，推荐了成本敏感和召回优先两种运行方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理在 offensive security 任务中，一个超出范围的工具调用可能违反用户约定的边界、破坏生产环境或使漏洞赏金失效。范围边界取决于用户请求的意图和上下文，而非固定策略所能枚举，因此需要一种能推断意图的实时拦截机制。

Method: 提出pre-execution gating方法：一个轻量级可信的LLM法官在每个工具调用执行前进行审查并决定接受或拒绝。构建ScopeJudge数据集，包含来自代理轨迹的4,897个工具调用，由专业渗透测试员标注，评估8个法官模型在5种对话策略下的表现。

Result: 静态策略下法官召回率接近于零，证明范围存在于用户请求中，必须进行基于请求条件的监控。报告了精确率、召回率和F1分数（专家一致性参考F1=0.78），并推荐了成本敏感和召回优先两个运行点。

Conclusion: 静态安全策略在范围边界执行方面存在结构性不足，请求条件监控是必要的。ScopeJudge数据集可支持实时监控和可扩展监督自主安全代理。

Abstract: As LLM agents take on offensive security work, a single out-of-scope tool call can breach a client's engagement boundary, disrupt production, or void a bug-bounty finding. Unlike a fixed safety policy, the boundary that matters is declared in the user's request and must be inferred from intent. That challenge is sharpened by the adversarial nature of offensive security: the same tool call is in or out of scope depending not on the action itself but on the target it touches and the context in which it runs, which no fixed policy can enumerate in advance. We study pre-execution gating: a cheap, trusted LLM judge inspects each call proposed by a strong, swappable agent, and accepts or rejects it before it runs. We introduce ScopeJudge, a benchmark of 4,897 tool calls (7.7% scope violations) from agent trajectories on tasks engineered to tempt agents out of scope and labeled at the call level by professional penetration testers, with substantial inter-grader agreement (Fleiss kappa = 0.64) that sets an expert agreement reference point of F1 = 0.78. We evaluate eight judge models under five transcript strategies, varying how much context the judge sees, from the static policy alone to the full raw transcript, and chart the resulting cost-accuracy Pareto frontier. We find that a static policy is structurally insufficient for scope enforcement: blind to the user's request, judge recall collapses to near zero, confirming that scope lives in the request and that request-conditioned monitoring is necessary. Because a missed violation costs more than a spurious rejection, we report precision, recall, and F1 separately and recommend two operating points: a cost-sensitive configuration and a recall-first one for high-stakes deployments. We release the ScopeJudge dataset to support real-time monitoring and scalable oversight of autonomous security agents.

</details>


### [118] [Open Models, Open Risks: Measuring Unsafe Generation in Text-to-Image Models In the Wild](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07827)
*Peilin Han,Yang Liu,Yilong Yang,Jingchun Zhang,Teng Li,Jianfeng Ma,Zhuo Ma*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对现实世界中文本到图像（T2I）模型的越狱安全进行了大规模实证研究。研究发现，仅依赖检测器的越狱评估指标会高估实际风险，并提出了改进指标Advanced ASR。通过对Hugging Face上200多个T2I模型的评估，发现许多下游模型即使没有显式安全防护仍保持一定安全水平，但同时也识别出一批高风险模型（包括明确NSFW导向和表面良性的模型），并对这些模型进行了追溯和向Hugging Face报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有T2I越狱安全研究大多在受控实验室环境下进行，仅关注少数典型模型，对快速发展的现实T2I生态系统的安全状况缺乏了解。同时，检测器指标存在局限，现实风险不仅来自对抗性提示，还来自不安全的发布实践和模型衍生。

Method: 首先指出现有检测器指标因语义漂移和生成伪影导致高估实际风险，提出Advanced ASR指标。然后对Hugging Face上200多个T2I模型在三种代表性越狱攻击下进行系统评估。

Result: 许多下游模型即使没有显式安全防护仍保持非平凡的安全水平，安全退化并非普遍一致。同时识别出高风险模型，包括明确NSFW导向的模型和仅在系统评估下才暴露不安全行为的表面良性模型。

Conclusion: 现实T2I生态的安全状况比预期复杂：既有令人欣慰的发现（许多模型保持安全），也存在隐患（高风险模型存在且来源多样）。研究还向Hugging Face报告了高风险案例。

Abstract: Existing safety studies on text-to-image (T2I) jailbreaks are largely conducted in controlled in-the-lab settings, typically on a small number of canonical models. As a result, the current safety status of the rapidly growing in-the-wild T2I ecosystem remains unclear. This uncertainty is amplified by two factors: existing detector-based metrics are designed for controlled evaluation, and in-the-wild risks may arise not only from adversarial prompting, but also from unsafe release practices and unsafe model derivatives.
  In this paper, we present a large-scale empirical study of in-the-wild T2I safety through the lens of jailbreak. We first show that detector-only jailbreak metrics substantially overestimate practical risk over in the wild due to semantic drift and generation artifacts, and we introduce Advanced ASR to better capture semantically valid and visually plausible unsafe generation. Using this refined metric, we evaluate 200+ in-the-wild T2I models from Hugging Face under three representative jailbreak attacks. Our results show that many downstream models retain a non-trivial degree of safety even without explicit post-hoc safeguards, indicating that safety degradation in the wild is neither universal nor uniform. At the same time, we identify a set of high-risk models, including explicitly NSFW-oriented releases as well as seemingly benign models whose unsafe behavior is only exposed through systematic evaluation. We further trace these models to their release context and report high-risk cases to Hugging Face.

</details>


### [119] [Closed-Loop Dynamic Validator Node Scaling in Private Substrate Blockchains Using Takagi-Sugeno Fuzzy Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07901)
*Thandile Nododile,Ayinde M. Usman,Clement N. Nyirenda*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出基于Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理的自适应验证节点管理系统，通过实时读取区块链参数（出块时间、区块大小、活跃节点数），输出效率评分和扩容建议（扩容/维持/缩容），在10节点Substrate网络上验证了其收敛性和稳定性优于阈值基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有区块链网络采用固定节点配置，无法适应动态工作负载：节点过多浪费资源，节点过少导致出块延迟和最终确认降级，且最优验证节点数量难以确定。

Method: 构建Takagi-Sugeno模糊推理系统，使用三角形隶属函数覆盖三个语言变量（出块时间、区块大小、活跃节点数），基于27条规则库和乘积t-范数聚合，并引入基于实测运行范围的隶属函数经验校准方法。

Result: 在10节点Substrate区块链（存储昆士兰州政府智能水表数据哈希）上，统计分析4/7/10节点配置产生不同操作特征；闭环实验自主双向调整验证节点参与并收敛至稳定均衡；与三种阈值基线相比，出块时间相当但缩放振荡更少。

Conclusion: TS模糊推理可支持私有区块链的自主验证节点管理，其稳定缩放行为是阈值方法无法比拟的。

Abstract: Private blockchain networks run with fixed node configurations that cannot adapt to changing workload conditions. Too many nodes serving a light workload waste resources; too few nodes facing heavy demand slow block production and degrade finalisation. The right validator count is hard to determine, as it depends on overlapping factors that shift over time. This paper presents a Takagi-Sugeno (TS) fuzzy inference system that reads live blockchain parameters (block production time, block size, and active node count) and outputs a continuous efficiency score alongside a scaling recommendation: Scale Up, Maintain, or Scale Down. The controller uses triangular membership functions across three linguistic variables, evaluated through a complete 27-rule base with product t-norm aggregation. A key contribution is an empirical recalibration of the membership functions, anchoring linguistic terms to the observed operating range of the testbed rather than to theoretical extremes. The system is evaluated on a 10-node Substrate blockchain network storing real smart water meter data hashes from the Queensland Government open data portal. Statistical analysis across configurations of 4, 7, and 10 active nodes confirms that the controller produces distinct operational profiles reflecting each configuration's provisioning state. In closed-loop experiments, the controller autonomously adjusts validator participation in both directions, activating validators under rising load and removing them under over-provisioning, converging to the same stable equilibrium from both directions. Compared against three threshold-based baselines, it shows fewer scaling oscillations while maintaining comparable block production times. Results show that TS fuzzy inference can support autonomous validator management in private blockchain deployments, with stable scaling behaviour threshold approaches cannot match.

</details>


### [120] [Mechanistic Interpretability of LLM Jailbreaks via Internal Attribution Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07903)
*Anupam Wagle,Ifrat Ikhtear Uddin,Chaowei Zhang,Longwei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于内部计算图的机制性框架，用于诊断大语言模型（LLM）在面对对抗性提示和越狱攻击时的脆弱性。通过构建并对比干净提示与攻击提示的配对计算图，揭示攻击如何系统性地改变模型内部推理（包括抑制安全相关组件、涌现攻击特定特征、重路由计算路径）。该框架将计算分解为不变、抑制和涌现三种结构，识别与失败模式相关的脆弱性基序，并通过因果干预直接评估其对攻击成功的贡献。实验表明，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为高度相关，针对脆弱性基序的干预可提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法主要通过输入-输出行为或归因方法分析LLM的脆弱性，对对抗性扰动如何改变模型内部推理机制的了解有限，导致不安全或错误行为背后的机理难以理解。

Method: 提出基于配对内部计算图的机制性诊断框架：(i) 为干净和攻击提示分别构建结构化因果交互的计算图；(ii) 将计算分解为不变、抑制和涌现结构；(iii) 识别与失败模式相关的脆弱性基序；(iv) 对节点、路径和子图进行因果干预以评估其对攻击成功的贡献。

Result: 在多个开源LLM和多种对抗性/越狱基准测试上，内部计算图的结构偏差与不安全行为高度相关；针对识别出的脆弱性基序进行干预可有效提升模型鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 内部计算图为理解、诊断和缓解LLM脆弱性提供了原则性基础，实现了从描述性归因到因果诊断的转变。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibit remarkable capabilities but remain highly vulnerable to adversarial prompts and jailbreak attacks. Existing approaches primarily analyze these failures through input-output behaviors or attribution methods, offering limited insight into how adversarial perturbations alter the model's internal reasoning. Consequently, the mechanisms underlying unsafe or incorrect behaviors remain poorly understood. We introduce a mechanistic framework for diagnosing LLM vulnerabilities using paired internal computation graphs, which represent prompt-specific inference as structured causal interactions among latent features. By constructing and aligning computation graphs for clean and attacked prompts, we reveal that adversarial attacks induce systematic transformations of internal reasoning, including suppression of safety-relevant components, emergence of attack-specific features, and rerouting of computation paths. Building on this representation, we propose a unified framework that (i) decomposes computation into invariant, suppressed, and emergent structures, (ii) identifies recurring vulnerability motifs associated with failure modes, and (iii) performs causal interventions on nodes, paths, and subgraphs to directly evaluate their contributions to attack success. This enables a transition from descriptive attribution to causal diagnosis of model failures. Experiments across multiple open-source LLMs and diverse adversarial and jailbreak benchmarks demonstrate that structural deviations in internal computation graphs strongly correlate with unsafe behaviors. Furthermore, targeted interventions on identified vulnerability motifs improve model robustness, establishing internal computation graphs as a principled foundation for understanding, diagnosing, and mitigating LLM vulnerabilities.

</details>


### [121] [KS-CFA: Control-Flow Attestation via Symbolic Replay Against Control-Flow Bending Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07926)
*Zhanyu Sha,Konstantinos Markantonakis,Carlton Shepherd,Amir Rafi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: KS-CFA是一种基于符号执行和输入源控制流依赖变量识别的控制流证明方案，能够检测四种跳转类型的控制流弯曲攻击，无需路径或值枚举，在RISC-V Keystone TEE上实现，Prover端开销为6.8-32.2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CFA方案存在测量空间大、仅覆盖部分攻击类型或依赖底层硬件修改等缺陷，难以有效检测控制流弯曲攻击。

Method: 结合符号执行与选择性识别输入源控制流依赖变量，在TEE内记录控制流轨迹及影响运行时变量的外部输入，验证者通过单路径符号执行重放路径预测每个跳转，定位信号攻击的偏差。

Result: Prover端开销在QEMU上为6.8-20.5倍，在FPGA上为6.7-32.2倍，验证无需路径或值枚举。

Conclusion: KS-CFA能有效检测间接调用、条件跳转、间接跳转和返回四种传输类型的CFB攻击，且无需枚举大量测量空间或硬件修改。

Abstract: Control-flow attestation (CFA) enables a remote entity to verify program execution on a target device by monitoring control-flow behaviour at runtime. However, control-flow bending (CFB) attacks remain difficult to detect, where an adversary steers execution along legal edges of the program's control-flow graph by corrupting branch flags, loop counters, and other runtime data. Existing solutions impose significant drawbacks: they require enumerating vast measurement spaces, cover only a reduced subset of attacks, or rely on low-level hardware modifications. In this work, we present KS-CFA, a new CFA scheme that detects CFB attacks across four transfer types (indirect calls, conditional and indirect jumps, and returns) without those costs. To this end, we combine symbolic execution and selective identification of input-sourced control-flow dependent variables: a strict subset of control-flow-relevant state whose values are directly read from external input. The proving device records, inside a trusted execution environment (TEE), a control-flow trace and the external inputs that determine relevant run-time variables. The verifier then replays the reported path through single-path symbolic execution, predicting each transfer and localising divergences that signal an attack. We implement and evaluate KS-CFA using the RISC-V Keystone TEE and Embench-IoT on QEMU and a Rocket-based FPGA platform (NiteFury II). Prover-side overhead relative to unattested execution ranges from 6.8-20.5x on QEMU and 6.7-32.2x on the FPGA, and verification requires no path or value enumeration.

</details>


### [122] [Who Broke the System? Failure Localization in LLM-Based Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07989)
*Yufei Xia,Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AgentLocate是一个针对基于大语言模型的多智能体系统的故障定位框架，能够将故障归因到特定智能体和最早关键决策步骤。它结合了LLM判断机制、多视角验证和置信度感知聚合策略，并通过轻量级微调优化判断器。实验表明其在责任智能体识别和故障步骤定位上优于现有方法，且高效实用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的多智能体系统在分布式协作中面临系统级故障诊断困难：由于长时间交互和智能体行为紧密耦合，当执行失败时，难以确定哪个智能体负有责任以及轨迹在何时首次出现不可逆转的错误方向。

Method: AgentLocate框架结合三种核心机制：(1) 基于LLM的判断机制；(2) 独立评估者的多视角验证；(3) 置信度感知的聚合策略。此外，通过轻量级微调对判断器进行自适应优化，提升归因质量。

Result: 在覆盖多样化任务、智能体配置和轨迹长度的两个互补基准上，AgentLocate在识别责任智能体和故障步骤方面持续优于现有的故障定位方法，同时在token使用和运行时间方面保持高效。

Conclusion: AgentLocate有效解决了多智能体系统中的故障定位难题，为分布式智能体系统的可靠诊断提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) based multi-agent systems enable complex problem solving through coordinated reasoning and action, but their distributed structure also introduces new challenges in diagnosing system-level failures. When an execution fails, identifying which agent is responsible and at what point the trajectory first becomes irreversibly misdirected is difficult due to long-horizon interactions and tightly coupled agent behaviors. In this paper, we study the problem of failure localization in LLM-based multi-agent systems and present AgentLocate, a framework that attributes failures to both a specific agent and the earliest decisive step. AgentLocate combines an LLM-based judging mechanism with multi-perspective verification by independent evaluators, whose assessments are aggregated using a confidence-aware strategy. The resulting feedback is further used to adapt the judge through lightweight fine-tuning, improving attribution quality. We evaluate AgentLocate on two complementary benchmarks covering diverse tasks, agent configurations, and trajectory lengths. Experimental results show that AgentLocate consistently outperforms existing failure localization methods in identifying both responsible agents and failure steps, while remaining efficient in terms of token usage and running time.

</details>


### [123] [Beware What You Autocomplete: Forensic Attribution of Backdoored Code Completions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08011)
*Anjun Gao,Yueyang Quan,Zhuqing Liu,Minghong Fang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CodeTracer是一个后门攻击取证框架，能从恶意代码补全追溯到其背后的微调数据，在多种攻击场景下保持高准确率和强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型代码补全模型易受后门攻击影响，现有防御技术难以应对自适应和复杂后门攻击，缺乏从恶意输出溯源到具体投毒数据的有效方法。

Method: CodeTracer在仅依赖微调语料库和报告的错误补全事件的情况下，首先从受损输出中提取结构化行为指纹，然后将搜索范围缩小到语义相关的代码样本，最后利用基于LLM的推理将不安全逻辑归因到特定的后门数据。

Result: 在三个代表性漏洞案例和十种后门攻击（含十六个竞争基线）上的评估表明，CodeTracer持续实现高取证准确率、低误识别率，并对自适应攻击表现出强鲁棒性。

Conclusion: CodeTracer是一种有效的后门攻击取证框架，能够在实际部署约束下可靠地追溯恶意代码补全的源头。

Abstract: Large language models have enabled powerful code completion systems that assist developers by predicting subsequent lines of code. However, these models remain vulnerable to backdoor attacks, where malicious fine-tuning data covertly implants unsafe behaviors. Despite advances in defensive techniques, adaptive and sophisticated backdoor attacks still evade detection and mitigation. We present CodeTracer, a forensic framework that traces malicious code completions back to the backdoor fine-tuning data responsible for them. Operating under realistic post-deployment constraints, CodeTracer relies solely on the fine-tuning corpus and the reported miscompletion event. It extracts a structured behavioral fingerprint from the compromised output, narrows the search to semantically relevant code samples, and employs LLM-based reasoning to attribute unsafe logic to specific backdoor data. Extensive evaluations across three representative vulnerability cases and ten backdoor attacks, along with sixteen competitive baselines, demonstrate that CodeTracer consistently achieves high forensic accuracy, low false identification rates, and strong robustness against adaptive attacks.

</details>


### [124] [Degree-Constrained Interval Optimization for Minimax Polynomial Approximation in Homomorphic Encryption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08042)
*Jiheon Woo,Donggyun Ryu,Yongjune Kim*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分布感知的区间优化方法，通过结合极小极大多项式与域扩展函数/多项式来平衡同态加密下激活函数近似的区间宽度与精度之间的权衡，并推导了可解析处理的代理目标及实现误差上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在同态加密（HE）的算术约束下，非线性激活函数必须用多项式近似。极小极大多项式（如Remez算法）的近似区间是关键超参数：区间越宽，对大幅值输入的鲁棒性越好，但在固定多项式次数下近似误差越大。现有方法缺乏对这种权衡的显式建模和优化。

Method: 1. 将区间选择形式化为分布感知的均方误差（MSE）最小化问题；2. 引入域扩展函数（DEF）及其HE可实现的对应物——域扩展多项式（DEP），用于在区间外近似截断操作以抑制多项式外推；3. 推导基于DEF的可解析处理的代理目标函数，捕获区间内极小极大误差与区间外截断误差之间的权衡；4. 通过实现误差分解及上界分析，将理想化目标与HE可实现的DEP构造联系起来。

Result: 推导了适用于DEF的代理目标函数，建立了该目标与DEP实现之间的误差上界关系。

Conclusion: 所提方法为HE下的多项式激活函数设计提供了一种理论驱动的区间优化策略，能够根据预激活分布自适应地选择近似区间，在保证安全性的同时提升近似精度。

Abstract: Homomorphic encryption (HE) enables privacy-preserving inference under arithmetic constraints that restrict encrypted evaluation to additions and multiplications. As a result, non-polynomial activation functions must be replaced by polynomial approximations. Among polynomial approximation methods, minimax approximation, typically computed by the Remez algorithm, is a standard approach because it minimizes the maximum approximation error over a given design interval. For minimax polynomial design, the approximation interval is a critical hyperparameter: a wider interval improves robustness to large-magnitude inputs while increasing the minimax approximation error under a fixed degree budget. In this paper, we formulate this trade-off as a distribution-aware interval optimization problem, where the approximation interval is chosen to minimize the mean-squared error (MSE) with respect to the pre-activation distribution of interest. To effectively control outside-interval inputs, we combine minimax polynomials with domain extension functions (DEFs) and their HE-realizable polynomial counterparts, domain extension polynomials (DEPs), which approximate a clipping operation outside the design interval and thereby suppress uncontrolled polynomial extrapolation. We first derive an analytically tractable DEF-based proxy objective that captures the trade-off between within-interval minimax approximation error and outside-interval clipping error. We then connect this idealized objective to HE-realizable DEP constructions through an implementation-error decomposition with an accompanying upper bound.

</details>


### [125] [zkComposer: Decomposing Proof Construction to Scale zkML](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08095)
*Pawan Kumar Sanjaya,Christina Giannoula,Valdy Oktavian,Mehdi Saeedi,Gabor Sines,Gururaj Saileshwar,Nandita Vijaykumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: zkComposer是一种模块化证明构造框架，通过将零知识机器学习推理证明分解为独立的子证明来实现额外维度的并行化，从而显著降低证明生成时间。在CNN和GPT-2上分别实现了最高3.25倍和6.84倍的加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有zkML系统证明生成成本过高，且其证明生成存在有限并行性——增加线程数并不能显著减少证明时间，原因是现有系统依赖单一的、整体的证明计算，为整个机器学习模型构造单个证明。

Method: zkComposer将zkML正确推理证明分解为独立的子证明，每个子证明覆盖推理计算的一个子集（如模型中的连续层子集）。相邻子证明通过边界层激活值的共享承诺进行密码学链接，无需额外的链接证明或修改底层密码学原语。

Result: 在CNN任务上，zkComposer相比zkCNN将证明时间和响应时间降低最高3.25倍；在GPT-2上，沿模型层分区时相比zkGPT降低最高4.83倍；同时在模型层和输入序列维度分区时，相比zkGPT降低最高6.84倍。

Conclusion: zkComposer通过模块化证明构造在现有证明内核并行性之外解锁了新的并行维度，显著降低了zkML的证明生成开销，同时保持了与整体证明相同的安全性保证。

Abstract: Zero-knowledge machine learning (zkML) enables a server to perform verifiable inference while keeping model parameters private from the client. However, existing zkML systems incur prohibitive proof-generation costs. We observe that proof generation exhibits limited parallelism; that is, prover time does not decrease significantly as the number of threads increases. This limitation is because existing systems rely on monolithic proof computation, constructing a single proof for the entire machine learning model. We introduce zkComposer, a modular proof-construction framework that unlocks an additional dimension of parallelism, in addition to the parallelism in existing proof kernels. zkComposer decomposes the zkML proof of correct inference into independent sub-proofs, each covering a subset of the computation for inference e.g., each independent sub-proof can cover a subset of contiguous layers in the ML model. Adjacent sub-proofs are cryptographically linked through shared commitments to the activations from the boundary layer. zkComposer provides the same guarantees as the monolithic proof without requiring additional linking proofs or changes to the underlying cryptographic primitives. We implement zkComposer and evaluate it on three CNNs and GPT-2. We show that, on CNN workloads, zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 3.25x relative to zkCNN [1]. On GPT-2, zkComposer reduces these times by up to 4.83x relative to zkGPT [2], when partitioning along the model layers. When partitioning across both model layers and input sequences in GPT-2, we show that zkComposer reduces prover time and response time by up to 6.84x relative to zkGPT [2].

</details>


### [126] [Securing Autonomous Vehicle Systems via Twin-Aware Federated Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08137)
*Zifan Zhang,Minghong Fang,Dianwei Chen,Zhuqing Liu,Prashant Khanduri,Xianfeng Yang,Anupam Das,Yuchen Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出SecAgg-p^2框架，通过数字孪生排练学习、历史聚合参数与中心梯度筛选机制，防御针对联邦强化学习系统的投毒攻击，保障自动驾驶决策安全，并提供理论收敛性保证与真实高速公路场景验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦强化学习在动态车辆环境中实现多智能体协作学习时，投毒攻击严重威胁系统安全与可靠性，尤其是在安全关键的自动驾驶领域，该漏洞尚未被充分探索。

Method: 提出SecAgg-p^2防御框架，整合数字孪生进行排练学习，利用历史聚合模型参数和选定的中心梯度，确保仅聚合良性数据，有效缓解恶意智能体的影响。

Result: 提供了存在投毒攻击时收敛性能的理论保证，并在开发的可模拟真实高速公路环境的数字孪生平台上验证了该框架在对抗条件下对自动驾驶车辆控制的有效性。

Conclusion: SecAgg-p^2能够显著增强面向安全关键自动驾驶场景的联邦强化学习系统的鲁棒性，有效抵御投毒攻击。

Abstract: Federated reinforcement learning (FRL) is crucial for enabling collaborative learning across multiple agents without sharing raw data, thereby enhancing privacy and scalability in the decision-making process within dynamic vehicular environments. However, poisoning attacks pose a significant threat to the security and reliability of FRL-based systems, particularly in safety-critical autonomous driving, where this vulnerability remains largely unexplored. These attacks can compromise the global control model by subtly injecting malicious system parameters, leading to potential hazards. To counter these challenges, we present \alg (\underline{Sec}ure \underline{A}ggregation with \underline{p}oisoning-\underline{p}revention and historical reinforcement) as a defensive framework aimed at enhancing the robustness of FRL systems designed for safety-critical driving scenarios. \alg strategically integrates digital twins for rehearsal-based learning and leverages historical aggregated model parameters along with a selected central gradient to ensure that only benign data is aggregated, effectively mitigating the influence of malicious agents. Theoretical guarantees are provided for the convergence performance of \alg in the presence of poisoning attacks. We also validate the effectiveness of \alg using developed digital twins that model realistic highway environments to evaluate the control of autonomous vehicles under adversarial conditions.

</details>


### [127] [Prismata: Confining Cross-Site Prompt Injection in Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08147)
*Corban Villa,Alp Eren Ozdarendeli,Sijun Tan,Raluca Ada Popa*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: Prismata是一种针对自主网页代理的防御机制，通过动态信任推导和结构约束实现上下文最小权限，在不依赖开发者标注的情况下，有效抵御提示注入攻击，同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主网页代理将自然语言解释为指令，这使第三方和用户生成内容能够通过提示注入劫持代理，而现有方法难以从与攻击者内容纠缠的页面结构中推导任务特定的安全策略。

Method: Prismata包含两个核心组件：1) 动态信任推导（Dynamic Trust Derivation），受经典完整性模型启发，为页面内容生成权限标签，保证标签只能降低权限且错误标注有界；2) 机械约束（Mechanical Confinement），通过编辑内容和限制代理能力来执行这些标签。该方法无需开发者标注。

Result: 在近期已发表的网页代理攻击（包括自适应变体）上，Prismata显著降低了攻击成功率，同时保持了良性任务的效用。

Conclusion: Prismata通过上下文最小权限原则有效防御网页代理的提示注入攻击，其动态信任推导和结构约束机制能够在无需开发者标注的情况下提供安全保障，适用于广泛的网站场景。

Abstract: Autonomous web agents promise to automate everyday browsing tasks, but inherit one of the web's oldest attack surfaces. Cross-Site Scripting proved that mixing trusted and untrusted content is dangerous, even on benign pages. Agents resurface this risk by interpreting natural language as instructions, allowing third-party and user-generated content to hijack the agent via prompt injection. The core challenge is that deriving a task-specific security policy requires reasoning over page structure that is entangled with the attacker's content.
  We present Prismata, a defense enforcing contextual least privilege for web agents, constraining both what the agent sees and what it can do. Prismata's dynamic trust derivation produces permission labels for page content, with structural confinement guarantees, inspired by classical integrity models, that bound any labeling errors so that labels can only decrease in privilege and mislabelings are bounded. Prismata's mechanical confinement enforces these labels by redacting content and restricting agent capabilities. Importantly, these mechanisms require no developer annotations, so Prismata supports the long tail of websites. Across recent published web agent attacks, including adaptive variants, Prismata substantially reduces attack success while preserving benign task utility.

</details>


### [128] [Out of Sight: Compression-Aware Content Protection against Agentic Crawlers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08180)
*Xuefei Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: CAPE是一个利用LLM智能体上下文压缩机制进行内容保护的框架，通过在文本中注入不可见扰动，在不影响人类可读性的前提下诱导智能体在压缩时产生信息丢失，实现对高价值文本的保护。实验表明在多种压缩设置下信息丢失率最高提升75.8%，并能迁移到LangGraph和GitHub Copilot等真实场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于LLM的智能体具备推理、总结和记忆能力，形成了传统防御无法应对的新威胁面。现有防御如访问控制易被模拟普通浏览器的智能体绕过，基于注入的防御则常降低人类可读性。亟需一种针对智能体压缩环节的、不影响人类阅读体验的内容保护方法。

Method: CAPE框架从可访问的替代压缩器中提取破坏性种子扰动，然后通过先验引导进化和偏好校准的候选优先级排序，将这些扰动适配到仅查询的目标压缩器，在低查询预算下实现有效保护。

Result: 在三种内容类型和四种压缩设置下，CAPE相比最强基线将信息丢失率最高提升75.8%，且保护后的内容在视觉上与原始内容不可区分。该方法还能迁移到LangGraph智能体工作流和GitHub Copilot等真实场景。

Conclusion: 本文揭示了上下文压缩可作为智能体时代内容保护的新防御层，CAPE证明了该方向的可行性与实用价值，为后续内容保护研究开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The rise of LLM-based agents with reasoning, summarization, and memory capabilities has created a new threat surface for online content that conventional defenses fail to address. Existing defenses like access controls can be circumvented by agents mimicking ordinary browsers, and injection-based defenses often degrade human readability. In this paper, we revisit the agent pipeline and identify context compression, which agents routinely invoke to fit context budgets, as a critical yet overlooked defense layer. We propose CAPE, a framework that protects high-value textual content by injecting invisible perturbations without changing its human-visible surface form, thereby inducing severe information loss during agent compression. CAPE extracts disruptive seed perturbations from an accessible surrogate compressor, then adapts them to query-only target compressors through prior-guided evolution and preference-calibrated candidate prioritization, achieving effective protection under a low query budget. Experiments on three content types and four compression settings show that CAPE improves information loss by up to 75.8% over the strongest baseline while keeping protected content visually indistinguishable from originals. CAPE also transfers to real-world settings, including the LangGraph agent workflow and GitHub Copilot, highlighting its generality and practical value. This paper aims to reveal context compression as a new defense layer, promoting content protection research in the agent era.

</details>


### [129] [MLQENABLER: Enabling Secure Machine Learning Queries over Encrypted Database in Cloud Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08197)
*Xu Zhou,Haoyang Chen,Xinyu Lei*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出MLQENABLER方案，通过索引辅助方法在加密云数据库上实现安全机器学习查询，在保证安全性的同时仅造成轻微性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共云服务商在提供云存储的同时利用客户数据提供ML服务，但云服务商不可信，可能泄露客户敏感数据；直接加密数据集会使数据库变为伪随机数而无法进行ML查询。

Method: MLQENABLER方案采用索引辅助方法（index-aid approach），在保持数据库加密状态的同时支持ML查询能力。

Result: 初始实验表明MLQENABLER达到了可接受的安全级别，仅引入轻微的ML性能下降。

Conclusion: MLQENABLER方案能够在加密数据库上同时实现安全性保障与机器学习查询能力，是一种可行的平衡方案。

Abstract: In cloud computing, the public cloud service providers (CSPs) can provide cloud storage as the primary service while providing additional machine learning (ML)-based services by using the clients' data in storage. This business model extends the border of cloud computing services and brings in new business growth possibilities. Although it is promising, the model also brings in security concerns since the public commercial cloud cannot be fully trusted. For example, the public commercial clouds may sell clients' sensitive data to the government or other companies. To address the security concerns, an immediate solution is to require clients to encrypt their datasets before outsourcing to the cloud. However, if a database is formally encrypted, then the database contains only pseudorandom numbers, making it impossible to enable ML over it. In this project, we propose MLQENABLER (ML Queries Enabler) scheme to enable secure ML queries over encrypted database in cloud storage. MLQENABLER employs an index-aid approach to achieve security and ML capability simultaneously. Our initial experiments show that MLQENABLER achieves an acceptable security level while incurring only a slight ML performance degradation.

</details>


### [130] [Threshold Authorization Without Threshold Signatures: Signature-Agnostic MPC Custody](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08226)
*Dariia Porechna*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一种双门架构，将成员认证与阈值授权分离，使数字资产托管系统能够灵活迁移至后量子签名方案，而无需重新设计协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后量子密码学转型破坏了现有基于阈值签名(TSS)的资产托管模型：哈希签名难以高效支持阈值签名，格基阈值协议尚不成熟。需要一种能平滑过渡到后量子时代的架构。

Method: 双门架构：成员用任意EUF-CMA签名方案进行个人签名认证；阈值授权由基于Shamir秘密共享的门限印章(seal)独立产生。签名方案作为部署参数可替换，迁移仅需密钥轮换。采用信息论安全的阈值方案，保证低于门限时无法伪造印章。

Result: 实现了签名方案与阈值机制的完全解耦，支持从ECDSA到SLH-DSA或ML-DSA的无缝迁移；低于门限的攻击者即使持有签名密钥也无法产生有效印章；架构可部署于智能合约、保险库模块或HSM等环境。

Conclusion: 该架构为后量子时代的数字资产托管提供了实用的过渡路径，将签名方案升级简化为密钥轮换操作，避免了协议层面的重构，同时保持了可编程授权计算的能力。

Abstract: Digital-asset custody has been built on threshold multi-party approval: no operation proceeds unless $t$ of $n$ parties approve, and fewer than t compromised parties can neither authorize nor learn the authorization secret. Threshold signature schemes (TSS) have been the standard mechanism, but the post-quantum transition disrupts this model: standardized hash-based signatures resist efficient threshold signing, and lattice-based threshold protocols remain an emerging research track.
  We present a dual-gate architecture that separates member authentication from threshold authorization. Each member signs its approval with an ordinary signature under any EUF-CMA scheme; the quorum jointly produces a threshold seal from Shamir-shared secrets bound to the operation. The seal is the base instance of a programmable authorization computation: simple quorum is the minimal policy, while richer policies can evaluate secret-shared state without making the member-signature scheme part of that computation. The signature scheme is a deployment parameter: migrating from ECDSA to SLH-DSA or ML-DSA is a key rotation, not a protocol redesign, and members holding keys in commodity HSMs participate through the standard sign API. The architecture can be deployed wherever the asset-control path supports programmable verification, such as smart contracts, vault modules, or HSMs guarding a master key, and produces an enforcement-layer authorization rather than a native chain signature. Below-threshold secrecy is information-theoretic; an adversary holding $\geq t$ signing keys but no coefficient shares still cannot produce the seal.

</details>


### [131] [Simulating the Resident: Generating Executable Smart Home Schedules via LLM Personas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08231)
*Victor Jüttner,Xenia Wagner,Christoph Jahn,Erik Buchmann*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出利用LLM生成多样化居民角色，在模拟智能家居中产生基于行为的设备交互计划，以支持可扩展、保护隐私的智能家居实验，无需依赖侵入式的真实数据采集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能家居HCI研究需要真实家庭数据集，但采集此类数据耗时、昂贵且存在严重隐私问题，因此需要一种不依赖侵入式真实数据收集的替代方案。

Method: (1) 设计框架：跨五个社会技术维度配置模拟家庭；(2) 多阶段LLM流水线：生成结构化、可执行的设备交互计划；(3) 概念验证：证明可行性。

Result: 提出了包含设计框架、多阶段LLM流水线和概念验证的整体方案，初步证明了可行性。

Conclusion: 该方法有望为智能家居实验提供可扩展且隐私友好的数据生成途径，减少对真实家庭数据采集的依赖。

Abstract: Smart homes have emerged as an important domain for HCI research, including work on usable security and privacy. Ideally, studies in these areas draw on datasets collected in real homes with real residents, capturing authentic device interactions, network traffic, and daily routines. However, creating such datasets is slow, expensive, and raises significant privacy concerns, as it requires long-term observation of people in their most private spaces. We propose using LLMs to generate diverse resident personas that interact with a simulated smart home, producing behaviorally grounded interaction schedules that can be executed on physical testbeds. We present (1) a design framework configuring simulated households across five socio-technical dimensions, (2) a multi-stage LLM pipeline that produces structured, executable device interaction schedules, and (3) a proof of concept demonstrating feasibility. As a work in progress, we aim to support scalable, privacy-conscious smart-home experimentation without relying on intrusive real-world data collection.

</details>


### [132] [Mini-Programs, Mega-Problems: Unveiling OAuth-based Authentication Misuses in Mini-Programs via Dynamic Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08232)
*Zidong Zhang,Zhentao Xie,Lingyun Ying,Qinsheng Hou,Yacong Gu,Wenrui Diao,Jianliang Wu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统性地发现了小程序生态中OAuth认证集成的新型运行时误用问题，开发了MINIAUTH分析框架，在4.4万+微信和百度小程序中发现了1834个误用案例，包括客户端身份伪造、认证绕过等严重漏洞，并发现百度OBA API存在密码学设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三方开发者在超级应用中集成OAuth登录时存在安全隐患，现有基于静态代码的研究无法检测运行时行为导致的OBA误用问题，亟需大规模系统性分析框架来评估实际影响。

Method: 设计并实现了MINIAUTH分析框架，能够自动定位小程序OBA登录页面、动态执行工作流并分析运行时行为，支持处理混淆代码，覆盖微信和百度两大平台。

Result: 在44,273个微信小程序和2,721个百度小程序中发现1,834个误用案例，包括客户端身份伪造（凭据泄露）、认证绕过（静态/明文标识符）等关键逻辑缺陷，并发现百度OBA API存在会话密钥可暴力破解的密码学设计缺陷。

Conclusion: OBA误用问题在小程序生态中普遍存在且跨平台一致，亟需加强开发者指导和完善平台级安全防护措施；已向相关平台和开发者负责任披露并获得认可。

Abstract: Mini-programs have become a dominant paradigm for lightweight application deployment within super apps such as WeChat. To support seamless integration, super apps provide OAuth mechanisms for user login. However, improper integration of OAuth-based Authentication (OBA) flows by third-party developers can lead to critical security flaws. In this paper, we discover three new types of runtime OBA misuses that differ from prior static-code-based studies, enabling attackers to impersonate victims. To assess their real-world impact, we design and implement MINIAUTH, the first analysis framework for systematically analyzing OBA misuse at scale. MINIAUTH automatically pinpoints the OBA login page of a mini-program, executes the workflow dynamically, and analyzes its runtime behaviors. This enables it to handle obfuscated mini-programs and uncover vulnerabilities that existing approaches cannot detect. Applying MINIAUTH to 44,273 WeChat and 2,721 Baidu mini-programs, we uncover 1,834 misuse cases, including critical logic flaws that enable client-side identity forgery via exposed credentials and authentication bypass through static or plaintext identifiers. Our cross-platform evaluation further shows that such misuses are not confined to a single ecosystem but consistently appear across different mini-program platforms. We also identify a cryptographic design flaw in Baidu's OBA APIs that allows brute-forcing of session keys. We responsibly disclosed our findings to the developers and platforms, receiving acknowledgments and assigned CNVD/CNNVD IDs. These results underscore the need for more robust developer guidance and enhanced platform-level safeguards.

</details>


### [133] [Multi-Agent Firewall Architecture for Privacy Protection of Sensitive Data in Interactions with Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08282)
*Hugo García Cuesta,Pablo Mateo Torrejón,Alfonso Sánchez-Macián*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个开源、注重隐私的用户端防火墙架构，结合浏览器扩展与代理拦截LLM交互流量，通过多智能体管道实现数据泄露防护，F1得分最高达94.93%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM集成到工作流中缺乏充分的安全保障，存在显著风险，需要一种面向用户的防火墙来保护Web和编程层面的LLM交互。

Method: 采用浏览器扩展加代理的双层架构，拦截HTTP(S)和WebSocket通信；核心为多智能体管道，结合确定性检测器与LLM驱动的语义分析实现混合数据泄露防护，支持可扩展组件及异构环境分层部署。

Result: 在最优配置下F1得分达到94.93%。

Conclusion: 该框架能够在兼顾计算成本、检测深度和延迟的同时，为LLM交互提供有效的安全防护，且具备良好的可扩展性。

Abstract: While Large Language Models (LLMs) have become essential productivity tools, their integration into workflows without adequate safeguards creates significant risks. This paper proposes an open-source, privacy-focused, user-facing firewall designed to secure both web-based and programmatic LLM interactions. The architecture combines a browser extension and a proxy for total traffic interception across both HTTP(S) and WebSocket communications. At its core, a flexible multi-agent pipeline delivers data leakage prevention through a hybrid approach combining deterministic detectors with LLM-driven semantic analysis, proprietary code leakage prevention, and extensible components designed for future security enhancements such as prompt injection evasion. The framework's layered architecture enables deployment across heterogeneous environments, allowing organizations to balance computational cost, detection depth and latency. Evaluation results demonstrate it achieves F1 scores of up to 94.93% on optimal configurations.

</details>


### [134] [From Legacy Documentation to OSCAL: An MCP-Based Agent Pipeline for Threat-Informed Continuous Compliance in Critical Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08288)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于MCP（模型上下文协议）的非侵入式多智能体管道，将自然语言系统描述转化为知识图谱和NIST OSCAL格式的审计就绪工件，用于持续自动化合规管理。在水务设施的合成场景中，实现了0.90的CVE召回率和完美的D3FEND召回率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键基础设施中的OT环境通常无法主动扫描，但又需要主动的系统反馈来进行风险评估和合规检查。

Method: 构建了MCP驱动的多智能体管道，将LLM推理与确定性知识检索（权威威胁情报源）解耦，由LLM从自然语言描述中提取资产，再通过MCP对接外部知识库进行验证和补全。

Result: CVE召回率0.90，D3FEND召回率完美；生成了符合Schema的OSCAL SSP和SAR报告。

Conclusion: MCP接地并不能完全消除幻觉等错误，但将错误迁移到了资产提取阶段——单个错误提取的实体可能导致后续产生真实但不相关的CVE。然而这使剩余风险变得可见、可验证且适合高效的人工审查。

Abstract: In critical infrastructure, operational technology environments often cannot be actively scanned, and yet active system feedback is needed for risk assessment and compliance. This paper presents a non-invasive, MCP-grounded multi-agent pipeline that converts natural-language system descriptions into source-verified knowledge graph and audit-ready artifacts in the NIST OSCAL format for continuous automated compliance management. The architecture decouples LLM-based reasoning from deterministic knowledge retrieval against authoritative threat-intelligence sources, reducing the risk of fabricated vulnerabilities and hallucinated attack paths.
  In an evidence-based synthetic scenario of a water utility, the pipeline achieves 0.90 CVE recall and perfect D3FEND recall. It generates a schema-valid OSCAL System Security Plan and an OSCAL Security Assessment Report. Nevertheless, the core insight is not that grounding via MCP eliminates errors (e.g., hallucinations) entirely from the pipeline, but that it shifts errors into the first phase of asset extraction from the natural language description. Here, a single incorrectly extracted entity can lead to genuine but irrelevant CVEs in subsequent stages of the pipeline, which consumes time and resources. However, it makes the remaining risk visible, verifiable, and suitable for a time-efficient manual review, since the infrastructure (e.g., version numbers, OS, etc.) is typically known.

</details>


### [135] [Reverse Engineering Compliance: A Dual-Graph Verification Framework for Auditing Legacy IT Security Concepts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08292)
*Lea Roxanne Muth,Marian Margraf*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ASSERT框架，通过本体提取、五类图差异分析和OSCAL导出，实现遗留IT安全文档的自动化合规验证，并发现文档-基础设施不一致性的可量化权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NIS-2指令要求持续、可审计的合规证据，推动从文档合规向机器可读合规制品迁移。OSCAL是相关标准，BSI正在适配Grundschutz++。但企业仍管理大量遗留IT安全概念文档，直接迁移可能引入过时资产。现有研究缺乏将遗留文档提取为可审计中间表示、确定性比较提取图与独立构建参考状态、并导出符合模式的OSCAL制品的验证框架。

Method: ASSERT框架包含三个核心步骤：(1) 基于本体的遗产文档提取，构建形式化文档图；(2) 五类图差异分析，与已验证参考图进行对比；(3) 导出为符合模式的OSCAL输出，用于系统描述和评估证据。使用BSI的RecPlast数据集，比较本地开源模型和商业模型在三种不同参考本体暴露程度配置下的表现。

Result: ASSERT能够量化文档-基础设施不一致性，但在发现未记录实体与强制执行模式之间存在权衡。

Conclusion: ASSERT框架弥补了遗留IT安全文档向机器可读合规格式迁移过程中的验证缺失，为NIS-2合规提供了可量化的审计手段。

Abstract: The NIS-2 Directive increases the need for continuous, auditable compliance evidence and motivates a shift from document-based compliance toward machine-readable compliance artifacts. The Open Security Controls Assessment Language (OSCAL) is a standard for this purpose, which the German Federal Office for Information Security (BSI) is adapting with Grundschutz++. However, companies are still managing extensive legacy IT security concepts (IT-SCs), and migrating them without verification could transfer outdated assets into the new format. While existing research primarily addresses the generation of new concepts, there is a lack of a verification framework that extracts legacy IT-SCs into an auditable intermediate representation, deterministically compares the extracted graph with an independently constructed reference state, and exports schema-valid OSCAL artifacts.
  This paper introduces the Automated Security Concept Structure Extraction and Reverse Topology-checking (ASSERT) Framework, which addresses this gap by using ontology-based extraction of legacy documents into formal document graphs, a five-class graph difference against a verified reference graph, and the export into schema-valid OSCAL outputs for system description and assessment evidence. Using the BSI's RecPlast dataset, we compare a local open-weight model and a commercial model across three configurations with different levels of reference-ontology exposure. The evaluation shows that ASSERT makes document-infrastructure inconsistencies measurable, but reveals a trade-off between discovering undocumented entities and enforcing a schema.

</details>


### [136] [Secure QR Codes: Authenticity Verification via EdDSA Signatures and CBOR Certificates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08383)
*Wojciech Jonderko,Wojciech Wodo*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种安全的QR码架构：(1) 完全离线的方案，使用EdDSA签名、CBOR证书和ZLIB压缩，在QR码静态容量内实现加密完整性；(2) 可扩展的混合Web PKI架构，利用JWKS端点、中央信任注册表和URL片段，支持动态实时验证并向后兼容标准相机，以消除QR码欺骗攻击。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QR码缺乏固有安全机制，易受欺骗攻击（如公共停车设备的quishing攻击），亟需可部署的安全解决方案。

Method: 首先实现完全离线方案（EdDSA/Ed25519签名 + CBOR证书 + ZLIB压缩）；随后提出混合Web PKI架构（JWKS端点 + 中央信任注册表 + URL片段），实现双模式（离线/在线）兼容设计。

Result: 离线方案证明可在QR码静态容量内实现加密完整性；混合架构解决了离线模型在智能城市大规模IoT部署中无法即时密钥撤销的可扩展性问题。

Conclusion: 双模式方案提供了从离线到在线验证的实用可部署路径，在保持向后兼容性的同时有效消除QR码欺骗攻击。

Abstract: QR codes are a ubiquitous part of daily life, widely trusted by millions. However, their lack of inherent security features has given rise to critical attack vectors, such as spoofing (quishing) on public infrastructure like self-service parking machines. To address this, we present a comprehensive evolution of secure QR code architectures. First, we evaluate a fully offline proof-of-concept leveraging EdDSA signatures (instantiated on the Ed25519 curve), CBOR-encoded certificates, and ZLIB compression, demonstrating that robust cryptographic integrity can be achieved within the QR code's strict static capacity. However, recognizing the scalability limitations of fully offline models-specifically the inability to perform immediate key revocation in massive smart-city IoT deployments-we subsequently propose a scalable Hybrid Web PKI architecture. This forward-looking model utilizes standardized JWKS endpoints, a Central Trust Registry, and URL fragments to ensure seamless backward compatibility with standard native cameras while providing dynamic, real-time validation for compliant applications. This dual-mode approach offers a practical, deployable path toward eliminating QR spoofing.

</details>


### [137] [Token-Flow Firewall: Semantic Runtime Auditing for Persistent AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08395)
*Puji Wang,Yingchen Zhang,Ruqing Zhang,Jiafeng Guo,Xueqi Cheng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TokenWall是一个针对持久化AI代理的运行时防御框架，通过在token流上构建语义防火墙，实现全覆盖的执行前安全审计。实验表明，它能将攻击成功率降至12.5%，同时保持97.4%的良性通过率，仅增加0.69秒延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持久化AI代理通过持久状态、可复用技能和工具调用扩展了LLM的能力，但这也使得不安全内容可以在组件间传播，形成更大的语义攻击面。现有方法依赖稀疏审计或远程大模型审查，无法实现全面覆盖且代价高昂。

Method: 提出TokenWall框架，作为token流上的语义防火墙：1）对组件间token流进行边界感知的语义审计，构建结构化的源-汇审计记录；2）在执行前进行轻量级本地检查；3）对高危模糊案例选择性升级到更强的仲裁模块。实现全覆盖的执行前中介而非事后稀疏审计。

Result: 在CIK-Bench上，TokenWall将攻击成功率降至12.5%，良性可执行通过率达97.4%，无需人工确认。良性案例仅引入0.69秒额外延迟。

Conclusion: TokenWall证明了语义级别的运行时隔离可以在持久化AI代理中实现实用的安全-效用权衡，为代理系统提供了可部署的安全屏障方案。

Abstract: Persistent AI agents extend large language models (LLMs) beyond single-turn interaction into long-lived software systems. Unlike traditional chat assistants, unsafe content in these agents can propagate through persistent state, reusable skills, and tool-mediated interactions, creating a substantially larger semantic attack surface. We observe that most security-critical interactions in such agents are transmitted through natural-language token flows, including memory updates, tool arguments, retrieved files, and inter-component communications. This observation enables a new security formulation: unsafe behavior can be intercepted as risky semantic flows before reaching privileged runtime sinks. Based on this insight, we propose TokenWall, a runtime defense framework that acts as a semantic firewall over agent token flows. TokenWall performs boundary-aware semantic auditing over these flows, constructing structured source-sink audit records, applying lightweight local inspection before execution, and selectively escalating ambiguous high-risk cases to stronger arbitration modules. Unlike prior approaches that rely on sparse auditing or remote large-model oversight, TokenWall enables full-coverage pre-execution mediation while reducing remote arbitration and latency. Experiments on CIK-Bench show that TokenWall reduces attack success rate to 12.5% while maintaining a 97.4% benign executable pass rate without human confirmation. TokenWall further introduces only 0.69 seconds of additional latency on benign cases, demonstrating that semantic runtime containment can achieve a practical security-utility trade-off for persistent AI agents.

</details>


### [138] [TRACE: A Two-Channel Robust Attribution Watermark via Complementary Embeddings for LLM-Agent Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08400)
*Zheng Gao,Xiaoyu Li,Xiaoyan Feng,Jiaojiao Jiang,Yang Song,Yulei Sui,Zhenchang Xing,Liming Zhu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: TRACE是首个在代理系统中实现无失真、自同步且对删除和重写具有不变性的行为水印方法，通过叠加选择通道（基于内容密钥）和计数通道（基于位置密钥）来抵御拥有轨迹日志完全读写权限的恶意经销商。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理通过经销商分发时，经销商可能篡改或替换模型，而归属争议依赖轨迹日志作为证据。现有水印方法无法抵御对日志具有完全读写权限的恶意攻击者，因为它们的归属信息直接存储在日志中。

Method: TRACE叠加两个水印通道：1) 选择通道——基于局部内容密钥，使用无失真采样器设置选择哪个动作，保证代理分布不变且在删除后能重同步；2) 计数通道——基于日志骨架（位置）密钥，设置每个决策组包含的记录数量，重写无法影响。

Result: 在ToolBench和ALFWorld上，TRACE匹配未水印代理的成功率；选择通道在长轨迹上达到z≈100的检测分数，在70%步骤删除下仍可检测；计数通道在任意强度LLM重写下完全不变。

Conclusion: 提出了首个同时满足无失真、自同步（抗删除）和重写不变性的代理水印方案，证明了行为水印的信号以决策熵为代价，每个决策至少贡献一半熵，确定性决策无贡献，且擦除两个通道会迫使经销商破坏其转售的轨迹。

Abstract: LLM agents reach users through resellers, who may rebrand a developer's agent or substitute a cheaper model. When provenance is disputed, attribution rests on the trajectory log (the record of tool calls, observations, and executed actions, not the model's reasoning), which the reseller stores and processes to meter usage. A watermark must therefore survive an adversary with full read/write access to the very evidence it is detected from; existing agent watermarks do not, as their attribution is read straight off that log. We present TRACE, to our knowledge the first agent watermark that is distortion-free in its action choices, self-synchronizing under deletion, and unconditionally invariant under rewriting. Deletion desynchronizes a position-derived key and rewriting alters content, so a deletion-robust key must come from content and a rewrite-robust key from position, and no single key serves both. A trajectory, however, has room for two watermarks. TRACE superposes a selection channel that sets which action is chosen, keyed on local content with a distortion-free sampler, so the agent's distribution is provably unchanged and detection resynchronizes after deletions, and a tally channel that sets how many records each decision group holds, keyed on the log's skeleton alone, which no rewriting can touch. We prove this behavioral watermark's signal is bought with decision entropy, each decision paying at least half its entropy and deterministic decisions nothing, and that erasing both channels forces the reseller to corrupt the trajectories it resells. On ToolBench and ALFWorld, TRACE matches the unwatermarked agent's success rate while its selection channel reaches detection scores near z = 100 on long-horizon trajectories, stays detectable under 70% step deletion, and keeps a tally channel exactly unchanged under LLM rewriting of any strength.

</details>


### [139] [TRM-Raft: A Byzantine-Resistant Raft Consensus via Integrated Trust and Reputation Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08666)
*Jie Zhang,Xubo Fan,Xiaohong Li,Zhiyong Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出TRM-Raft，通过非侵入式集成区块链信任与声誉模型（B-TRM）增强Raft共识的拜占庭容错能力，在保持低开销的同时有效防御选举伪造和日志篡改等恶意行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Raft共识协议仅假设崩溃故障，无法抵御拜占庭行为（如选举伪造、日志篡改）。现有BFT协议开销过高，而针对性加固缺乏统一防御机制。

Method: 将区块链信任与声誉模型（B-TRM）非侵入式嵌入Raft共识核心，量化节点多维度行为，采用自适应惩罚区分偶然故障与恶意行为，并将声誉值融入领导者选举和日志复制流程。利用Schnorr签名机制让跟随者验证日志完整性。

Result: 在Hyperledger Fabric上真实互联网环境测试，即使在40%拜占庭节点下，恶意领导者比例低于5%，吞吐量损失<10%，延迟增加<5%。

Conclusion: TRM-Raft为依赖Raft的互联网软件系统提供了一条轻量级、实用的可信增强路径。

Abstract: Internetware envisions autonomous software entities collaborating over the open Internet. Raft consensus is widely adopted for its simplicity and performance in distributed coordination, e.g., service registries and blockchains. However, Raft assumes crash faults only, making it vulnerable to Byzantine behaviors like election forgery and log tampering. Existing BFT protocols incur high overhead, while ad-hoc hardening lacks unified defense.
  We propose \textbf{TRM-Raft}, a Byzantine-resistant enhancement that non-intrusively integrates a Blockchain-based Trust and Reputation Model (B-TRM) into the consensus core. It quantifies multi-dimensional node behaviors, applies adaptive penalties distinguishing accidental faults from malice, and embeds reputation into leader election and log replication. A reputation-aware election penalizes term/index forgery, excluding low-reputation nodes from leadership. A Schnorr-signature-based mechanism lets followers verify log integrity; tampering triggers reputation decay and leader replacement. Evaluated on Hyperledger Fabric in a realistic Internetware setting, TRM-Raft keeps malicious leader ratio below 5\% even with 40\% Byzantine nodes, with <10\% throughput loss and <5\% latency increase over vanilla Raft. TRM-Raft offers a lightweight, practical trustworthiness path for Internetware systems relying on Raft.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [140] [The Regularization Parameter: Sparse Precision Matrix Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07735)
*Aryan Eftekhari,Daniel Sergio Vega,Ernst-Jan Camiel Wit,Olaf Schenk*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一种闭式矩阵值正则化参数，基于ℓ1正则化高斯极大似然估计的一阶最优性条件的采样分布，无需交叉验证即可实现精度矩阵估计的稀疏性控制，在保证统计一致性的同时大幅降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维低样本量数据中，稀疏精度矩阵估计的正则化参数选择是关键挑战，传统交叉验证方法计算成本高且难以平衡欠拟合与过拟合。

Method: 从ℓ1正则化高斯极大似然估计的一阶最优性条件的采样分布出发，推导闭式矩阵值正则化参数；通过规定重采样下估计量非零条目满足最优性条件的概率，避免交叉验证。

Result: 在标准条件下，该正则化参数具有渐近缩放性质，保证估计量的一致性和稀疏一致性。在合成高斯/非高斯数据、基因微阵列和神经影像应用中，估计精度与交叉验证相当，支持恢复更优，运行时间降低数个数量级。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持统计性能的同时实现了计算效率的大幅提升，为高维精度矩阵估计提供了一种无需交叉验证的实用替代方案。

Abstract: Sparse precision matrix estimation provides an interpretable and computationally efficient framework for modeling conditional dependencies in high-dimensional, low-sample-size data. A recurring challenge is appropriately selecting the regularization parameter that controls estimator sparsity and strikes a balance between underfitting and overfitting. We propose a closed-form, matrix-valued regularization parameter derived from the sampling distribution of the first-order optimality conditions of the $\ell_1$-regularized Gaussian maximum-likelihood estimator. By prescribing the probability that each nonzero entry of the estimator satisfies its optimality condition under resampling, we eliminate the need for cross-validation. The resulting regularization parameter is shown to attain asymptotic scaling properties that, under standard conditions, provide consistency and sparsistency of the estimator. On synthetic Gaussian and non-Gaussian datasets, as well as real-world gene microarray and neuroimaging applications, the proposed approach achieves estimation accuracy comparable to cross-validation, delivers superior support recovery, and reduces runtime by several orders of magnitude.

</details>


### [141] [Distributionally Faithful Imputation via Positive Semi-Definite Kernel Density Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07767)
*Andrea Basteri,Carlo Ciliberto,Alessandro Rudi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PSD Impute方法，将缺失值插补问题重新定义为基于遮蔽观测的密度估计，利用正半定核密度实现凸经验风险优化，在MCAR假设下提供统计一致性和自适应快速超额风险，实验表明在分布准确性上具有竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有插补方法多依赖启发式或参数化假设，忽略了联合数据分布，缺乏理论保证。作者旨在将插补问题与密度估计统一，提供有统计理论支撑的插补框架。

Method: 将MCAR下的插补重构为从遮蔽观测中进行密度估计，利用正半定核密度构建凸经验风险问题，其边际分布具有闭式解，并通过牛顿内点法求解。

Result: 模型支持单次和多次插补，具有统计一致性，自适应超额风险率可突破维度诅咒（对非常正则的概率分布），在1个合成和11个真实数据集上取得了与流行基线方法相当的分布准确性。

Conclusion: PSD Impute在理论上具有一致性保证和快速收敛率，实践上展现出良好的分布准确性，具有强大的应用前景。

Abstract: Missing values undermine statistical inference and machine learning pipelines, yet most imputation methods rely on heuristics or restrictive parametric assumptions that ignore the joint data distribution. We recast imputation under missing completely at random (MCAR) as density estimation from masked observations: estimate a distribution whose observed marginals exactly match those in the data. Leveraging positive semi definite (PSD) kernel densities we obtain a convex empirical risk problem with closed form marginals, solvable by a Newton interior point method. The resulting PSD Impute model yields both single and multiple imputations from the same fitted density, enjoys statistical consistency with fast adaptive excess risk beating the curse of dimensionality for very regular probabilities. Preliminary experiments on one synthetic and eleven real world datasets already indicate competitive distributional accuracy compared with popular imputation baselines, suggesting strong practical promise.

</details>


### [142] [Expressivity and Statistical Trade-offs in Diffusion Policy Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07967)
*Viet Vu,Renyuan Xu,Jiacheng Zhang,Yufei Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了扩散策略的Lipschitz预算K如何控制其表达能力和统计行为，建立了近似误差与统计复杂度之间的权衡，推导出神经网络的有限样本性能界，并提出了根据样本量选择K的实际原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散策略在强化学习中展现出强大的表达能力，能够建模复杂、多模态、非高斯的动作分布，但其数学基础尚不清晰，特别是这种表达能力的来源以及如何在有限数据下充分利用它。

Method: (1) 将漂移Lipschitz预算K识别为控制扩散策略表达性和统计行为的关键量；(2) 通过近似理论量化表达能力：K-Lipschitz漂移可以逼近最优确定性策略，近似误差为1/K阶；(3) 分析神经网络参数化下的统计代价，得到有限样本性能界；(4) 通过数值实验验证K的样本依赖权衡。

Result: (1) 表达能力上界：近似误差与1/K成比例，且有匹配下界；(2) 通用神经网络漂移的有限样本性能差距为Õ(n^{-2/(m+6)})；(3) 单边耗散漂移类的更优率为Õ(n^{-2/(m+4)})；(4) 数值实验支持理论权衡。

Conclusion: 扩散策略的Lipschitz预算K是控制表达能力和统计复杂度的核心量，提出了根据样本量选择K并固定对应Lipschitz系数的神经网络架构的实践原则。

Abstract: Diffusion-based policies have recently emerged as powerful policy parameterizations for reinforcement learning, representing state-conditioned action distributions as terminal laws of diffusion processes with parameterized drifts. This terminal-law representation has shown substantial expressive flexibility in practice, enabling diffusion policies to model complex, multimodal, and highly non-Gaussian action distributions; however, it remains unclear what mathematically drives this expressivity and how to fully exploit it when the policy is learned from finite data. In this paper, we identify the drift Lipschitz budget $K$ as a central quantity governing the expressivity and statistical behavior of diffusion policies. We quantify expressivity through approximation: diffusion policies with $K$-Lipschitz drifts can concentrate near optimal deterministic policies and achieve value approximation error of order $1/K$; moreover, we prove a matching lower bound under nondegenerate diffusion noise. This increased expressivity comes with a statistical cost. When the drift is parameterized by neural networks, increasing $K$ improves approximation but increases statistical complexity. Balancing these two terms yields a finite-sample performance gap of order $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+6)})$ for generic neural-network drifts, and a sharper rate $\tilde{O}(n^{-2/(m+4)})$ for one-sided dissipative drift classes, where $n$ is the sample size and $m$ is the dimension of the state space. Numerical experiments provide empirical evidence for the sample-dependent trade-off in $K$, supporting both theoretical regimes. Our framework also suggests a practical implementation principle: choose the diffusion budget $K$ according to the available sample size, and then select a neural-network architecture with the corresponding fixed Lipschitz coefficient.

</details>


### [143] [Bayesian Experimental Design via Score Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08335)
*Angus Phillips,Gavin Kerrigan,Tom Rainforth*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将贝叶斯实验设计（BED）中期望信息增益（EIG）的双重难解性与策略学习分离的方法，通过先求解一个与策略无关的分数匹配问题，再使用学到的分数近似以单重难解的方式训练策略，从而将关键的计算成本从乘法变为加法，大幅降低策略训练的计算负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于策略的贝叶斯实验设计方法中，期望信息增益（EIG）的双重难解性导致策略训练需要昂贵或复杂的近似计算，限制了策略优化的投入，亟需一种能隔离双重难解性、降低策略训练成本的方法。

Method: 首先求解一个与策略无关的分数匹配问题，得到对数边际似然的分数近似；然后利用该分数近似将EIG从双重难解转化为单重难解，从而以更低成本训练深度策略网络。

Result: 在不引入似然评估乘法成本的情况下训练了多个竞争性策略，通过选择最佳策略（即使不进行超参数或架构搜索）提升了性能。

Conclusion: 该方法将关键计算成本从乘法变为加法，显著减轻策略训练的计算负担，使得多次训练策略（如用于架构搜索、超参数调优或避免局部最优）变得更加经济可行。

Abstract: Policy-based approaches to Bayesian experimental design (BED) allow the learning of deep policy networks that adaptively make intelligent design decisions based on previously collected data. However, the training of such policies is often held back by a fundamental challenge: the double intractability of the expected information gain (EIG). This necessitates expensive or complex approximations that restrict the effort one can invest in optimising the policy itself. To address this, we show that the double intractability of the EIG can be isolated from the policy learning by first solving a score matching problem that is independent of the policy used, then using the learned score approximation to train the policy in a singly intractable manner. This turns the key multiplicative cost into an additive one and reduces the computational burden on the policy training itself, making it far cheaper to train the policy multiple times when needed, e.g. for architecture search, hyperparameter tuning, or avoiding local optima. In our experiments we train multiple competitive policies without inducing a multiplicative cost in likelihood evaluations, which can increase performance by allowing us to select the best policy even without performing hyperparameter or architecture searches.

</details>


### [144] [Prediction-Powered Active Testing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08347)
*Kianoosh Ashouritaklimi,Valentin Kilian,Daolang Huang,Tom Rainforth,François Caron*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出PPAT框架，通过结合LURE无偏估计器与预测驱动控制变量，在主动测试中利用黑盒模型预测来高效估计风险。该方法通过残差化损失保持无偏性、降低方差，并设计了面向方差缩减的获取规则。实验表明PPAT在风险估计和置信区间覆盖方面优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有主动测试方法未能充分利用黑盒模型的信息性预测来提升标签效率，尽管在许多标签昂贵的场景中这些预测已可获取。

Method: 将LURE无偏估计器与预测驱动控制变量结合，通过残差化损失而非伪标签方式保持无偏性；推导了面向方差缩减的oracle和基于代理的获取规则；并建立了渐近正态性以保证置信区间有效性。

Result: 在表格回归和图像分类任务上，PPAT在风险估计上优于现有方法，且置信区间在更少标签和更窄宽度下达到目标覆盖率。

Conclusion: PPAT是一种标签高效的风险估计框架，能在利用黑盒模型预测的同时保持无偏估计，并通过针对性获取规则进一步提升效率。

Abstract: Active testing provides a label--efficient approach to risk estimation by adaptively selecting which test points should be labelled. However, existing estimators fail to exploit the informative predictions of powerful black--box models, even though such predictions are increasingly available in settings where labels remain expensive. To address this, we propose \textbf{Prediction--Powered Active Testing (PPAT)}, a novel label--efficient risk estimation framework that combines the unbiased LURE estimator \citep{farquhar2021statistical} with a prediction--powered control variate. Rather than using proxy predictions as biased pseudo--labels, PPAT uses them to residualise the loss, preserving unbiasedness while reducing variance. Beyond the estimator itself, PPAT also changes which points should be acquired: we derive oracle and practical surrogate--based acquisition rules tailored to reducing the variance of our estimator. Moreover, we establish asymptotic normality for PPAT, yielding asymptotically valid confidence intervals and thus a principled estimate of the uncertainty around our estimates. Across tabular regression and image--classification tasks, PPAT outperforms existing methods in risk estimation, while its confidence intervals attain the target coverage with substantially fewer labels and smaller widths.

</details>


### [145] [Statistical Efficiency and Inference of Quantile Distributional Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08444)
*Zijie Cheng,Yang Peng,Zhihua Zhang*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文从统计效率角度研究基于分位数的分布强化学习，证明了分位数投影贝尔曼方程诱导的估计量在固定分位数和非参数极限下均能达到半参数效率界，并建立了Berry-Esseen定理为统计推断提供理论基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布强化学习旨在刻画回报分布而非期望回报，但现有方法缺乏严格的统计效率分析。本文动机是回答：基于分位数的分布策略评估在统计上是否最优？能否达到半参数效率界？

Method: 基于生成模型构建经验马尔可夫决策过程的估计量η_m^(n)；在固定分位数m下推导非渐近误差界（W_∞度量下误差为Õ(√(m/n))）；推导渐近正态性并刻画半参数效率界；进一步研究分位数数量趋于无穷时的渐近协方差结构，证明其匹配非参数模型的效率界；最后建立平滑泛函的Berry-Esseen定理。

Result: 固定分位数下估计误差为Õ(√(m/n))，达到最优参数√n收敛速度；渐近分布达到半参数效率界；分位数数量趋于无穷时仍保持渐近有效，匹配非参数模型的效率界；Berry-Esseen定理为统计推断提供了理论保证。

Conclusion: 基于分位数的分布策略评估方法在统计上是渐近有效的，无论是在固定维度还是无限维极限下均能达到对应的效率界，为分布强化学习提供了严格的统计理论基础。

Abstract: In this paper, we study quantile-based distributional reinforcement learning from the perspective of statistical efficiency. We focus on distributional policy evaluation, whose goal is to characterize the return distribution, namely the distribution of discounted cumulative rewards under a given policy. To obtain a finite-dimensional representation of the return distribution, we consider the quantile fixed point $η_m$ induced by the quantile-projected distributional Bellman equation. Assuming access to a generative model, we construct an estimator $η_m^{(n)}$ based on an empirical Markov decision process. For a fixed number of quantiles $m$, we establish a non-asymptotic error bound for $η_m^{(n)}$ and $η_m$ under the supremum $W_\infty$ metric, showing that the estimation error scales as $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{m/n})$ with respect to $m$ and $n$. This implies that the quantile-based distributional policy evaluation problem can be solved with sample efficiency, achieving the optimal parametric $\sqrt{n}$ convergence rate. We derive the asymptotic distribution of the quantile parameters $\sqrt{n}(θ_m^{(n)}-θ_m)$ and characterize the semiparametric efficiency bound, which is attained by our estimator. Beyond the fixed-dimensional setting, we investigate the asymptotic regime in which the number of quantiles diverges. We characterize the limit covariance structure and show that it matches the semiparametric efficiency bound of the nonparametric model for distributional policy evaluation, showing that quantile-based estimators remain asymptotically efficient in the infinite-dimensional limit. Finally, we establish a Berry--Esseen theorem for smooth functionals $\sqrt{n}(η_m^{(n)}(s)-η_m(s))f$, thereby providing a foundation for statistically valid inference on functionals of the quantile-projected return distribution.

</details>


### [146] [High-Dimensional Procrustes Matching via Tree Counts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08538)
*Xiaochun Niu,Tselil Schramm,Jiaming Xu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文针对高维Procrustes匹配问题，提出了一种基于计数特定“宽”树家族的多项式时间算法，在常数相关性下实现精确恢复，并给出了信息论下界与低度优势分析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Procrustes匹配问题在低维(d=O(log n))已被充分研究，但高维(d≫log n)区域仍几乎空白——现有匹配保证即使在信息论恢复层面也要求近乎完美的相关性ρ=1-o(1)。本文旨在填补高维场景下的理论空白。

Method: 通过计算并比较一组精心选择的“宽”树的加权计数来实现匹配。算法在d≥polylog(n)时，对于任意ρ²>√α（α≈0.338为Otter树计数常数）能以高概率成功。

Result: 提出了多项式时间算法，在常数相关性下实现精确恢复；改进了信息论保证，表明当ρ²≳max{log n/d, √(log n/n)}时精确恢复可行；低度优势计算暗示ρ²>√α是任何树计数算法的必要条件。

Conclusion: 本文在高维Procrustes匹配问题上取得了突破，首次在常数相关性下实现多项式时间精确恢复，并给出了紧的信息论下界和算法条件必要性分析。

Abstract: Suppose we observe two sets of $n$ Gaussian vectors in $\mathbb{R}^d$, with the promise that, after applying a permutation of $[n]$ and a rotation of $\mathbb{R}^d$, the two sets are $ρ$-correlated. The Procrustes matching problem asks us to recover the unknown permutation of $[n]$ that aligns the two sets. The problem is well-studied in the low-dimensional regime $d=O(\log n)$, but the high-dimensional regime $d\gg \log n$ has remained largely uncharted: prior matching guarantees require nearly perfect correlation $ρ=1-o(1)$, even for information-theoretic recovery.
  Our main result is a polynomial-time algorithm for exact recovery at constant correlation. The algorithm works by computing and comparing weighted counts of a specially chosen family of ``wide'' trees. So long as $d\ge \mathrm{polylog}(n)$, the algorithm succeeds with high probability for any $ρ^2>\sqrtα$, where $α\approx 0.338$ is Otter's tree-counting constant.
  We complement this algorithmic result with an improved information-theoretic guarantee, showing that exact recovery is possible when $ρ^2 \gtrsim \max\{\log n/d,\sqrt{\log n/n}\}$. We also carry out a low-degree advantage calculation, which suggests that the condition $ρ^2 > \sqrtα$ is necessary for any tree-counting algorithm.

</details>


### [147] [Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08757)
*Yiwei Zhou*

Main category: stat.ML
