TL;DR: 论文证明了分数匹配的前向边际误差小并不保证逆向采样的数值稳定性，构造了反例使得前向误差任意小、路径空间总变差距离趋于零，但Euler-Maruyama离散化的每个Wasserstein距离发散。针对紧支撑数据给出了投影去噪器的正面结果，实验验证了罕见轨迹上的大增长及其抑制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分数匹配训练仅控制前向边际下的平均误差，但逆向采样器沿自身轨迹评估学习到的分数场，二者之间的失配可能导致数值不稳定。现有理论缺乏对这种不稳定性的严格刻画。

Method: 构造了一个光滑分数场，使其前向边际L²误差任意小，逆向过程在路径空间总变差距离上任意接近真实过程，但Euler-Maruyama离散化虽然依概率收敛，每个正阶矩却发散（因此所有Wasserstein距离W_p, p≥1发散）。进一步在固定神经网络架构内构造了有界全局Lipschitz去噪器族，使前向误差和路径空间TV同时趋于零，但Euler-Maruyama端点发散。对于紧支撑数据，提出投影到已知有界闭凸集上的方法保持逐点精度、提供网格一致矩界并保证Wasserstein收敛。

Result: 前向边际误差小→数值稳定不成立；存在反例使弱收敛成立但所有Wasserstein距离发散；投影方法有效缓解该问题；实验验证了理论发现。

Conclusion: 分数匹配训练应关注逆向采样过程的数值稳定性而非仅前向边际误差；投影到数据支撑集是简单有效的补救措施。

Abstract: Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges.
  The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [The logistic-normal integral and the moments of the logistic-normal distribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07889)
*Dan Pirjol*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究逻辑斯蒂-正态积分的数值计算，提出基于最优级数展开的高精度算法，并给出逻辑斯蒂-正态随机变量前两阶矩的显式结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 逻辑斯蒂-正态积分在含高斯随机效应的逻辑斯蒂模型及广义线性混合模型的统计估计中频繁出现，其精确数值计算对模型推断至关重要。

Method: 研究该积分的数值评估及其导数，给出特定点的闭式解和级数展开；在连续的级数展开谱系中筛选出适合数值计算的最优级数，并基于此设计算法，在尾部具有良好的近似误差控制。

Result: 提出了基于最优级数的高精度数值评估算法，尾部误差可控；给出了逻辑斯蒂-正态随机变量前两阶矩的显式结果。

Conclusion: 所提算法能有效且精确地计算逻辑斯蒂-正态积分及其导数，为相关统计模型的估计提供了可行的数值工具。

Abstract: The logistic-normal integral appears in problems of statistical estimation for logistic models with Gaussian random effects, and generalized linear mixed models. We study the numerical evaluation of this integral and of its derivatives, and give closed form evaluations at certain points and series expansions. There is a continuum of possible series expansions, and we single out one series expansion which is optimal for numerical evaluation. We propose an algorithm for a precise numerical evaluation, based on the optimal series, with good approximation error control in the tails. As an application we give explicit results for the first two moments of a logistic-normal random variable.

</details>


### [149] [From Bayes' Rule to Bayes Rules: Optimal Information Processing and Axiomatic Foundations Beyond Probability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08019)
*Jeremie Houssineau,Badr-Eddine Chérief-Abdellatif*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文为可能性推断开发了规范更新规则，从信息守恒和公理化两个视角导出了一致的后验更新公式：先验-似然乘积再取上确界归一化。两种推导仅在学习率参数的位置上有差异，该参数控制认知强度，无法仅从归一化证据中识别，从而澄清了广义贝叶斯更新中类似参数的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为可能性推断建立原则性的更新规则，并从信息守恒和公理化两个互补基础推导出同一规范更新公式，同时澄清学习率参数在广义贝叶斯更新中的作用。

Method: 从信息守恒视角（基于最大熵/最小信息原理）和公理化视角（基于合理性假设）两个互补基础分别推导，两者均得到相同的后验更新公式：先验与似然的乘法结合后经上确界归一化，仅在学习率参数的引入位置上有差异。

Result: 两个推导路径得到相同的规范更新规则：后验 = 上确界归一化(先验 × 似然)。学习率参数控制认知强度，但无法仅通过归一化证据识别。

Conclusion: 为可能性推断提供了理论基础，所推导的更新规则与广义贝叶斯更新框架建立了联系，并解释了学习率参数的角色与识别问题。

Abstract: This paper develops principled updating rules for possibilistic inference, where uncertainty about a fixed parameter is represented by a possibility function, the maxitive analogue of a probability distribution, and comparisons are made pointwise via a partial order. From two complementary foundations, an information-conservation viewpoint and an axiomatic viewpoint, we derive the same canonical update: the posterior is the prior-likelihood product followed by supremum normalisation. The two derivations agree for an arbitrary loss, differing only in where the learning-rate parameter enters. This parameter controls epistemic strength and is not identifiable from the normalising evidence alone, clarifying the role of analogous learning-rate parameters in generalised Bayesian updating.

</details>


### [150] [Parameter inference for partially observed branching processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08157)
*Simone Baldassarri,Michel Mandjes,Jiesen Wang*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种年龄依赖的分支过程，在将种群分为青少年和成人两个年龄组的最简单设置下，仅利用总种群规模（青少年加成人）的观测数据来估计模型参数。针对模型的遍历性状态，提出了一种矩估计方法并建立了其渐近正态性，同时讨论了包括多个年龄组在内的多种扩展情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在年龄依赖的分支过程中，实际观测往往仅能获得总种群规模而无法获取各年龄组的详细数据（例如青少年和成人的具体数量），因此需要发展仅基于总种群规模观测的参数估计方法。

Method: 针对年龄依赖分支过程的遍历性状态，提出了一种矩估计方法（method-of-moments estimator），并建立了估计量的渐近正态性。

Result: 建立了所提出矩估计量的渐近正态性，并讨论了扩展到多个年龄组（超过两个）的情况。

Conclusion: 本文的方法能够在仅观测总种群规模的条件下有效估计年龄依赖分支过程的模型参数，并具有良好的渐近统计性质，且可推广至更一般的多年龄组设置。

Abstract: In this paper, we study an age-dependent branching process. In the simplest setting, the population is divided into two age groups, namely juveniles and adults. Our objective is to estimate the model parameters using observations of the total population size only (i.e., juveniles plus adults). Focusing on the ergodic regime of the model, we introduce a method-of-moments estimator and establish its asymptotic normality. Several extensions are discussed, including models with more than two age groups.

</details>


### [151] [An Exact Distribution-Free Test for Means of Nonnegative Random Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08415)
*Nikos Vlassis,Philip S. Thomas*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明了一个关于独立非负随机变量的分布自由p值猜想：当每个变量的期望≤1时，统计量K(X)服从均匀分布(0,1)，可用于检验联合均值假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 证明Gaffke(2005)提出的猜想，即构造一个有限样本、分布自由的p值来检验多个独立非负随机变量的期望是否均不超过1。

Method: 利用Dirichlet分布(1,...,1)与独立非负随机变量的独立性，定义统计量K(x)=P{∑x_iD_i≤1}，通过概率不等式证明对任意n≥1，当E[X_i]≤1时，P{K(X)≤α}≤α成立。

Result: 对于任意n≥1，只要每个E[X_i]≤1，就有P{K(X)≤α}≤α对所有α∈[0,1]成立，即K(X)是有效的p值。

Conclusion: 成功证明了Gaffke(2005)的猜想，提供了一个有限样本、无需分布假设的p值方法，可用于检验多个独立非负随机变量的均值联合约束。

Abstract: Let $X=(X_1,\ldots,X_n)$ be independent nonnegative random variables, not necessarily identically distributed. Let $D=(D_0,D_1,\ldots,D_n)\sim\operatorname{Dir}(1,\ldots,1)$ be independent of $X$, and define $K(x)=\mathbb{P}\{\sum_{i=1}^n x_iD_i\le1\}$. We prove that, for every $n\ge1$, whenever $\mathbb{E} X_i\le1$ for every $i$, $\mathbb{P}\{K(X)\leα\}\leα$ for all $0\leα\le1$. Thus $K(X)$ is a finite-sample, distribution-free $p$-value for testing the null hypothesis $\mathbb{E}X_i \le 1$ for all $i$. This proves a conjecture of Gaffke (2005).

</details>


### [152] [Building confidence regions for Reeb graphs using the interleaving distance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08458)
*Matteo Pegoraro,Alberto Conforti,Mathieu Carrière*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文针对Reeb图从有限样本中构建置信区域的问题，采用交织距离（interleaving distance）作为度量。以点云及其滤波函数为输入，构建有限邻近图并线性扩展滤波，用所得滤图的Reeb余层作为主估计量。Mapper图被视为该估计量基于受控覆盖的粗化版本，从而将统计逼近与可视化分离。在内在度量与外蕴度量（正可达假设下）下证明了Reeb估计量的稳定性，并基于标准采样假设或子采样Hausdorff尺度估计推导了交织距离置信区域。此外，证明了扩展持续伪度量不超过两倍交织距离，且H₀相关分量的常数可达1。数值实验展示了如何识别统计显著特征并投影至Mapper图进行解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为Reeb图从有限样本中构建置信区域，填补该领域缺乏统计推断框架的空白；同时分离统计估计与可视化两个环节，使Mapper图的参数选择具有统计依据。

Method: 以交织距离为核心度量，将Reeb余层作为主估计量，Mapper图作为受控粗化版本；在内在和外蕴度量下建立稳定性界，通过标准采样假设或子采样Hausdorff尺度估计推导置信区域；并分析了与扩展持续伪度量之间的关系。

Result: 在内在和外蕴度量下均获得Reeb估计量的稳定性界；推导出基于交织距离的置信区域；证明了扩展持续伪度量被两倍交织距离控制，H₀相关分量的常数可达1。

Conclusion: 提出了一套从有限样本构建Reeb图置信区域的完整框架，有效分离了统计推断与可视化问题，为Mapper图的参数选择提供了统计理论基础。

Abstract: We develop confidence regions for Reeb graphs from finite samples using the interleaving distance. Given a point cloud equipped with a filter function, we construct a finite proximity graph, extend the filter linearly, and use the Reeb cosheaf of the resulting filtered graph as the primary estimator. Mapper graphs are then treated as controlled cover-based coarsenings of this estimator, separating the statistical approximation problem from the visualization problem. We prove stability bounds for the Reeb estimators obtained both using intrinsic and extrinsic metrics, the latter under positive-reach assumptions, and derive interleaving-distance confidence regions from either \((a,b)\)-standard sampling assumptions or subsampling-based Hausdorff scale estimates. We also compare this object-level metric viewpoint with persistence-based guarantees by showing that the extended-persistence pseudometric is bounded by twice the interleaving distance, with sharp constant \(1\) for the \(H_0\)-related components. Numerical experiments illustrate how statistically significant features can be identified and then projected to Mapper graphs for interpretation.

</details>


### [153] [A screening approach to nonparametric inference from the M/G/1 workload](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08472)
*Royi Jacobovic,Binyamin Kobzantsev*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文解决了Hansen和Pitt(2006)提出的一个长期未解决的开放问题：在M/G/1工作负荷模型中，无需稳定性或平稳性假设，仅基于离散观测的工作负荷轨迹，以参数化$L^1$风险率（除对数因子外）估计服务时间分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决Hansen和Pitt(2006)提出的关于M/G/1工作负荷模型中服务时间分布的非参数推断的长期开放问题，该问题此前缺乏一个无需平稳性或稳定性假设、且能达到参数化收敛速度的估计方法。

Method: 提出一个仅基于观测工作负荷轨迹的估计量$B_n(w)$，通过筛选机制从工作负荷过程中提取条件独立同分布的复合泊松增量，将依赖数据问题转化为拉普拉斯变换反演框架。

Result: 在$B(\cdot)$满足连续可微、在$w$处二次可微且二阶矩有限等温和假设下，建立了$\mathbb{E}|B_n(w)-B(w)| = \mathcal{O}(\log n / \sqrt{n})$的误差界，这是首个达到参数化$L^1$风险率（除对数因子外）的解。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在不要求平稳性、稳定性或已知到达率的情况下，以接近参数化的收敛速度解决了Hansen-Pitts问题，具有重要的理论意义。

Abstract: We address a long-standing open problem posed by Hansen and Pitts (2006) on nonparametric inference for the service-time distribution in an M/G/1 workload model. We consider an M/G/1 queue with unknown arrival rate $λ>0$ and service-time distribution $B(\cdot)$, without assuming stability or stationarity. A statistician observes the workload process at discrete times $t=0,1,\ldots,n$ and aims to estimate $B(w)$ at a fixed point $w>0$.
  We propose an estimator $B_n(w)$ based solely on the observed workload trajectory. The construction relies on a screening mechanism that extracts conditionally i.i.d. compound Poisson increments from the workload process, thereby reducing the dependent-data problem to a Laplace-transform inversion framework.
  Under mild regularity assumptions on $B(\cdot)$, i.e., continuous differentiability on $[0,\infty)$, twice differentiability at $w$, and a finite second moment, we establish the bound \[ \mathbb{E}\bigl|B_n(w)-B(w)\bigr| =\mathcal{O}\!\left(\frac{\log n}{\sqrt{n}}\right), \qquad n\to\infty. \]This provides the first solution to the Hansen-Pitts problem achieving a parametric $L^1$-risk rate (up to a logarithmic factor), without requiring stationarity, stability, or knowledge of the arrival rate.

</details>


### [154] [Functional dependence and synchronous coupling in ergodic autoregressions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08567)
*Paul Doukhan,Lionel Truquet*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文指出，非线性时间序列分析中常用的函数依赖度量，其概率空间可能不同于模型的自然动力学概率空间。对于一致遍历的自回归过程，当模型参数变化时，自然创新耦合的行为会发生质变，且该转变与李雅普诺夫指数符号变化一致。正李雅普诺夫指数可能阻止相同创新驱动下轨迹对初始扰动的遗忘，尽管马尔可夫链是一致遍历的。这强调了指定概率空间的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 指出函数依赖度量在非线性时间序列分析中通常基于给定的创新表示构建，但其概率空间可能与模型的自然动力学概率空间不一致，从而影响度量解释和应用的有效性。

Method: 构建一致遍历的自回归过程模型族，分析自然创新耦合在不同参数下的行为变化，并将该变化与李雅普诺夫指数符号联系起来，揭示正指数对初始扰动遗忘的阻碍作用。

Result: 发现均匀遍历自回归过程的自然创新耦合行为随模型参数变化发生质变，该转变与李雅普诺夫指数符号变化一致；正李雅普诺夫指数阻止相同创新驱动下轨迹对初始扰动的遗忘。

Conclusion: 在解释或应用函数依赖度量时，必须仔细指定底层概率空间，因为自然动力学概率空间与创新表示概率空间可能不同，且正李雅普诺夫指数会破坏创新耦合的遗忘性质。

Abstract: Functional dependence measures have become an important tool in the analysis of nonlinear time series and are typically formulated with respect to a given innovation representation of the process. This note points out that the probability space on which such representations yield the expected memory loss properties may not always coincide with the natural dynamical probability space of the model. We exhibit classes of uniformly ergodic autoregressive processes for which the behavior of the natural innovation coupling undergoes a qualitative transition as the model parameter varies. For this family of models, this transition coincides with a change in the sign of an associated Lyapunov exponent. In particular, a positive Lyapunov exponent may prevent the forgetting of initial perturbations along trajectories driven by the same innovations, despite uniform ergodicity of the associated Markov chain. These observations highlight the importance of carefully specifying the underlying probability space when interpreting or applying functional dependence measures.

</details>


### [155] [Exact Permutation Recovery Under Unknown Scalar Affine Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08628)
*Tigran Galstyan,Avetik Karagulyan,Arshak Minasyan*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文研究当两组特征向量之间存在未知标量仿射变换时的特征匹配问题，提出先标准化估计变换参数，再通过最小化对数和的（LSL）方法估计排列。理论表明在特征向量最小分离距离满足特定条件下可完美恢复排列，该条件在温和异方差下与非仿射设置一致，但存在需要更大分离距离的困难配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两组存在未知标量仿射变换和异方差噪声的特征向量之间的匹配问题。

Method: 两步法：先标准化特征向量以估计未知仿射变换参数，再利用估计的变换通过最小化对数和方法（LSL）估计排列。

Result: 完美恢复排列所需的最小分离距离条件为 $\sqrt{ρ_σ} \vee (d\log n)^{1/4} \vee \sqrt{\log n}$；在温和异方差下与非仿射设置速率一致；但存在需要更大分离距离的配置，表明该问题从极小极大视角比非仿射设置更具挑战性。

Conclusion: 标准化数据可隐式估计仿射参数；证明了异方差噪声下仿射参数估计器的非渐近集中界。

Abstract: We study the problem of matching two sets of noisy feature vectors when underlying true features are related by an unknown scalar affine transformation. Our method comprises two primary steps. First, we standardize the feature vectors to estimate the unknown scalar affine transformation. Subsequently, we estimate the permutation by minimizing the Least Sum of Logarithms (LSL) between two sets of observations using the estimated transformation.
  Our main result shows that the unknown permutation can be perfectly recovered given that the minimal separation distance of true feature vectors scales as $\sqrt{ρ_σ} \vee (d\log n)^{1/4} \vee \sqrt{\log n}$, where $d$ is the ambient dimension, $n$ is the sample size, and $ρ_σ$ is the maximal ratio of noise magnitudes. Interestingly, the obtained rate, under mild heteroscedasticity, coincides with that of the non-affine setting. We additionally demonstrate that there exist configurations requiring a larger minimal separation distance for perfect recovery. The latter makes the matching problem more challenging from minimax perspective compared to the non-affine setting.
  Consequently, we show that in the problem of feature matching, standardizing the data implicitly estimates the scalar affine parameters. As part of our analysis, we prove non-asymptotic concentration bounds for the affine parameter estimators in the presence of heterogeneous noise magnitudes.

</details>


### [156] [Low-Rank Matrix Recovery via Heavy-Tailed Quadratic Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08671)
*Gao Huang,Song Li*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明，在采样向量条目仅具有有限 $4+\delta$ 阶矩的弱假设下，核范数最小化和半定约束经验风险最小化两种凸方法能够实现低秩埃尔米特矩阵的均匀、稳定且鲁棒的重建，且样本复杂度达到最优 $m = \mathcal{O}(rn)$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有低秩矩阵恢复理论大多假设采样向量服从高斯或次高斯分布，这在实际应用中往往不成立。许多实际场景中采样向量呈现重尾分布，但该领域的理论理解仍然匮乏。本文旨在弥合这一理论空白。

Method: 利用解耦技术建立二次型的矩估计，并结合重尾协方差估计的最新进展，分析核范数最小化和半定约束经验风险最小化两种凸方法。

Result: 在采样向量条目仅具有有限 $4+\delta$ 阶矩的弱假设下，两种凸方法均实现了均匀、稳定且鲁棒的重建，样本复杂度为最优的 $m = \mathcal{O}(rn)$（依赖于矩常数）。此外，还建立了复投影4-设计采样下的最优样本复杂度，改进了先前结果，并在弱矩假设下获得了相位检索的稳定性保证。

Conclusion: 本文成功将低秩矩阵恢复的理论保证从高斯/次高斯假设推广到更实际的重尾分布场景，为相位检索等应用提供了更坚实的理论基础。

Abstract: The problem of recovering an (approximately) low-rank Hermitian matrix $\pmb{M}_0 \in \mathbb{C}^{n \times n}$ of rank $r$ from quadratic sampling matrices of the form $\{\pmb{a}_k \pmb{a}_k^*\}_{k=1}^m$ arises in a variety of applications, including phase retrieval. To obtain rigorous recovery guarantees, the sampling vectors $\{\pmb{a}_k\}_{k=1}^m$ are typically modeled probabilistically. However, most existing theoretical results rely on Gaussian or sub-Gaussian assumptions, which may not accurately capture practical data models. In many applications, sampling vectors exhibit heavier tails, while theoretical understanding in such regimes remains scarce.
  In this paper, we bridge this gap. We show that two widely used convex approaches, nuclear norm minimization and semidefinite-constrained empirical risk minimization, achieve uniform, stable, and robust recovery under the mild assumption that the entries of the sampling vectors have only finite $4+δ$ moments, with the optimal sample complexity $m = \mathcal{O}(rn)$ up to moment-dependent constants. The two main ingredients of our analysis are moment estimates for quadratic forms established via decoupling, together with recent advances in covariance estimation in heavy-tailed settings. As byproducts, we also establish the optimal sample complexity for low-rank matrix recovery under complex projective $4$-design sampling, thereby improving upon previous results, and obtain stability guarantees for phase retrieval under similarly weak moment assumptions.

</details>


### [157] [Statistical inverse learning problems with random observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2312.15341)
*Abhishake Rastogi,Tapio Helin,Nicole Mücke*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文综述了随机实验设计下统计逆问题的最新进展，涵盖线性和非线性问题，讨论了谱正则化、投影正则化及凸惩罚正则化等方法，在希尔伯特尺度下分析了收敛速度与极小极大最优性，并应用于药代动力学/药效学(PK/PD)模型中的药物浓度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统计逆问题在随机实验设计下具有重要的理论与实际意义，需系统梳理正则化方法的最新进展，特别是在收敛速度与极小极大最优性方面的理论成果，并探索其在PK/PD等实际模型中的应用。

Method: 综述了三种正则化方案：谱正则化方法、投影正则化（在希尔伯特尺度框架下提供新见解）以及凸惩罚正则化。利用再生核希尔伯特空间(RKHS)结构建立统计学习框架，在期望和概率意义下分析极小极大收敛速度。

Result: 在概率意义上建立了样本量的收敛速度，导出了期望和概率两方面的极小极大最优速率。在投影正则化方面提供了新的理论见解，并阐明了证明所需的关键假设条件。

Conclusion: 该综述系统总结了随机实验设计下统计逆问题的正则化理论进展，并将这些理论概念成功应用于非线性PK/PD模型中的药物浓度变化预测任务。

Abstract: We provide an overview of recent progress in statistical inverse problems with random experimental design, covering both linear and nonlinear inverse problems. Different regularization schemes have been studied to produce robust and stable solutions. We discuss recent results in spectral regularization methods and regularization by projection, exploring both approaches within the context of Hilbert scales and presenting new insights particularly in regularization by projection. Additionally, we overview recent advancements in regularization using convex penalties. Convergence rates are analyzed in terms of the sample size in a probabilistic sense, yielding minimax rates in both expectation and probability. To achieve these results, the structure of reproducing kernel Hilbert spaces is leveraged to establish minimax rates in the statistical learning setting. We detail the assumptions underpinning these key elements of our proofs. Finally, we demonstrate the application of these concepts to nonlinear inverse problems in pharmacokinetic/pharmacodynamic (PK/PD) models, where the task is to predict changes in drug concentrations in patients.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [158] [LiST: Lipschitz Scaling Training for Robust and Calibrated Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07745)
*Arthur Chiron,Franck Mamalet,Thomas Massena,Thomas Deltort,Mathieu Serrurier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST)，通过将Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）建立理论联系，实现了在精度-鲁棒性-校准度三个指标上同时优化的训练范式。LiST能够自动找到非平凡的最优Lipschitz常数L*，使得网络天然具备良好校准性，并通过边际参数构建完全校准的Pareto前沿，在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常分别处理精度、鲁棒性和校准度这三个指标，缺乏同时优化三者的方法。Lipschitz约束模型虽能保证鲁棒性，但Lipschitz常数L需手动选择，且其校准特性未被充分研究。

Method: 1. 理论上建立Lipschitz约束与温度缩放（Temperature Scaling）之间的关联，发现存在最优L*使网络天然校准。2. 提出LiST训练范式，迭代调整全局Lipschitz常数以到达校准状态下的精度-鲁棒性Pareto最优。3. 通过训练损失中的边际参数构建完全校准的Pareto前沿。4. 收敛后将校准数据重新整合到训练中，提升样本效率。

Result: 在CIFAR-10/100和Tiny-ImageNet上，LiST在有约束和无约束的基线方法中均取得了具有竞争力的精度和鲁棒性，同时实现了开箱即用的良好校准。

Conclusion: 校准度可以作为选择精度-鲁棒性Pareto前沿上明确定义操作点的原则性准则，LiST成功实现了精度、鲁棒性和校准度的联合优化。

Abstract: While accuracy, robustness, and calibration are all essential for reliable neural networks, they are often studied separately; developing models that satisfy all three simultaneously remains a central challenge. Lipschitz-constrained models guarantee robustness by design, yet the manual selection of the Lipschitz constraint L governs the resulting accuracy-robustness trade-off, and their calibration properties remain largely underexplored. In this work, we highlight a theoretical and empirical link between the enforced Lipschitz constraint and Temperature Scaling, a state-of-the-art calibration method. Specifically, we find that for a given training scheme, there exists a non-trivial value L* that yields an out-of-the-box calibrated network, and that calibration acts as a principled criterion to select a well-defined operating point on the accuracy-robustness Pareto front. Leveraging these insights, we introduce Lipschitz Scaling Training (LiST), a novel training paradigm that iteratively adjusts the global Lipschitz constant to reach this operating point. Through a margin parameter in the training loss, LiST further enables the construction of a fully calibrated Pareto front, allowing users to navigate the accuracy-robustness trade-off while remaining calibrated throughout. At convergence, LiST also enables the reintegration of calibration data into training, improving sample efficiency without sacrificing calibration. We validate LiST on CIFAR-10/100 and Tiny-ImageNet, demonstrating competitive accuracy and robustness against constrained and unconstrained baselines, while remaining calibrated out of the box. Code is available at GitHub.

</details>


### [159] [A law of robustness for two-layer neural networks with arbitrary weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07778)
*Yitzchak Shmalo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了Bubeck-Li-Nagaraj关于两层神经网络拟合噪声标签时Lipschitz常数下界的猜想（除一个对数因子外），适用于连续分段线性激活函数（如ReLU）。关键在于用函数空间覆盖替代参数空间覆盖，并提出了一个刚性引理：在d≥3时，每个规范kink的系数被Lipschitz常数控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 验证Bubeck-Li-Nagaraj的猜想：对于两层神经网络，拟合n个带噪标签所需的Lipschitz常数至少为Ω(√(n/m))，且不限制权重大小。此前结果仅限于有界参数情形，无界权重情形需要全新论证。

Method: 1) 提出刚性引理：在B₂球和d≥3的球面上，规范kink的系数被Lipschitz常数控制，因为不同超平面上的kink在一般点上无法相互抵消。2) 用函数空间覆盖代替参数空间覆盖来解决权重无界的问题。3) 在d=2时刚性引理失效，构造显式两层ReLU插值器在宽度2n时仅需O(1) Lipschitz常数，与猜想在过参数化端点匹配。

Result: 对任何连续分段线性激活函数（特别是ReLU），在d≥3时，均匀球面或高斯数据下，拟合噪声标签时Lipschitz常数下界为c ε √(n/(m̃ log(C m̃ n d/ε)))，其中m̃=(K-1)m+1。此外，对任何实际实现的两层分段线性函数，若kink超平面数k(f)≤n，则下界中m̃可替换为k(f)+1。

Conclusion: 证实了Bubeck-Li-Nagaraj猜想（差一个对数因子），揭示了神经网络记忆噪声时Lipschitz常数与数据量-宽度比之间的基本权衡，并展示了d=2时的反常行为。

Abstract: Bubeck, Li and Nagaraj conjectured that, for generic data, any two-layer neural network with $m$ neurons that fits $n$ noisy labels must have Lipschitz constant at least of order $\sqrt{n/m}$, with no restriction on the size of the weights. Bubeck and Sellke proved a universal version of this law for Lipschitz-parameterized classes, but under a polynomial bound on the parameters; at depth three that boundedness hypothesis is genuinely necessary. The two-layer unbounded-weight case requires a different argument. We prove the conjectured law, up to one logarithmic factor, for every continuous piecewise-linear activation, in particular for ReLU networks. For data drawn uniformly from $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$, $d\ge3$, or from $N(0,I_d/d)$, labels in $[-1,1]$ with noise level $σ^2>0$, and any width-$m$ two-layer network with arbitrary real weights, biases and affine skip connection, fitting the data $\varepsilon$ below the noise floor forces $\mathrm{Lip}(f)\ge c\,\varepsilon\sqrt{n/(\bar m\log(C\bar m nd/\varepsilon))}$, $\bar m=(K-1)m+1$, with high probability. A realized-kink-count version holds on the same event: every realized two-layer piecewise-linear function with $k(f)\le n$ distinct kink hyperplanes obeys the bound with $\bar m$ replaced by $k(f)+1$, irrespective of how many redundant hidden units parameterize it. The proof replaces parameter-space covering, impossible for unbounded weights, by a function-space covering. The central deterministic ingredient is a rigidity lemma: on $B_2$, and on $\mathbb{S}^{d-1}$ for $d\ge3$, the coefficient of each canonical kink is controlled by the Lipschitz constant of the realized function, because kinks on distinct hyperplanes cannot cancel at generic points. Rigidity genuinely fails at $d=2$, and an explicit two-layer ReLU interpolant with $O(1)$ Lipschitz constant at width $2n$ matches the law at the overparameterized endpoint.

</details>


### [160] [Reinforcing the Generation Order of Multimodal Masked Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08056)
*Yidong Ouyang,Zhe Wang,Sourav Bhabesh,Dmitriy Bespalov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于GRPO的可学习生成顺序控制模块，用于优化扩散语言模型在文本到图像生成和多模态理解中的生成顺序，在GenEval和VLMEvalKit上分别取得4.08%和4.85%的相对提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明自适应生成顺序能提升数学推理和代码生成任务的性能，但在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务中，模型logits本身不足以确定最优生成顺序，因此需要引入可学习的控制机制。

Method: 通过GRPO（组相对策略优化）训练一个可学习的控制模块，用于确定扩散语言模型在文本到图像生成和多模态理解任务中的token生成顺序。

Result: 在GenEval基准上实现4.08%相对提升，在VLMEvalKit上实现4.85%相对提升，有效增强了模型对细粒度空间关系的捕捉能力和多模态推理理解能力。

Conclusion: 可学习的生成顺序控制能够显著改善扩散语言模型在文本到图像对齐和多模态理解方面的性能，证明了该方法在多个任务上的广泛有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have recently achieved substantial progress in natural language generation tasks. Recent research demonstrates that adaptive token generation ordering can significantly improve performance in mathematical reasoning and code synthesis applications. In this work, we investigate the optimization of generation order for both text-to-image synthesis and multimodal understanding. We first establish that, unlike structured problems in language generation such as Sudoku puzzles, model logits alone are insufficient for determining optimal generation sequences in text-to-image generation and multimodal understanding. To address this challenge, we introduce a learnable control module trained via Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) to determine the generation order. Our results demonstrate that learning this control block substantially improves both text-to-image alignment and multimodal understanding in DLMs. In particular, it enhances the model's ability to capture fine-grained spatial relationships in generated images while also strengthening performance on multimodal reasoning and comprehension tasks. We evaluate our framework on GenEval, an object-focused benchmark for text-to-image alignment, where it achieves 4.08% relative improvements. In addition, experiments on VLMEvalKit confirm 4.85% relative improvements in multimodal understanding, highlighting the broad effectiveness of our approach.

</details>


### [161] [Structure Learning on Clustered Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08238)
*Ryan Thompson,Matt P. Wand,Veerabhadran Baladandayuthapani*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种融合固定效应与随机效应的混合模型框架，用于从聚类数据中估计全局有向无环图结构，同时捕捉局部聚类层面的变异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DAG结构学习方法假设总体完全同质，无法处理存在聚类特异效应（如患者个体效应）的聚类数据，因此需要一种能在全局结构与局部聚类效应之间取得平衡的新方法。

Method: 将经典混合模型的固定-随机效应框架扩展到结构学习；设计了一种可微图耦合机制，确保固定效应图与随机效应图的并集保持无环性；提出了可证明收敛的一阶优化方法，并利用跨聚类的批量更新提升效率。

Result: 在真实与合成数据实验中，该方法能够检测到其他替代估计器遗漏的依赖关系，验证了其在聚类场景下结构学习的价值。

Conclusion: 该模型具有统计可辨识性，且能渐近地恢复真实结构，为异质性群体中的因果发现提供了有效工具。

Abstract: Recent algorithmic advances have made directed acyclic graph (DAG) structure learning scalable for causal discovery. Yet, the currently available techniques assume a completely homogeneous population, precluding their application to clustered data where cluster-specific variations (e.g., patient-specific effects) are common. We address this issue by introducing a new approach that estimates a global structure while accounting for local cluster-level effects. The key idea is to extend the fixed- and random-effects framework of classical mixed models to the structure learning setting. Towards this end, we present a differentiable graph coupling mechanism that guarantees the union of the fixed- and random-effects graphs remains acyclic. Computationally, we provide a provably convergent first-order method and leverage efficient batched updates across clusters. Statistically, we establish identifiability of the model and show that our approach recovers the true structure asymptotically. In experiments on real and synthetic data, our proposal detects dependencies missed by alternative estimators, underscoring its value for structure learning in clustered settings.

</details>


### [162] [An interpretable Good--Turing restart criterion for k-means++](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08243)
*Renato Cordeiro de Amorim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GTRC（Good-Turing重启准则），基于Good-Turing估计、无条件界和置信界来动态决定k-means++算法是否还需重启，在36个数据集上实现了与固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，但重启次数根据数据难度自适应调整，避免了固定重启数的随意性和计算浪费。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: k-means++算法通常被多次重启以避免陷入局部最优，但重启次数几乎总是任意选择且对所有数据集统一使用，忽视了数据集的难度差异。这种做法既在简单数据集上浪费计算资源，又在困难数据集上可能重启不足，且导致依赖此参数的比较结果不可靠。

Method: 提出了GTRC（Good-Turing重启准则），该准则结合了三个关键组件：（1）Good-Turing估计，用于估计未观测到的聚类质量改进的概率；（2）一个经过证明的无条件界；（3）一个基于置信度的界，用于评估再次重启能改善当前结果的概率。当该概率低于用户指定的容忍度ε时，算法停止重启。

Result: 在36个数据集上的实验表明，GTRC达到了与精心选择的固定重启次数相当的聚类质量，同时重启次数根据数据集难度自适应变化，由可解释的数据依赖信号而非固定规则控制。

Conclusion: GTRC为预先固定k-means++重启次数提供了一种有原则的、可报告的替代方案，使得重启决策由数据驱动而非任意选择，提高了计算效率和比较的可靠性。

Abstract: The k-means++ algorithm is commonly restarted multiple times to avoid poor local optima, yet the number of restarts is almost always chosen arbitrarily and applied uniformly regardless of data set difficulty. This undermines any comparison relying on such a choice and wastes computation on easy data sets while potentially under-serving hard ones. We introduce GTRC, a restart criterion combining a Good-Turing estimate, a proven unconditional bound, and a confidence-based bound on the probability that a further restart would improve on the current result, stopping once this probability falls below a user-specified tolerance $\varepsilon$. Across 36 data sets, GTRC reached clustering quality competitive with well-chosen fixed restart counts, while the number of restarts used varied considerably and appropriately with data set difficulty, governed by an interpretable, data-dependent signal rather than a fixed rule. GTRC offers a principled and reportable alternative to fixing the number of $k$-means++ restarts in advance. Software:https://github.com/RCdeAmorim/Good-Turing-Restart-Criterion.

</details>


### [163] [AutoAnchor: Stable Diffusion Unlearning Using Cross-Attention as a Manifold Surrogate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08337)
*Siyuan Wen,Jiahao Zeng,Ningning Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对文本到图像扩散模型中的遗忘学习（unlearning）问题，提出了一种两阶段框架MYSYS，通过自动合成流形近端锚点（manifold-proximal anchors）实现稳定、鲁棒且无偏的遗忘学习。理论证明了缺乏流形近端锚点会导致法向空间漂移，进而降低遗忘性能。实验表明MYSYS在概念移除和非目标实用性上均有显著提升，并能轻松集成到现有方法中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散遗忘学习方法存在两大问题：基于锚点的方法依赖人工选择的语义锚点，存在偏差风险；无锚点方法因潜在更新不受约束而鲁棒性差。作者从流形假设出发，理论证明缺乏流形近端锚点会导致法向空间漂移，从而降低遗忘性能。因此需要一种自动、高效合成流形近端锚点的方法来实现稳定遗忘。

Method: 提出两阶段框架MYSYS：第一阶段自动合成流形近端锚点；第二阶段利用这些锚点进行遗忘学习。为解决直接几何流形优化计算不可行的问题，引入交叉注意力一致性损失（cross-attention consistency loss）作为流形近端的高效代理。该损失通过约束模型在锚点上的注意力分布，近似实现流形近端约束。

Result: 实验结果表明MYSYS在多种先进基线方法上实现了鲁棒且无偏的遗忘学习：目标概念移除提升高达31.04%（CLIP分数），非目标实用性提升高达4.18%（CLIP分数）。此外，MYSYS可轻松集成到现有扩散遗忘方法中，平均提升概念移除6.30%、实用性6.65%。

Conclusion: 本文从理论角度揭示了扩散遗忘学习中不稳定性的根源（法向空间漂移），并提出了一种自动合成流形近端锚点的实用框架MYSYS，通过交叉注意力一致性损失高效逼近流形近端约束，实现了更稳定、鲁棒且无偏的遗忘学习。该框架具有很好的通用性和可集成性。

Abstract: Diffusion unlearning is essential for mitigating the generation of harmful or copyrighted content in text-to-image models. Current diffusion unlearning techniques determine the model update direction by either using alternatives of the target concept as an anchor or using empty prompts. The anchor-based method relies on manually and semantically-chosen anchors that risk biased unlearning, while the anchor-free method inherently suffers from unrobust unlearning due to unconstrained latent updates. In this work, we theoretically formalize such unstable diffusion unlearning issues under the manifold hypothesis and prove that lacking a manifold-proximal anchor inevitably induces significant normal-space drift that degrades unlearning performance. To achieve stable unlearning, we propose \mysysn, a two-stage framework that automatically synthesizes manifold-proximal anchors. However, direct geometric manifold optimization is computationally intractable. To address this challenge, \mysys introduces a novel cross-attention consistency loss which serves as a highly efficient surrogate of manifold proximity. Experimental results demonstrate that \mysys effectively achieves robust and unbiased unlearning across various state-of-the-art baselines, significantly improving targeted concept removal (by up to 31.04\% in CLIP score) and non-target utility (by up to 4.18\% in CLIP score). Moreover, \mysys can also be easily integrated into existing diffusion unlearning methods to enhance their unlearning performance (by 6.30\% for concept removal and 6.65\% for utility on average).

</details>


### [164] [Beyond Backpropagation: Monte Carlo Method Can Train Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08406)
*Hong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文证明了一种最简单的蒙特卡洛算法——随机突变一个参数，若损失下降则保留，否则重试——可以在单GPU上有效训练深层网络，无需梯度、批归一化或残差连接，甚至支持纯剪枝训练、离散权重、高斯激活函数等非传统场景，并在20层以上深度网络、宽网络和简单Transformer上验证了可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向传播依赖梯度，存在梯度消失和爆炸等固有问题，长期以来无梯度方法一直是人工智能领域追求的目标。

Method: 最简单的蒙特卡洛算法：随机突变一个参数，若损失下降则保留该突变，否则重试。无需批归一化或残差连接等常见技术。

Result: 该方法在20层以上的深度网络、隐藏神经元达16,384个的单隐层宽网络以及简单的Transformer架构（MNIST图像分类和Tiny Shakespeare字符级语言建模）上均验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 这种简单的无梯度方法为理解神经网络的自组织和学习机制提供了互补视角，也为构建物理启发的深度学习系统提供了替代路径。

Abstract: Backpropagation (BP) dominates deep learning training, but its reliance on gradients brings inherent troubles -- vanishing and exploding gradients. The pursuit of gradient-free methods has long been a goal in the field of artificial intelligence. This paper shows that indeed the simplest Monte Carlo algorithm implemented on a single GPU -- randomly mutate a parameter, keep it if the loss decreases, otherwise retry -- can practically train deep networks. This gradient-free method does not even need common techniques such as batch normalization or residual connections to directly train sufficiently deep networks. More remarkably, its flexibility extends to several nontrivial scenarios: it enables pure pruning training, supports discrete weights, accommodates unconventional transfer functions such as Gaussian, and reveals the substantial redundancy of deep networks. We have demonstrated its feasibility on deep networks with more than 20 layers, single-hidden-layer wide networks with up to 16,384 hidden neurons, and even a simple Transformer architecture trained on both image classification (MNIST) and character-level language modeling (Tiny Shakespeare). This simple gradient-free method may offer a complementary perspective for understanding the self-organization and learning mechanisms of neural networks, and also provides an alternative route for building physically inspired deep learning systems.

</details>


### [165] [Multimodal Unlearning Across Vision, Language, Video, and Audio: Survey of Methods, Datasets, and Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07907)
*Nobin Sarwar,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Zheyuan Liu,Vaidehi Patil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了多模态遗忘（Multimodal Unlearning）领域，旨在从视觉、语言、音频和视频等模态中选择性移除敏感、偏见或版权信息，同时保持模型整体性能。文章提出了统一的系统化视角，构建了分类体系，并分析了删除强度、保留性、效率、可逆性和鲁棒性之间的权衡，最后指出了开放问题与实践方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态基础模型（如VLM、DM、LLM、AFM）在训练数据中可能编码敏感、版权、偏见或不安全的跨模态关联，而重新训练成本过高，针对性遗忘因知识分布在共享表示中而困难，因此需要多模态遗忘技术来选择性移除特定知识。

Method: 采用综述方法，提出统一的系统化视角，构建分类体系（taxonomy），对不同模型架构和模态进行系统性比较，分析删除强度、保留性、效率、可逆性和鲁棒性等关键指标的权衡关系。

Result: 提供了一个涵盖视觉、语言、音频和视频的多模态遗忘综合综述，建立了分类体系以支持跨架构和跨模态的系统比较，并发布了相关资源仓库。

Conclusion: 多模态遗忘是解决数据删除请求和政策更新的有效途径，但仍面临开放问题和实际部署挑战，需要未来进一步研究。

Abstract: With the growing adoption of VLMs, DMs, LLMs, and AFMs, these multimodal foundation models can inadvertently encode sensitive, copyrighted, biased, or unsafe cross-modal associations that originate from their training data. Retraining after deletion requests or policy updates is often impractical, and targeted forgetting remains difficult because knowledge is distributed across shared representations. Multimodal unlearning addresses this challenge by enabling selective removal across modalities while retaining overall utility. This survey offers a unified, system-oriented view of multimodal unlearning across vision, language, audio, and video, grounded in recent advances, emerging applications, and open problems. Our taxonomy enables systematic comparison across model architectures and modalities, clarifying trade-offs among deletion strength, retention, efficiency, reversibility, and robustness. This survey highlights open problems and practical considerations to support future research and deployment of multimodal unlearning. We release a curated repository: https://smsnobin77.github.io/Awesome-Multimodal-Unlearning/

</details>


### [166] [Efficient Safety Alignment of Language Models via Latent Personality Traits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07918)
*Mohamed Amine Merzouk,Nolan Smyth,Damiano Fornasiere,Linh Le,David Williams-King,Adam Oberman*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在个性对齐（LPA），仅用66条个性相关无害语句进行对抗训练，无需有害数据即可实现接近零的攻击成功率，且训练速度极快、不损害模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全方法易受对抗攻击，潜在对抗训练（LAT）虽有效但会降低模型性能且需要大量有害提示数据进行训练，因此需要更高效、无损性能的鲁棒防御方法。

Method: 提出潜在个性对齐（LPA），利用来自心理测量个性文献的66条与伤害无关的陈述进行对抗训练，通过稳定个性锚定的表示来隐式约束越狱攻击可利用的子空间，从而替代显式的有害内容拒绝机制。

Result: 在HarmBench上，LPA在直接请求和五种越狱方法下均达到接近零的攻击成功率；在标准基准测试上无性能损失；训练仅需单GPU数分钟，且使用的示例数量比标准LAT少75倍。

Conclusion: LPA是一种高效、鲁棒且可推广的安全对齐方法，证明了个性锚定表示与伤害回避之间存在共享的潜在结构，为无需有害数据的模型安全提供了新路径。

Abstract: Current safety methods for large language models are known to be vulnerable to adversarial attacks, motivating research into robust alternatives. Latent Adversarial Training (LAT) is among the most effective defenses, but can degrade utility and requires training on large datasets of harmful prompts. We introduce Latent Personality Alignment (LPA), which replaces explicit harm refusal with adversarial training on just 66 harm-agnostic statements drawn from psychometric personality literature. We hypothesize that personality-anchored representations share latent structure with harm avoidance, so adversarially stabilizing them implicitly constrains the subspace exploited by jailbreak attacks. LPA achieves near-zero attack success rates on HarmBench across direct requests and five jailbreak methods, despite never seeing harmful content during training and no loss of performance on standard benchmarks. Moreover, the training process is lightweight; the entire procedure completes in minutes on a single GPU and uses 75x fewer examples than standard LAT. Extensive ablations demonstrate the robustness, efficiency, and generalization of our method.

</details>


### [167] [Towards the Explainability of Temporal Graph Networks via Memory Backtracking and Topological Attribution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07716)
*Yazheng Liu,Xi Zhang,Sihong Xie,Hui Xiong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MemExplainer方法，通过拓扑归因树和记忆回溯树为TGN预测提供忠实解释，在9个时序图数据集上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时序图神经网络解释方法忽略了记忆模块（记录和更新节点历史的核心组件），导致历史事件对模型预测的影响未被充分探索。

Method: 1) 通过拓扑归因树捕获邻居及其记忆向量的影响；2) 通过记忆回溯树量化历史事件如何塑造节点记忆向量；3) 在TGN中应用LRP确保事件总贡献等于模型logits；4) 设计优化目标解决从logits到概率的非线性映射导致top-k选择不忠实的问题。

Result: 在9个时序图数据集（涵盖节点属性预测、链接预测和图分类任务）上，该方法提供了忠实的解释，并超越了现有最先进的基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过显式建模记忆模块的贡献，MemExplainer能够更忠实地解释TGN预测，且代码已开源。

Abstract: Temporal graphs are ubiquitous in real-world applications and Temporal Graph Networks (TGNs) have achieved superior predictive accuracy. Understanding which historical events drive model predictions can enhance trustworthiness of TGNs. Existing explanation methods overlook the memory module, the core component that records and updates node histories, leaving the influence of past events unexplored. To address this, we attribute TGNs predictions through the topology attribution tree and memory backtracking tree. The topology attribution tree captures the influence of neighbors and their memory vectors, then the memory backtracking tree quantifies how historical events shape node memory vectors. We apply the LRP in TGNs, ensuring that the total contribution of events equals the logits of model. Finally, top-k selection may be unfaithful due to the nonlinear mapping from logits to probabilities, we design optimization objectives to identify the important events. Experiments on nine temporal graph datasets, spanning node property prediction, link prediction tasks and graph classification tasks, show that our method provides faithful explanations and outperforms state-of-the-art baselines. The code is available at https://github.com/yazhengliu/MemExplainer

</details>


### [168] [Who Gets Missed in the Tail? Thresholded Subgroup Underdiagnosis in Long-Tailed Chest X-ray Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07717)
*Ha-Hieu Pham,Hai-Dang Nguyen,Dang P. M. Cao,Thanh-Huy Nguyen,Min Xu,Trung-Nghia Le,Ulas Bagci,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在胸部X光分类中，长尾多标签模型从分数转换为决策后，稀有阳性患者仍可能低于阈值，尤其是子群体中。通过诊断阶梯方法（类别级长尾损失、子群感知加权、群体鲁棒性和阈值选择），结合组尾加权和尾感知阈值化，可显著降低尾部FNR和最差群体FNR，但残余漏诊率仍然较高。研究支持一个狭窄的审计主张：稀有标签公平性取决于发现、子群体和操作阈值的联合作用，而非仅标签频率或排序指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究胸部X光分类模型中，即使排序性能可接受，稀有阳性患者（尤其是子群体中）仍可能被漏诊的预部署公平性问题。

Method: 采用诊断阶梯方法，依次应用类别级长尾损失、子群感知加权、群体鲁棒性（GroupDRO）和阈值选择，并在VinDr-CXR和MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT数据集上进行实验对比。

Result: 在VinDr-CXR上，组尾加权+尾感知阈值化将尾部FNR从0.665降至0.269，性别最差群体FNR从0.705降至0.157，年龄最差群体FNR从0.822降至0.133，宏mAP从0.611提升至0.635。在MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT上，尾部FNR从0.866降至0.741，各子群体最差FNR均降低，但残余漏诊率仍高。配对Bootstrap验证支持阈值化FNR的改善，GroupDRO参照实验表明聚合群体鲁棒性不足消除稀有子群体漏诊。

Conclusion: 稀有标签公平性在胸部X光分类中是一个依赖于发现类别、子群体和操作阈值的联合问题，不能仅靠标签频率或排序指标来解决。

Abstract: In chest X-ray (CXR) classification, acceptable ranking performance can still leave rare-positive patients below threshold, especially within subgroups. We study this pre-deployment fairness problem as an audit question: after a long-tailed multi-label CXR model is converted from scores into decisions, who is missed? Across VinDr-CXR and MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, we use a diagnostic ladder to separate class-level long-tail losses, subgroup-aware weighting, group robustness, and threshold selection. On VinDr-CXR, group-tail weighting followed by tail-aware thresholding reduces tail FNR from 0.665 to 0.269, sex worst-group FNR from 0.705 to 0.157, and age worst-group FNR from 0.822 to 0.133, while macro-mAP increases from 0.611 to 0.635. On MIMIC-CXR/CXR-LT, the same score-to-threshold comparison reduces tail FNR from 0.866 to 0.741 and lowers worst-group FNR across sex, age, race, and insurance; residual missed-positive rates nevertheless remain high. Paired bootstrap contrasts on VinDr support the thresholded FNR reductions, and GroupDRO reference runs indicate that aggregate group robustness alone does not remove rare subgroup misses in this setting. The study supports a narrow audit claim: rare-label fairness in CXR depends jointly on the finding, subgroup, and operating threshold, not on label frequency or ranking metrics alone.

</details>


### [169] [LLT: Local Linear Transformer for PDE Operator Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07718)
*Oded Ovadia,Eli Turkel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Local Linear Transformer (LLT)，一种结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合的 Transformer 架构，用于偏微分方程算子学习。在多个 PDE 问题（弹性、塑性、翼型流、管道流、达西流）及三维汽车空气动力学数据集上，LLT 在精度上与现有方法持平或更优，并在结构化网格上训练速度比 Transolver 提升 1.8~2.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准注意力机制在 PDE 应用中存在两个主要局限：计算复杂度随节点数二次增长，且缺乏对局部交互的显式偏置。为克服这些问题，作者提出 LLT。

Method: LLT 结合线性全局注意力与局部空间混合模块，并融入坐标与几何信息。架构设计兼顾全局依赖建模与局部交互偏置。

Result: 在弹性、塑性、翼型流、管道流、达西流等多个 PDE 问题上，LLT 达到与现有神经算子和 Transformer 基线方法竞争或更低的相对 L2 误差。在结构化离散化上，每个训练迭代的 wall-clock 时间相比 Transolver 减少 1.8~2.5 倍。在含 32,186 个非结构化网格点的三维汽车空气动力学数据集上也验证了可扩展性。

Conclusion: LLT 提供了一种准确且计算高效的 PDE 算子，适用于不同离散化方式、网格类型和问题设置。

Abstract: Neural operators have become a common approach for learning PDE solution maps and accelerating numerical simulations. Transformer-based neural operators are of particular interest, since attention can learn long-range dependencies in the computational domain. However, standard attention has two major limitations when applied to PDEs: it scales quadratically with the number of computational nodes, and it lacks an explicit bias toward local interactions. To address these issues, we introduce Local Linear Transformer (LLT) for PDE operator learning. The architecture combines linear global attention with local spatial mixing, and incorporates coordinate and geometry information. We evaluate LLT on several PDE problems, including elasticity, plasticity, airfoil flow, pipe flow, and Darcy flow. The reference data for these problems span finite-element, finite-volume, and finite-difference discretizations on structured and unstructured meshes. Compared with other neural-operator and transformer baselines from prior studies, LLT achieves competitive or lower relative $L_2$ error across these problems. On matched structured discretizations, wall-clock time per training iteration is reduced by factors of 1.8 to 2.5 relative to Transolver. We also scale the approach and apply it to a three-dimensional car aerodynamics dataset with 32,186 unstructured mesh points per sample. Together, these results indicate that LLT provides an accurate and computationally efficient operator for PDE problems across discretizations, mesh types, and problem settings.

</details>


### [170] [ReCoLoRA: Spectrum-Aware Recursive Consolidation for Continual LLM Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07719)
*Wentao Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ReCoLoRA提出了一种频谱感知的递归低秩适配器整合框架，通过从预训练权重的随机SVD初始化适配器、肘部准则选择有效秩、以及递归分解当前有效权重来避免连续任务中的灾难性遗忘，在六任务连续GLUE序列上以更少参数取得最优平均分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LoRA类方法在连续微调场景下，低秩更新会不断堆叠在同一个冻结权重上，导致新任务覆盖旧任务的知识，造成灾难性遗忘。

Method: 1) 从预训练权重的随机SVD初始化适配器；2) 通过肘部准则逐层选择有效秩；3) 优先适配主子空间，再开放残差容量；4) 每进入新任务前，递归分解当前有效权重（而非原始权重）为冻结残差、缓慢更新的主成分和新鲜适配器。

Result: 在四个7-8B规模基座模型上的六任务连续GLUE序列中，ReCoLoRA在三个基座上取得了最佳平均分，超越了LoRA、PiSSA、AdaLoRA和DoRA的rank-sweep基线，同时训练参数更少。

Conclusion: 递归整合策略能有效缓解连续微调中的灾难性遗忘，且参数量更少；oracle路由的任务库变体可作为完全任务隔离下的上界参考。

Abstract: Parameter-efficient fine-tuning adapts a large language model to one task cheaply, but across a task sequence LoRA-style methods keep stacking low-rank updates on the same frozen weight, so each new task tends to overwrite the previous ones. We present ReCoLoRA (Recursive Consolidation of Low-Rank Adapters), a spectrum-aware framework for continual fine-tuning: adapters are initialized from a randomized SVD of the pretrained weight, per-layer effective ranks are selected by an elbow criterion, and the principal subspace is adapted before residual capacity is opened. Before each new task, ReCoLoRA re-decomposes the current effective weight, rather than the original one, into a frozen residual, a slowly updated principal component, and a fresh adapter (recursive consolidation), so every task starts from the model that has already absorbed its predecessors. On a six-task continual GLUE sequence over four 7-8B backbones, ReCoLoRA attains the best final average score on three of the four backbones against rank-swept LoRA, PiSSA, AdaLoRA, and DoRA baselines while training fewer parameters; an oracle-routed task-bank variant serves as an upper bound under full task isolation. Code: https://github.com/bhqy666/ReCoLoRA.

</details>


### [171] [Omni-Sleep: A Sleep Foundation Model via Hierarchical Contrastive Learning of CNS--ANS Dynamic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07720)
*Zhoujie Hou,Song Wang,Kexin Lou,Mo Wang,Chen Wei,Quanying Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Omni-Sleep是一种利用中枢神经/自主神经系统分区作为生理先验的睡眠基础模型，通过三项对比学习目标（系统内一致性、系统间同步、掩码时序建模）在10万+小时多中心多模态PSG数据上预训练，在睡眠分期和疾病分类任务中优于现有基础模型，具有更好的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和模态鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有睡眠基础模型以拓扑无关的方式融合异构生物信号，忽略了CNS与ANS之间的生理组织结构，导致表征学习的结构性不足。

Method: 提出Omni-Sleep，以CNS/ANS分区作为生理先验，通过三项目标进行结构化表征学习：1) 系统内一致性（捕获子系统内共享因子）；2) 系统间同步（对齐子系统轨迹以建模脑-体动力学）；3) 掩码时序建模（捕获长程睡眠动态）。

Result: 在睡眠分期和多疾病分类任务中，Omni-Sleep超越现有基础模型基线，展现出更优的标签效率、跨数据集泛化能力和缺失模态鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 生理层次结构（CNS/ANS分区）对可泛化的睡眠表征学习具有重要价值，为多模态生理信号基础模型设计提供了新范式。

Abstract: Sleep physiology arises from the coordinated dynamics of the central nervous system (CNS) and autonomic nervous system (ANS), as reflected by multimodal polysomnography signals including EEG, EOG, EMG, ECG, and respiration. However, existing sleep foundation models often fuse heterogeneous biosignals in a topology-agnostic manner, overlooking their physiological organization. We introduce Omni-Sleep, a sleep foundation model that uses the CNS/ANS partition as a physiological prior for topology-constrained representation learning. Omni-Sleep learns structured representations through three objectives: intra-system consistency, which captures shared subsystem-level factors within neural and cardio-respiratory signals; inter-system synchronization, which aligns subsystem trajectories to model brain--body dynamics; and latent-space masked temporal modeling, which captures long-horizon sleep dynamics. Pre-trained on over 100,000 hours of multi-center multimodal PSG data, Omni-Sleep is evaluated on sleep staging and multi-disease classification. Across datasets and modality-ablation settings, Omni-Sleep outperforms strong foundation-model baselines, showing improved label efficiency, cross-dataset generalization, and robustness to missing modalities. These results highlight the value of physiological hierarchy for generalizable sleep representation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/AutoBrain-sleep/OmniSleep.

</details>


### [172] [Uncertainty-gated selection for block-sparse attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07724)
*Thomas Rossi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于信息价值的路由器（value-of-information router），通过检测查询的top-k截断是否果断，对不果断的查询动态增加保留块数，从而弥补块稀疏注意力在近邻分数相同时的误截断问题。在LongBench-v2和RULER上显著提升召回率，接近全注意力性能，且适用于多种模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 块稀疏注意力（block-sparse attention）通过per-query top-k选择关键块来替代O(N²)的softmax，但当第k块与第k+1块分数接近时，该选择过程是短视的——它会直接截断而不增加额外预算，导致携带答案证据的块被丢弃且无法在下游恢复。现有方法缺乏对截断决策质量的评估机制。

Method: 提出一种价值信息路由器（value-of-information router），针对每个查询衡量top-k截断的果断程度（即第k块与第k+1块的分数差距），对差距最小的查询（即截断最不果断的查询）动态增加保留块数。该规则与骨干架构无关，可叠加现有块评分方法（如Quest）使用。

Result: 在LongBench-v2 medium上（n=215），router-on-Quest的配对召回率为0.75，top-k仅为0.47（+28pp，McNemar p<0.01）。在RULER NIAH multikey上，与全注意力的差距在2pp以内。该方法在Qwen2.5、Mistral-Nemo、Qwen3.6三种架构的四个模型上均复现提升。128K上下文下，Qwen2.5-7B-1M和Qwen3.6上保持全注意力准确率的0.81和0.89（SSA-style top-k仅0.09），且融合选择加核的流水线运行时间为全注意力的0.62倍和0.80倍。

Conclusion: 提出的路由器有效解决了块稀疏注意力的短视截断问题，在保持计算效率的同时大幅提升长上下文任务的召回率和准确率，接近全注意力性能，且具有模型架构通用性。

Abstract: Block-sparse attention scales long-context language models by replacing the O(N^2) softmax with a per-query top-k selection over key blocks. This cutoff is myopic: when the k-th and (k+1)-th blocks are nearly tied in score, the selector commits without spending extra budget, and a dropped block carrying answer evidence is unrecoverable downstream. We propose a value-of-information router that measures, for each query, how decisively the top-k cut was made, and doubles the kept set for the queries where that gap is smallest; the rule is backbone-agnostic and stacks with existing block-scoring methods such as Quest. On LongBench-v2 medium at n=215 (the entire dataset subset), router-on-Quest reaches paired recall 0.75 vs. top-k 0.47 -- +28 pp over the SSA-style baseline (McNemar p<0.01) -- and lands within 2 pp of dense on RULER NIAH multikey at the same context. The lift reproduces on four models from three architectures (Qwen2.5, Mistral-Nemo, Qwen3.6). At 128K, the router preserves 0.81 and 0.89 of dense accuracy on Qwen2.5-7B-1M and Qwen3.6 (vs. SSA-style top-k at 0.09 on the former) while the fused selection-plus-kernel pipeline runs at 0.62x and 0.80x dense wall time.

</details>


### [173] [SHIFT: Survival Prediction from Incomplete and Heterogeneous Genomic Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07725)
*Muhammet Sami Yavuz,Ayhan Can Erdur,Sabri Mustafa Kahya,Benedikt Wiestler,Jana Lipkova*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SHIFT是一种缺失感知的生存预测模型，利用掩码自注意力机制直接从不完整基因组输入进行预测而无需测试时插补，在跨机构、跨测序面板的泛化中表现优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因组预测模型跨机构迁移时因测序面板差异导致特征结构性缺失，现有方法（限制共有基因、排除不完整患者、测试时插补）降低了鲁棒性并限制了多中心数据利用。

Method: SHIFT模型：1) 独立表示每个基因组特征；2) 使用掩码自注意力机制和特征可用性掩码，仅基于观测输入进行预测；3) 训练时引入变速率特征掩码以增强对异质性缺失模式的鲁棒性。

Result: 在胶质母细胞瘤和肺鳞癌的多队列外部验证中（包括严重跨队列面板不匹配场景），SHIFT表现出强泛化能力，优于标准生存基线和基于插补的方法，且单一模型可处理不同特征集。

Conclusion: 缺失感知建模是多中心生存预测的实用策略，包含部分观测队列的数据可提升外部性能，无需在模型构建中排除不完整队列。

Abstract: Genomic prediction models often fail to transfer across institutions because sequencing panels differ across sites, creating structural feature missingness at deployment. Existing approaches to this challenge typically restrict analysis to genes shared across cohorts, exclude patients with incomplete profiles, or rely on test-time imputation, all of which can reduce robustness and limit the use of multi-center data. We propose Survival prediction Handling Incomplete Features using Transformer (SHIFT), a missingness-aware survival model that directly predicts from incomplete genomic inputs without test-time imputation. SHIFT represents each genomic feature separately and uses masked self-attention, along with a feature-availability mask, so that predictions are based only on observed inputs. Further, we introduce variable-rate feature masking during training to improve robustness to heterogeneous missingness patterns. We evaluate the approach on glioblastoma and lung squamous cell carcinoma with external validation across multiple cohorts, including a challenging setting with severe cross-cohort panel mismatch. Across these settings, SHIFT shows strong generalization and compares favorably with standard survival baselines and imputation-based approaches, while using a single model across differing feature sets. We also find that incorporating patients from incomplete cohorts during development can improve performance on external data, suggesting that partially observed cohorts need not be excluded from model building. These results support missingness-aware modeling as a practical strategy for multi-center survival prediction in precision oncology.

</details>


### [174] [Jet-Long: Efficient Long-Context Extension with Dynamic Bifocal RoPE](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07740)
*Haozhan Tang,Zerui Wang,Yuxian Gu,Song Han,Han Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Jet-Long是一种免调零样本长上下文扩展方法，通过自适应动态缩放因子、局部与长距离双窗口设计、包含-排除注意力合并及即时RoPE校正，在不牺牲短上下文 fidelity 的前提下实现干净的长上下文外推，在多种基准测试上取得最佳性能，并兼容混合注意力架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有零样本长上下文方法使用固定缩放因子，激进因子损害短上下文 fidelity，保守因子在长上下文中失效，亟需一种无需微调、能同时兼顾短上下文保真度和长上下文外推能力的方法。

Method: 提出Jet-Long：1）局部RoPE保真窗口 + 长距离窗口，其缩放因子根据当前序列长度动态自适应；2）包含-排除注意力合并（inclusion-exclusion attention merge）和即时RoPE校正旋转，使双焦点构造在推理时几乎无额外开销；3）融合为单一CuTe kernel，实现高效预填充和生成。

Result: 在Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B上128K上下文，RULER基准分别领先最强基线+4.79/+2.18/+2.03pp；HELMET-RAG取得最佳整体准确率；PG-19困惑度最低；长上下文预填充吞吐达FA2的1.39倍，生成开销≤4%。

Conclusion: Jet-Long在无需微调、不牺牲短上下文性能的前提下实现了有效的零样本长上下文扩展，兼容混合注意力架构，且超参数鲁棒，易于部署。

Abstract: Modern LLMs are increasingly deployed in long-context applications such as retrieval-augmented generation, repository-level coding, and agentic workflows whose accumulated reasoning and tool traces routinely push the input an order of magnitude past the pretraining window, making zero-shot context extension the dominant deployment path for open-weight checkpoints. Most existing zero-shot methods fix a single rescaling factor up front, so an aggressive factor sacrifices short-context fidelity while a conservative one breaks down at long contexts. We propose Jet-Long, a tuning-free zero-shot method that pairs a local RoPE-faithful window with a long-range window whose rescaling factor adapts dynamically to the current sequence length, recovering the base model exactly at short inputs while extrapolating cleanly at long ones. An inclusion-exclusion attention merge and an on-the-fly RoPE correction rotation make the bifocal construction essentially free at inference; fused into a single CuTe kernel, long-context prefill reaches up to $1.39\times$ FA2 throughput on H100 (approaching the Hopper-only FA4), and single-batch generation incurs $\le 4\%$ overhead at every length. On Qwen3-1.7B/4B/8B up to 128K context, Jet-Long leads RULER by $+4.79$/$+2.18$/$+2.03$~pp over the strongest baseline at 1.7B/4B/8B, achieves the best overall accuracy on HELMET-RAG (a benchmark identified by HELMET as the most efficient predictor of downstream long-context performance) and attains the lowest PG-19 perplexity. Jet-Long also generalizes to hybrid attention architectures such as Jet-Nemotron for further long-context improvement without retraining, and remains hyperparameter-resilient for ease of deployment.

</details>


### [175] [Architecture Generalization with MetaNCA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07743)
*Meet Barot,Daniel Berenberg,Sina Khajehabdollahi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA)框架，通过学习局部规则来自组织人工神经网络的权重。该框架使用基于线性注意力的Weight Transformer架构，在计算图上通过局部交互迭代更新任务网络权重，无需反向传播即可生成不同架构的网络权重，在MNIST和CIFAR-100上验证了MLP、CNN和ResNet的有效性，可扩展到200万参数，并泛化到未见过的架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受生物神经元局部交互、高效学习和自适应连接能力的启发，现有神经细胞自动机(NCA)模型虽然能通过局部更新规则学习形态发生并展现稳定性和鲁棒性，但尚未有工作将这种局部自组织机制扩展到人工神经网络的权重生成中。本文旨在弥合这一空白，探索能否通过学习局部规则来自组织生成神经网络权重。

Method: 提出MetaNCA框架，包含一个学习到的规则网络（采用Weight Transformer架构，使用线性注意力聚合邻接权重和隐藏状态的信号），通过计算图上的局部交互迭代更新任务网络的权重。训练阶段元学习规则网络，推理阶段规则网络无需反向传播即可生成具有不同架构的任务网络权重。

Result: 在MNIST和CIFAR-100数据集上，MetaNCA成功为前馈MLP、CNN和ResNet生成了有效权重，可扩展到200万参数的网络。该方法能够泛化到元训练期间未见的架构，且训练阶段的架构多样性增强了这种泛化能力。

Conclusion: MetaNCA证明了通过局部自组织规则生成神经网络权重的可行性，为无需反向传播的权重生成提供了一种新范式，且具有良好的架构泛化能力，为未来研究生物启发式学习算法提供了新方向。

Abstract: Self-organization is an emergent property of life, driven by the collective behavior of individual components acting on local information. Biological neurons, through local interactions transmitted through synapses, are able to learn efficiently and can adapt their connections over an organism's lifespan. Motivated by these desirable properties of adaptability and local interaction, neural cellular automata (NCA) models have been successful at learning morphogenesis solely through local update rules, demonstrating stability over many updates and robustness to perturbations. In this work, we introduce Meta Neural Cellular Automata (MetaNCA), a framework that learns local rules which self-organize the weights of artificial neural networks. A learned rule network iteratively updates the weights of a task network using only local interactions on the computation graph. We propose a novel Weight Transformer architecture for the local rule network, which uses linear attention to aggregate signals from neighboring weights and hidden states. Once trained, the rule network generates task networks of diverse architectures without backpropagation. We show that MetaNCA generates weights for feedforward MLPs, CNNs, and ResNets on MNIST and CIFAR-100, scaling to networks of 2 million parameters. We further show that MetaNCA generalizes to architectures not seen during meta-training, and that architectural diversity in the training phase strengthens this generalization.

</details>


### [176] [Selective Left-Shift: Turning Test-Time Compute and Difficulty-based Curation into Training Data for Low-Resource Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07748)
*Didula Samaraweera,Anjana Supun,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对低资源编程语言，提出三阶段流水线（离线数据合成→SFT→RLVR），将推理时计算左移至离线阶段，以低成本提升小语言模型的代码生成性能，在Julia和Ballerina上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在Python、Java等高资源语言上表现优异，但在Julia等低资源编程语言（LRPLs）上性能急剧下降。改进小语言模型（SLMs）面临三重困境：监督微调受限于数据稀缺，推理时扩展成本过高，从头强化学习几乎无收益。需要一种低成本、高数据效率的方法来提升LRPLs上的代码生成能力。

Method: 提出三阶段流水线：(1) 将推理时计算左移（left-shift）至离线数据合成引擎，通过迭代编译器和测试反馈生成经过验证的训练样本；(2) 在合成验证数据上微调SLM，嵌入强语法先验；(3) 应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），以语言无关的输入/输出测试为奖励信号，SFT先验约束探索空间、避免语法错误。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B上，Julia的MultiPL-E Pass@1提升+7.6个点，Agnostics LiveCodeBench提升+14.2个点，超越当前最优；仅使用SOTA方法1/3的数据和1/6的成本。Ballerina上达到49.7% MultiPL-E Pass@1（该语言预训练表示几乎为零）。

Conclusion: 通过将语法获取与算法推理解耦，该三阶段流水线有效解决了低资源编程语言上的三重困境。SFT阶段和执行验证奖励对于稳定训练均不可或缺。该方法具有良好泛化性，即便对预训练中几乎不可见的语言也有效。

Abstract: Large Language Models achieve strong code generation for high resource languages like Python and Java but suffer sharp performance drops on Low-Resource Programming Languages~(LRPLs) such as Julia. Improving Small Language Models~(SLMs) for these languages faces a trilemma: Supervised Fine-Tuning~(SFT) is bottlenecked by data scarcity, inference-time scaling is too expensive for deployment, and Reinforcement Learning from scratch yields near zero advantages. We propose a three-phase pipeline that resolves this trilemma by decoupling syntax acquisition from algorithmic reasoning. First, we \emph{left-shift} inference-time compute to an offline data synthesis engine that uses iterative compiler and test feedback to generate verified training examples. Second, we fine-tune an SLM on this synthetic, verified data to embed strong syntactic priors. Third, we apply Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Reward~(RLVR) grounded by language-agnostic Input/Output tests, where the SFT prior constrains exploration away from syntax errors. Applied to Qwen3-8B, our pipeline improves pass@1 by up to +7.6 points on MultiPL-E and +14.2 points on the Agnostics LiveCodeBench for Julia compared to SOTA results. Furthermore, the pipeline only used $\frac{1}{3}$ data and $\frac{1}{6}$ cost over the previous state-of-the-art. We further demonstrate that the pipeline generalizes to Ballerina achieving 49.7\% MultiPL-E Pass@1, a language with near-zero pretraining representation. Ablations confirm that both the SFT phase and execution-grounded rewards are necessary for stable training.

</details>


### [177] [Principled Analysis of Deep Reinforcement Learning Evaluation and Design Paradigms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07769)
*Ezgi Korkmaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文分析了深度强化学习在扩展性、容量和复杂性方面的核心问题，证明了在规范设计和评估范式下，强化学习算法的渐进性能与数据规模之间存在非单调关系，并通过大规模实验表明，遵循传统范式的许多研究得出了错误结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 过去十年强化学习研究取得了显著进展，但规范化的评估和设计范式可能存在根本性问题。作者旨在揭示传统范式下得出的结论是否可靠，为强化学习研究提供更科学的评估基础。

Method: 引入强化学习中的扩展律（scaling laws）理论基础，从理论上证明算法性能排名与数据 regimes 之间不存在单调关系；随后设计并执行大规模实验验证理论预测。

Result: 理论分析表明强化学习算法的渐进性能与数据规模的关系是非单调的；大规模实验证实，遵循传统设计和评估范式的大量强化学习研究得出了错误或不可靠的结论。

Conclusion: 当前强化学习的规范评估范式存在根本性缺陷，研究者需要对扩展性、容量和复杂性进行更深入的分析，以建立更可靠的评估方法。

Abstract: Starting from the utilization of deep neural networks to approximate the state-action value function that led to winning one of the most challenging games, to algorithmic advancements that allowed solving problems without even explicitly stating the rules of the challenge at hand, reinforcement learning research has been the center of remarkable scientific progress for the past decade. In this paper, we focus on the key ingredients of this research progress and we analyze the canonical evaluation and design paradigms in reinforcement learning. We introduce the theoretical foundations of scaling laws in reinforcement learning and show that the asymptotic performance of reinforcement learning algorithms does not have a monotone relationship between performance rankings and data-regimes. We conduct large-scale experiments and our results demonstrate that a line of reinforcement learning research under the canonical design and evaluation paradigms resulted in incorrect conclusions. Our analysis and results provide a core analysis on scaling, capacity and complexity of deep reinforcement learning.

</details>


### [178] [Graph-Regularized Deep Learning for EEG-Based Emotion Recognition with Psychologically-Grounded Label Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07773)
*Dongyang Kuang,Zizheng Ma,Yushan Zhang,Xiaocong Zeng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出图正则化学习框架，将情感类视为图节点并通过三种正则化策略（图标签平滑、图拉普拉斯通勤距离、切片Wasserstein距离）强制模型预测符合情感拓扑，在SEED-IV和SEED-V数据集上使多个骨干架构取得+5.42%准确率提升和39%心理上不合理误分类减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习方法常将情感类别视为孤立标签，忽略了情感之间基于维度理论的心理相互依赖关系。

Method: 将情感构建为图节点，边编码基于情感维度理论的邻近关系；设计三种按计算复杂度递增的正则化策略：图标签平滑、图拉普拉斯通勤距离、切片Wasserstein距离，惩罚偏离情感拓扑的模型预测。

Result: 在SEED-IV（4类）和SEED-V（5类）数据集上，最佳准确率提升+5.42%，心理上不合理误分类减少39%。

Conclusion: 该框架是架构无关的，能够提升标准方法的上限性能，有助于构建更符合情感心理学的EEG情感识别模型。

Abstract: EEG-based emotion recognition is critical for mental health monitoring and affective brain-computer interfaces, yet existing deep learning approaches often treat emotion classes as isolated labels, ignoring their psychological interdependencies. We propose a graph-regularized learning framework that conceptualizes emotions as nodes in a graph where edges encode proximity based on dimensional emotion theories. We adapt three complementary regularization strategies--Graph Label Smoothing (intuitive soft labeling), Commuting distance on graph via Graph Laplacian (spectral graph theory), and Sliced Wasserstein Distance (optimal transport on graph)--ordered by increasing computational complexity. These strategies penalize model predictions that deviate from the established emotion topology. Our framework is evaluated across three representative backbone architectures: AudioTransformer (pure transformer), Conformer (CNN-transformer hybrid), and DCGNN (causal graph neural network), demonstrating architecture-agnostic benefits. Experiments on SEED-IV (4 classes) and SEED-V (5 classes) datasets show consistent improvements: best case up to +5.42% accuracy and 39% reduction in psychologically implausible misclassifications. Ultimately, our framework help raise the upper bound of performance achievable with standard approaches. Code will be released.

</details>


### [179] [Predicting Pseudo-nitzschia harmful algal blooms along the Portuguese Coast using satellite-derived predictors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07834)
*Ayman Bnoussaad,El Khalil Cherif,Ligia Pinto,Ramiro Neves,Alexandra D. Silva,Alexandre Bernardino*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文开发并评估了一个基于卫星遥感数据和机器学习框架的时空预测模型，用于预测葡萄牙大西洋沿岸Pseudo-nitzschia硅藻引发的有害藻华（HAB）事件。研究利用十年数据集（2013-2023年）和超过1000个卫星预测变量，在严格的时空交叉验证策略下，集成树方法实现了最高预测性能（随机森林AUC 0.74, Extra Trees 0.77）。研究表明该框架在东部边界上升流海岸具有操作相关的HAB早期预警能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Pseudo-nitzschia硅藻对葡萄牙大西洋沿岸的生态系统和贝类养殖构成持续风险，现有预测手段在现实预报约束下能力有限。作者希望利用纯卫星衍生预测因子，开发在操作层面可行的有害藻华预测框架，以支持早期预警系统。

Method: 基于5,882条观测数据刻画了九个贝类生产区（L1-L9）的环境与生物变异特征。针对热点区域L1-L2，使用十年数据集（1,440条观测，超过1,000个卫星变量，包括海表温度、上升流指数、叶绿素a、浮游植物功能类型等）构建预测模型。采用河流感知空间聚类方案将采样点划分为生态有意义的子区域，并通过同时保留完整年份和空间聚类的严格时空交叉验证策略防止数据泄露，模拟真实预报条件。

Result: 有害藻华发生概率在各模型类型和特征配置下均表现出中等可预测性。集成树方法表现最优：随机森林在环境预测因子下AUC达0.74±0.05；Extra Trees在加入生物变量后AUC达0.77±0.06。特征重要性分析表明季节性结构、空间背景和滞后环境条件主导模型决策，生物指标在物理条件适宜时段内细化藻华概率。

Conclusion: 该框架在东部边界上升流海岸展示了操作相关的卫星支持有害藻华早期预警能力，验证了纯卫星衍生变量在现实约束条件下实现中等以上预测精度的可行性，为HAB监测系统提供了可部署的技术路径。

Abstract: Pseudo-nitzschia diatoms pose recurrent risks to coastal ecosystems and shellfish harvesting along the Portuguese Atlantic coast. Here we develop and evaluate a spatio-temporal machine-learning framework to predict harmful algal bloom (HAB) occurrence using exclusively satellite-derived predictors under realistic forecasting constraints. We characterised environmental and biological variability across shellfish production zones (L1-L9) using 5,882 observations, providing system-wide context. Predictive models were developed for zones L1-L2, a hotspot for Pseudo-nitzschia and domoic acid events, using a decade-long dataset (2013-2023; 1,440 observations; more than 1,000 satellite-based predictors including sea surface temperature, an upwelling index, chlorophyll-a, and plankton functional types). Sampling locations were partitioned into ecologically meaningful sub-regions using a river-aware spatial clustering scheme. A stringent spatio-temporal cross-validation strategy that simultaneously withholds entire years and spatial clusters prevents leakage and closely mimics real-world forecasting conditions. HAB occurrence proved moderately predictable across model classes and feature configurations. Ensemble tree-based methods achieved the strongest discrimination: Random Forest reached 0.74 +/- 0.05 with environmental predictors; Extra Trees reached 0.77 +/- 0.06 with biological variables added. Feature-importance analyses revealed that seasonal structure, spatial context, and lagged environmental conditions dominate model decisions, while biological indicators refine bloom likelihood within physically favourable periods. The framework demonstrates operationally relevant skill for satellite-supported HAB early-warning systems along eastern boundary upwelling coasts.

</details>


### [180] [Explaining Near-Zero Hessian Eigenvalues Through Approximate Symmetries in Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07845)
*Marcel Kühn,Bernd Rosenow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文论证了训练损失Hessian矩阵中大量接近零的特征值起源于网络参数化中连续对称性的弱提升伪戈德斯通模。在深度线性网络中这些对称性是精确的，产生平坦方向和精确零模；ReLU非线性作为微扰会弱显式破缺这些对称性，使得大部分低曲率方向位于对称性子空间内，而高曲率方向与之正交。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有工作解释了Hessian矩阵最大特征值的来源，但大量趋近于零的特征值（bulk）的起源仍然不清楚，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) 在深度线性网络中构造精确零模的特征向量；2) 将ReLU非线性视为微扰，分析其对连续对称性的弱破缺；3) 在两层ReLU师生模型、CIFAR-10训练的网络和卷积网络中进行实验验证。

Result: Hessian矩阵的bulk几乎完全位于对称性子空间内，高曲率方向则与对称性子空间正交。该现象在多层感知机和卷积网络中均成立。

Conclusion: Hessian矩阵的bulk源于网络参数化中连续对称性的弱破缺，这澄清了近零模的起源，并将几何分析与对称性破缺理论联系起来。

Abstract: The Hessian of the training loss governs the local geometry of the loss landscape, yet despite existing explanations for its largest eigenvalues, the origin of the vast multitude of vanishingly small eigenvalues remains elusive. We argue that the bulk consists of the weakly lifted pseudo-Goldstone modes of the continuous symmetries of the network parametrization. In deep linear networks these symmetries are exact: they generate flat directions and hence exact zero modes, whose eigenvectors we construct explicitly. Introducing a ReLU nonlinearity as a perturbation, we show that it breaks these symmetries weakly and explicitly. Resolving the spectrum at the level of eigenvectors, we find that the high-curvature directions are orthogonal to the symmetry subspace, while the bulk lies almost entirely within it. We demonstrate the mechanism in a two-layer ReLU student--teacher model and in a network trained on CIFAR-10. A convolutional example demonstrates that the same diagnostic extends beyond fully connected layers. Together, these results link the Hessian bulk to weakly broken symmetries and clarify the origin of near-zero modes.

</details>


### [181] [Linear Attention Architectures: Mechanisms, Trade-offs, and Cross-Layer Routing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07953)
*Tommaso Cerruti,Tim Rieder,George Rowlands,Lingfeng Jin,Imanol Schlag*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对softmax注意力与四种近期循环线性注意力架构（DeltaNet系列）进行了比较研究，在350M-3B参数规模、15B token训练设置下，评估了它们在表达能力、训练吞吐量、实现复杂度等方面的差异，并提出了跨层值路由（CLVR）机制用于改进DeltaNet类记忆模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力机制存在序列长度的二次计算成本问题，限制了长上下文场景下的训练与推理。为探索更高效的替代方案，需要对现有线性注意力架构进行系统比较，并寻找进一步改进的方法。

Method: 1) 将softmax注意力和四种线性注意力架构（DeltaNet、Gated DeltaNet、Kimi Delta Attention、Gated DeltaNet-2）统一表示为循环记忆符号形式，对比其表达能力、记忆衰减、擦除/写入控制等特性。2) 在350M参数/15B token设置下进行系统实验，包括优化器对比、混合vs纯架构对比、运行时间测量，并扩展到1.3B和3B参数规模。3) 提出跨层值路由（CLVR）机制，将较低层的写入值转发到较高层的隐藏流中。

Result: 1) Kimi Delta Attention + Muon优化器在350M/15B设置下达到最低验证损失。2) 纯Gated DeltaNet + AdamW具有最高归一化训练吞吐量。3) 混合架构以吞吐量代价换取损失改善。4) Muon优化器在匹配架构下持续优于AdamW。5) 提出的CLVR机制对DeltaNet和Gated DeltaNet均能降低最终验证损失。

Conclusion: 不同线性注意力架构在效率与效果之间存在权衡；CLVR作为一种轻量级跨层信息路由机制，能够有效改进DeltaNet类模型的性能；Muon优化器在训练此类架构时具有优势。

Abstract: Self-attention lets each token retrieve information from the full context, but its quadratic cost in sequence length limits training and inference at long context. This paper presents a comparative study of softmax attention and four recent recurrent linear-attention architectures: DeltaNet, Gated DeltaNet, Kimi Delta Attention, and Gated DeltaNet-2. We express these mechanisms in a common recurrent-memory notation, making explicit how they differ in expressivity, memory decay, erase and write control, training throughput, and implementation complexity. Our experiments center on 350M-parameter models trained for 15B tokens, and include optimizer and learning-rate comparisons, hybrid-versus-pure stack comparisons, sequence-length runtime measurements, larger DeltaNet runs at 1.3B and 3B parameters, and a small set of downstream evaluations. The reported speed results measure training throughput and iteration time; we do not provide an empirical inference-speed benchmark. Within the reported 350M-parameter, 15B-token sweep, Kimi Delta Attention with Muon reaches the lowest final validation loss, a pure Gated DeltaNet stack trained with AdamW has the highest normalized training throughput, hybrid stacks generally improve loss at a throughput cost, and Muon consistently lowers final validation loss relative to AdamW in the matched architecture settings we evaluate. We introduce and evaluate lightweight cross-layer routing mechanisms for DeltaNet-style memories. The most natural DeltaNet-inspired formulation, forwarding a lower layer's delta-rule write error into the next layer's value target, does not improve over matched baselines. Routing into the aligned hidden stream and forwarding the write value instead yields a modest improvement in the matched runs we report: Cross-Layer Value Routing (CLVR) lowers final validation loss for both DeltaNet and Gated DeltaNet.

</details>


### [182] [KronQ: LLM Quantization via Kronecker-Factored Hessian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.07964)
*Donghyun Lee,Yuhang Li,Ruokai Yin,Priyadarshini Panda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: KronQ提出了一种利用梯度协方差进行Kronecker近似海森矩阵的后训练量化框架，通过双向非相干处理和新的混合精度分配指标，在LLaMA-3-70B的2-bit量化中实现了7.93 perplexity，远超GPTQ和GPTAQ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有二阶PTQ方法（如GPTQ）仅从输入激活统计量构建量化目标，假设所有输出通道对逐层重构目标的贡献相同。作者质疑这一假设，认为量化损失应同时依赖于激活协方差和梯度协方差。

Method: (1) 基于Kronecker分解的海森近似，将梯度协方差引入量化流程；(2) 双向非相干处理：利用梯度协方差扩展输入侧随机旋转到输出维度，降低权重在输入和输出两个维度上的方差；(3) 基于梯度和激活海森迹的层间混合精度分配新指标。

Result: 在LLaMA-3-70B的2-bit权重量化中，GPTQ和GPTAQ发散或产生退化量化（perplexity >2000），而KronQ实现了7.93 perplexity。

Conclusion: KronQ通过引入梯度协方差改进了PTQ性能，尤其在极低位宽量化下显著优于现有方法，验证了输出通道非均匀贡献假设的正确性。

Abstract: Post-training quantization (PTQ) is a widely adopted technique for compressing large language models (LLMs) without retraining. Existing second-order PTQ methods, including GPTQ, construct quantization objectives exclusively from input activation statistics, effectively assuming that all output channels contribute equally to the layer-wise reconstruction objective. We propose KronQ, a PTQ framework that challenges this assumption by introducing the gradient covariance into the quantization pipeline. Under the Kronecker-factored Hessian approximation, the quantization loss depends jointly on both the activation and gradient covariances, and KronQ exploits this at two complementary levels. (1) KronQ introduces bidirectional incoherence processing, extending the existing input-side random rotation to the output dimension using the gradient covariance, reducing weight magnitude variance across both input and output dimensions. (2) KronQ derives a new sensitivity metric for inter-layer mixed-precision allocation, driven by the gradient and activation Hessian traces. Notably, in the case of 2-bit weight-only quantization on LLaMA-3-70B, while GPTQ and GPTAQ diverge or produce degenerate quantizations (>2000 perplexity on WikiText-2), KronQ achieves 7.93 perplexity.

</details>


### [183] [Provably Optimal Learning Algorithms for Assistance Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08012)
*Nivasini Ananthakrishnan,Mark Bedaywi,Michael I. Jordan,Stuart Russell,Nika Haghtalab*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了重复援助博弈的在线变体，提出了首个可证明高效的学习算法，实现了$\widetilde{O}(T^{3/4})$的$(1-1/e)$近似援助遗憾率，并证明了更好的近似因子在计算上不可行，同时在伪去中心化设置中达到了最优的$\widetilde{O}(T^{1/2})$遗憾率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在援助博弈框架中，知情智能体（人类）观察到世界潜在状态，而不知情智能体（助手）仅能观察到人类的行动。现有工作缺乏对此类重复交互场景下可证明高效的学习算法的研究，本文旨在填补这一理论空白。

Method: 定义了援助遗憾（assistance regret）作为性能度量指标；提出了去中心化算法，人类和助手分别运行，助手可使用任意无遗憾算法；设计了伪去中心化变体，利用共享随机字符串提升效率。

Result: 在去中心化设置中实现了$\widetilde{O}(T^{3/4})$的$(1-1/e)$近似援助遗憾率；证明了优于$(1-1/e)$的近似因子计算不可行；在伪去中心化设置中达到了$\widetilde{O}(T^{1/2})$的最优遗憾率（对数因子内最优）。

Conclusion: 为重复援助博弈提供了首个可证明高效的学习算法，给出了算法效率的理论边界，揭示了近似最优率与计算复杂性之间的权衡关系。

Abstract: This paper studies an online variant of the assistance games framework, where an informed agent and an uninformed agent repeatedly interact over $T$ timesteps to optimize a common reward function. While the informed agent (the human) observes a latent state of the world, the uninformed agent (the assistant) observes only the human's actions. We provide the first provably efficient learning algorithms for repeated assistance games. We introduce the notion of assistance regret: the gap between the cumulative utility of interactions and that of the optimal joint policies in hindsight, which map latent states to action pairs. We present decentralized algorithms for both the human and the assistant that achieve a $(1-1/e)$-approximate assistance regret rate of $\widetilde{O}(T^{3/4})$, with runtime polynomial in the size of the action and state spaces. These algorithms are general; in particular, they accommodate any no-regret algorithm for the assistant. We prove that achieving a regret approximation factor better than $(1-1/e)$ is computationally intractable. Furthermore, we demonstrate how these generic no-regret algorithms can be tailored to a pseudo-decentralized setting -- using a shared random string -- to achieve a rate of $\widetilde{O}(T^{1/2})$, optimal up to logarithmic factors.

</details>


### [184] [Collate: Collaborative Neural Network Learning for Latency-Critical Edge Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.08013)
*Shuo Huai,Di Liu,Hao Kong,Xiangzhong Luo,Weichen Liu,Ravi Subramaniam,Christian Makaya,Qian Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Collate是一个协同学习异构模型的联邦学习训练框架，通过动态归零-恢复方法和原型校正聚合机制，在满足多个边缘系统延迟约束的同时提升推理精度，平均提升1.96%-3.09%的准确率，且几乎无额外训练开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦学习优化主要关注训练效率，忽略了推理速度优化，尤其在边缘系统存在延迟约束时，设备异构性严重影响推理模型性能。

Method: 提出Collate框架，包含动态归零-恢复方法（dynamic zeroizing-recovering）调整本地模型架构以满足延迟约束，以及原型校正联邦聚合方案（proto-corrected federated aggregation）聚合异构本地模型。

Result: 在延迟约束下，扩展模型平均准确率提升1.96%，缩减模型平均准确率提升3.09%，几乎无额外训练开销。

Conclusion: Collate能够通过单次训练过程同时满足不同边缘系统的延迟约束并保持高精度，有效解决了联邦学习中的推理延迟问题。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) empowers multiple clients to collaboratively learn a model, enlarging the training data of each client for high accuracy while protecting data privacy. However, when deploying FL in real-time edge systems, the heterogeneity of devices among systems has a severe impact on the performance of the inferred model. Existing optimizations on FL focus on improving the training efficiency but fail to speed up inference, especially when there is a latency constraint.
